ANNOUNCEMENT
Happy Holidays from Hypixel!
Updated: December 2, 2024
It snowed a little earlier in our lobbies this year, but we're finally closer to the holiday season! We've got our holiday decorations up, along with a bunch of new content!
- Holiday Sale - Up to 30% off!
- New Main Lobby Minigame - Baker's Bash!
- Event Shop Updates
- Brand New Prototype Lobby
- Holiday Lobbies with Present Hunt
  - Duels First time!
  - Wool Games First time!
  - Prototype New lobby!
  - Main Lobby
  - Bed Wars
  - SkyWars
  - Housing
- Grinch Simulator Returns
- Main Lobby Ice Fishing Returns
- Advent Calendars starting December 1st!
- New Bed Wars and SkyWars Maps
- New SkyBlock Holiday Rank Bundles
Read on for all the details!
## ► Holiday Sale
Our Holiday Sale is now active! It will offer up to a 30% discount on everything in the store in-game, while the web store will offer up to a 25% discount.
Check out the web store here! ​ ► Event Shop Updates Our Event Shop has received some new arrivals for the holiday season. There's also some cosmetic types first seen in the Event Shop, like a SkyBlock Barn Skin and some Quake parts for a new railgun!
SkyBlock Ice Hydra Helmet Skin & Holiday Chest Backpack Skins
SkyBlock Winter Barn Skin
## ► SkyBlock Bundles
Our Rank Bundles for the Holidays have been refreshed with our brand-new Sprays and Furniture! Claim them before the event ends!
New Sprays
New Furniture
## ► Regarding Seasonal Bingo
Recently, we spoke to the community about the future of seasonal Bingo activities and gathered feedback on its future. That thread can be found here: LINK. We came to the conclusion that Bingo would better suited as an exclusive part of the yearly Hypixel Anniversary Event. Thank you to everyone who shared their thoughts!
ConnorLinfoot Said
Thank you to everyone who gave suggestions/feedback regarding bingo. After reading all of the replies we've had an internal discussion to come up with a plan for where to go with bingo. Our current plan is as follows:
- Players will be given the option to select a bingo card with a list of pre-selected games, along with a difficulty of their choosing.
  - We'll likely have 3 "game categories" for players to choose from. These will contain different games, so players can pick the group they are most interested in playing.
  - Players can also choose which difficulty card they wish to complete, easy or hard. Players will still be able to complete both difficulties if they want to.
    - We have chosen to reduce the number of card options for difficulties to simplify the objectives as mentioned further down, as well as reduce the number of options with there also been 3 groups of objectives to pick from.
  - All players will be given the same card options and objectives, so if you wish to group up with a friend or even compete to complete a card that is still possible.
- Cards will be reduced from a 5x5 to a 4x4 grid, going from 25 objectives per card to 16 objectives.
- We'll be reviewing objectives and removing/simplifying some that are too difficult.
  - We've seen a lot of feedback that some objectives result in newer players having extreme difficulty completing them, while hard objectives should be more difficult, we don't want to make players spend hours trying to complete it and maybe even resort to boosting to be able to complete it.
  - We've also seen a good amount of feedback on making objectives more simple, this is something we'll do to some extent but we believe there is a happy middle ground. Some of the fun of bingo is completing specific objectives that may require you to go out of the norm in some cases, but we understand that these objectives shouldn't be too difficult as previously touched on.
- We'll be adding a new bingo shop along with a form of simple currency (bingo bucks anyone?)
  - Each card completion will reward the currency with more being granted based on the difficulty of the card.
  - This currency can then be used to purchase specific cosmetics that will be exclusive to this bingo shop.
  - We'll also aim to add at least 2 new cosmetics for each new bingo event, so players who have already completed previous events still have something to earn.
  - Completing rows of bingo objectives will still give other rewards as it does currently.
- Bingo will become a once-a-year event, being part of the Hypixel Anniversary that occurs in April every year. With this, the anniversary event will also be extended to last longer than a week, likely closer to the usual 1 month for most seasonal events.
  - This will allow us to free up time for other changes during the other seasonal events, as well as put a bit more focus on bingo and its rewards each year.
- We'll also be improving marketing for bingo and attempting to bring more attention to the event.
  - Many players have rightfully pointed out that bingo has been kinda hidden for the seasonal events, and not something really advertised anywhere. With this being a once-a-year event it'll be better to market it in the usual ways, like an NPC, and the lobby boss bar.
- With all of the above in mind, this means that for the Holidays event in December, there will not be a bingo event running. But as mentioned above this will allow us to put a bit more time into that event and make some other additions/improvements.
  - You'll see the next bingo event in April next year with the changes above hopefully implemented if all goes to plan.
  - We'll also keep players up to date with these plans if anything changes, likely as part of the minigame developer update posts.
For this plan, we have tried to take into account as many players' preferences and opinions as possible, but we have leaned towards what would be better for the casual player who doesn't currently play bingo, as our goal is to increase participation in the event overall but we still hope to create an event for all players to enjoy.

# Changelog/2024/December 21
Categories: 2024 Updates, Updates
ANNOUNCEMENT
Taylor's Winter 2024 Bundle + Permanent Shop
Updated: December 21, 2024
Hey folks!
Today we are releasing another edition of seasonal cosmetics, along with new permanent offerings within Taylor's Shop.
## ► Bonus Hot Cocoa Rabbit Shop Bundle
As a special addition this year, when you purchase the 16,400 Gem Bundle, you'll receive the new day and night Hot Cocoa Rabbit skin as a bonus!
This bonus skin is only available from the 21st of December 2024 to the 4th of January 2025
Take advantage of the chance to collect the cozy day and night Hot Cocoa Rabbit skin! Simply purchase the 16,400 Gem Bundle on the Hypixel Store and talk to Jerry on your private island to get ahold of this limited time item.
Don't wait until the last minute! Act now and pick up some great deals before the sale ends.
CHECK OUT THE STORE AT store.hypixel.net Or check out the in-game store for even bigger discounts!
## ► Taylor's Winter Bundle
Taylor's Winter 2024 Bundle is this year's edition of our Winter Cosmetics bundle. It features 10 new items that will hopefully make your holiday season more holly and jolly.
This bundle will be available starting from December 21st up until March 20th the following year.
Here's a full list of items being added to Taylor's Winter 2024 Bundle: Mythic Rarity (1350 Gems) Polar Nights Power Orb Skin Legendary Rarity (950 Gems) Spirit Wolf Day/Night Skin Cookie Sandwich Snowman Day/Night Skin Epic Rarity (650 Gems) Ham Barn Skin Main Street Barn Skin Red Nose Reindeer Pet Skin Frozen Diver Helmet Skin Dyes (375 Gems) Snowflake Dye Black Ice Dye Other Hot Cocoa Backpack Skin (350 Gems)
## ► Taylor's Collection
Taylor's Collection is a new selection of items that will be available for the foreseeable future. It features 20 new skin variants for some of the most popular pets in the game, along with some other, more special pet choices.
Here's a full list of items being added to Taylor's Collection:
Ultimate Rarity (1800 Gems)
- Super Plushie Ender Dragon Pet Skin
- Super Plushie Golden Dragon Pet Skin
Mythic Rarity (1350 Gems)
- Knight Diamond Necron Head Skin
  - This cosmetic features swappable skin variants
- Drake Dragon Armor Helmet Skin
  - A variant for each Dragon Armor Helmet (e.g Superior, Wise, Strong, Unstable)!
Epic Rarity (650 Gems)
- Clay Golem Pet Skin
- BBQ Pigman Pet Skin
- Hollow Rock Pet Skin
- German Wolf Pet Skin
- Bald Eagle Griffin Pet Skin
- Myceliyum Burger Mooshroom Cow Pet Skin
- Roast Chicken Pet Skin
- Sock Monkey Pet Skin
- Dragon Water Hydra Helmet Skin
## ► Availability
Here's a warm reminder regarding the availability of items in the two Taylor's Shop collections and previous seasonal bundles:
- The Taylor's Winter 2024 Bundle will come back in 2025 along with even more items and goodies!
- Items from Taylor's Collection will be available for the foreseeable future. Items within Taylor's Collection may eventually rotate but you can expect them to be made available one way or another.
- We currently have no plans to bring back seasonal items from past bundles.
- The Hot Cocoa Rabbit skin is only available from the 21st December 2024 to the 4th of January 2025 and will not return.
We hope that these non-limited offerings will allow for players to be able to use and enjoy these cosmetics instead of having the majority of these items stashed away as investments.
Thanks for the support throughout the years and we hope you enjoy these cosmetics.
Happy Holidays!

# Changelog/2024/December 3
Categories: 2024 Updates, Updates
0.20.9
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.20.9 - Crimson Isle QoL
Updated: December 3, 2024
Greetings, folks!
Welcome to Hypixel SkyBlock 0.20.9. Today we're kick-starting the holiday season with a small update to the Crimson Isle. Read on to find out what's in store!
## ► Kuudra Armor Changes
### ➥ General Changes
All Kuudra Armor now has a combat requirement and Kuudra Tier completion requirement, as follows:
- Basic: Combat 22, Basic Tier Kuudra Clear
- Hot: Combat 27, Hot Tier Kuudra Clear
- Burning: Combat 32, Burning Tier Kuudra Clear
- Fiery: Combat 37, Fiery Tier Kuudra Clear
- Infernal: Combat 42, Infernal Tier Kuudra Clear
### ➥ Crimson Armor
While Crimson Armor's Swipe ability still remains, many facets of said ability have been changed:
Dominus Stacks and Stack Gain
- Instead of gaining stacks on kills, you now gain stacks on melee hits.
- Magic Damage no longer interact with gaining Dominus stacks nor does it proc the Swipe ability.
- The stack gain cooldown scales based on the amount of Crimson Armor pieces you're wearing, as follows:
  - 2 Pieces: 1.5s
  - 3 Pieces: 1s
  - 4 Pieces: 0.5s
- Depending on your lowest tier of Crimson Armor, you gain stats based on how many stacks you have active:
  - Basic: 0.05 Swing Range
  - Hot: 0.1 Swing Range
  - Burning: 0.1 Swing Range + 1 Ferocity
  - Fiery: 0.1 Swing Range + 2 Ferocity
  - Infernal: 0.1 Swing Range + 2 Ferocity + 10 Additive Damage
Swipe Ability and Functionality
- The damage calculation of Swipe has been overhauled.
  - In most cases, the swipe damage is 15-25% of your melee hit.
- Swipe deals double damage the first time it swipes a mob.
- Swipe can trigger multiple times on the same mob.
- Swipe no longer can duplicate Combat XP on kill (kill duping).
- Swipe Damage is dependent on both the number of Crimson Armor pieces and the lowest tier of Crimson Armor that you're wearing. This damage increase is reflected as a multiplier, as follows:
|Crimson|Infernal|Fiery|Burning|Hot|Basic|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|4/4|3|2.625|2.25|1.875|1.5|
|3/4|2|1.75|1.5|1.25|1|
|2/4|1|0.875|0.75|0.625|0.5|
These changes allow Crimson Armor to provide more consistent damage output when fighting any mob and gives players an incentive to upgrade their Crimson Armor for increased damage scaling. The reduced damage scaling also helps with future-proofing the armor set for when we make combat content in the future.
Related Changes
Some changes have been implemented to retain some of Crimson Armor's current use cases:
- Blaze Slayer
  - Adjusted the health and Combat EXP for Blaze Slayer mobs:
    - Smoldering Blaze: 4m → 2m HP, 240 → 320 Combat EXP (1.33x)
    - Millennia Blaze: 20m HP → 12m HP, 660 → 960 Combat EXP (1.45x)
  - Reduced the HP of Infernal Demonlord slayer mini-bosses:
    - Tier 3: 12,000,000 → 9,000,000
    - Tier 4: 25,000,000 → 17,500,000, 75,000,000 → 50,000,000
  - Reduced the damage of the minibosses by ~25%.
  - The kill requirement for T3 and T4 Infernal Demonlord has been reduced by 25%.
    - The numbers have been adjusted post-alpha, taking into account the 45 Combat Wisdom available from Slayer Weapons.
- Enderman Slayer
  - Increased the base damage of Enderman Katanas by ~25%.
  - Increased the crit damage of Enderman Katanas by 5 - 20%.
  - Slightly increased the delay of the Voidgloom Seraph going into hits phase.
  - Slightly reduced the duration of Voidgloom Seraph's beams phase.
To compensate for the removal of "kill duping" and to give players an incentive to use a melee set, we've implemented the following changes:
- Slayer Weapons now have conditional Combat Wisdom for their respective mobs, ranging from 15 - 45 Combat Wisdom.
- The maximum amount of progress that a kill can contribute towards your Slayer Quest has been raised from 50% to 75%.
  - The Combat XP from certain slayer bosses, minibosses, and related mobs have been slightly adjusted to prevent slayer trading from being overpowered.
Lastly, the following fixes relating to slayer phase-bypassing exploits have been implemented:
- The "Fire Dagger" line's Burning Vengeance ability now has a damage cap of 50,000,000.
- End Stone Sword's Extreme Focus ability now has a damage boost cap of 500% and a revamped ability description.
### ➥ Terror Armor
Terror Armor's ability functionality has remained largely the same, with some minor damage adjustments.
Hydra Stacks and Stack Gain
- The stack gain cooldown (0.2) is consistent regardless of how many Terror Armor pieces you're wearing.
- The damage boost per Hydra Stack is dependent on both the number of Terror Armor pieces and the lowest tier of Terror Armor that you're wearing, as follows:
|Terror|Infernal|Fiery|Burning|Hot|Basic|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|4/4|15|12.75|10.5|8.25|6|
|3/4|10|8.5|7.0|5.5|4|
|2/4|5|4.25|3.5|2.75|2|
- Depending on your lowest tier of Terror Armor, you gain arrow speed and side arrow damage based on how many stacks you have active:
  - Basic: +1% Arrow Speed, 20 Side Arrow Damage
  - Hot: +1.5% Arrow Speed, 30% Side Arrow Damage
  - Burning: +2% Arrow Speed, 40% Side Arrow Damage
  - Fiery: +2.5% Arrow Speed, 50% Side Arrow Damage
  - Infernal: +3% Arrow Speed, 60% Side Arrow Damage
Related Changes
Some adjustments have been made to Kuudra as a result of the Terror Armor adjustments:
- Decreased the amount of hits required for Kuudra’s hit phase by 20%.
- Decreased the amount of damage required to kill Kuudra final phase by 25%.
### ➥ Aurora Armor
Aurora Armor has a brand-new ability in Homing Missiles, providing you with a slight damage bonus in most situations.
Arcane Energy Stacks and Stack Gain
- The stack gain cooldown scales based on the amount of Aurora Armor pieces you're wearing, as follows:
  - 2 Pieces: 1s
  - 3 Pieces: 0.7s
  - 4 Pieces: 0.5s
Homing Missile Ability and Functionality
- At 10 stacks, you can sneak to activate the homing missile ability, firing 3-5 missiles to targets near you.
- The number of missiles fired depends on the tier of Aurora Armor, as follows:
  - Basic: 3
  - Hot, Burning: 4
  - Fiery, Infernal: 5
- The base damage of the missiles is 5000.
- The damage from Homing Missiles are considered Magic Damage and has a scaling of 0.3.
  - The damage scales with your usual Mage stats of Intelligence and Ability Damage.
- The missiles can only hit a singular target. It does not deal splash damage.
This set ability provides a decent DPS boost to players not using Wither Impact. For those that are, the damage increase is much less significant. This is intended design-wise as Wither Impact does not need help at the present time.
Related Changes
- Aurora Helmet now has Ability Damage that scales throughout armor tier, making it an expensive yet better option than Wither Goggles.
### ➥ Fervor Armor
Fervor Armor gains a new ability in Ground Pound, allowing tank builds to deal damage while building for tankiness.
Fervor Stacks and Stack Gain
- The stack gain cooldown scales based on the amount of Fervor Armor pieces you're wearing, as follows:
  - 2 Pieces: 1.5s
  - 3 Pieces: 1s
  - 4 Pieces: 0.5s
Ground Pound Ability and Functionality
At 10 stacks, you can sneak to activate the Ground Pound ability, allowing you to leap into the air and quickly slam back to deal damage to nearby enemies. This damage scales off of your EHP (Defense and HP). The damage currently does not scale based off of your armor tier as you naturally have higher stats at higher armor tiers, thus dealing more damage.
Intimidate In addition to Ground Pound, Fervor Armor has a bonus passive (Intimidate) that has a 50% chance to redirect aggro of 3 nearby mobs to the player on melee hit.
### ➥ Hollow Armor
The abilities of Hollow Wand have been reworked to be more relevant as a support build.
Spirit Stacks and Stack Gain
- Hollow Armor gains Spirit Stacks both from you hitting a mob, and up to 4 nearby players hitting a mob in a 25 block radius.
- The stack gain cooldown is individual to each nearby player - if you and 4 people hit a mob within a second, you should gain 5 stacks.
- The stack gain cooldown scales based on the amount of Hollow Armor pieces you're wearing, as follows:
  - 2 Pieces: 3s
  - 3 Pieces: 2s
  - 4 Pieces: 1s
Hollow Wand Ability Changes
Spirit Spark [✤✤] - 10 Spirit | Heal yourself + all nearby players for 8% max HP.
Hollowed Rush [✦✦] - 15 Spirit | Grant all nearby players 1.3x Mana Regeneration for 6s.
Raging Wind [✦✤] - 20 Spirit | Grant all nearby players +30% Damage and +10 Ferocity for 20s. Stacks up to 3 times.
Ichor Pool [✤✦] - 30 Spirit | Spawn a pool with ~10 block radius. Any player standing within this pool gains 1.2x Damage and +40 Ferocity. Pool lasts for 20s.
- Abilities do not stack unless stated in the ability description, even if activated by different players.
- The ability casting patterns have been simplified to two inputs.
- The hidden ability of Hollow Wand has been removed as it did not fit with the set's support theme.
## ► Lava Fishing Changes
### ➥ Trophy Fishing
Trophy Fish Chance
To allow for easier tracking of all the boosts towards catching trophy fish, Trophy Fish Chance is now a stat.
To compensate for Trophy Fish Chance now being additive (and thus losing out on ~16-20% Trophy Fish Chance), the following things received a buff to their Trophy Fish Chance:
- Increased Trophy Fish Chance on Trophy Hunter armor set bonus, as follows:
  - Bronze: 0 → 5%
  - Silver: 5 → 10%
  - Gold: 10 → 20%
  - Diamond: 20 → 30%
- Increased Trophy Fish Chance on Magma Rod, Inferno Rod, and Hellfire Rod, as follows:
  - Magma Rod: 10 → 15%
  - Inferno Rod: 15 → 20%
  - Hellfire Rod: 20 → 25%
- Added Trophy Fish Chance to Odger's Teeth, as follows:
  - Bronze: 1%
  - Silver: 2%
  - Gold: 3%
  - Diamond: 4%
Trophy Fish Rate Adjustments
- Pity now triggers at 600 catches for all fishes.
- The catch rates of the following fish have been adjusted:
  - Vanille: 3 → 8%
  - Karate Fish: 1 → 2%
  - Slugfish: 10 → 15%
  - Blobfish: 15 → 25%
- Golden Fish Appearance Rate: 15 to 20 minutes → 8 to 12 minutes
Design Notes
- The Blobfish change aims to make it roll last, preventing it from blocking desired catches.
- The Golden Fish adjustment should make it much more likely you catch its Diamond before you catch all the other fish.
- The goal of the pity adjustment is to reduce the variance in Trophy Fishing.
- With the adjusted rates, Trophy Fishing should be less than 100 hours total for most players.
Other Changes
- There's now an instance-wide announcement when a player catches a Diamond Trophy Fish for the first time.
- You can now check the amount of each tier of Trophy Fish you've caught at Odger's menu.
- Instead of needing to salvage your Trophy Hunter armor set for the Magmafish reward, you will now be granted 20 Diamond Magmafish upon completing Diamond Trophy Hunter.
  - This reward can be retroactively claimed by anyone at Odger who has achieved Diamond Trophy Hunter.
- The upgrade cost of Trophy Hunter armor has been standardized across all rarities.
### ➥ Treasure Loot Pool
The Lava Fishing treasure loot pool is now different inside and outside of the Crimson Isle.
Crimson Isle Loot Pool
The Crimson Isle variant has many items exclusive to the Crimson Isle, such as Lava Shells and Kuudra Keys.

## ➥ Other Fishing Changes
- Diamond Magmafish now costs 20 Gold Magmafish to craft.
- Added +2 Fishing Speed to Finwave, Ichthyic, and Gillsplash equipment.
- Finwave, Ichthyic, and Gillsplash equipment can now be upgraded (starred).
- Added 15 Fishing Speed to Hot Bait.
## ► Crimson Isle Item Unsoulbinding
Most items from the Crimson Isle are now no longer soulbound, allowing you to trade your excess resources with other players. Upgrading your items will now also no longer soulbind them.
For items that are upgraded and soulbound due to them being donated in the museum, they will currently stay soulbound. We will look into ways to let you unsoulbound these items as soon as possible.
The following items are now no longer soulbound:
- Mob Drops
  - Blaze Ashes
  - Mutated Blaze Ashes
  - Millennia-Old Blaze Ashes
  - Spectre Dust
  - Wither Soul
  - Bezos
  - Magmag

- Special
  - Nether Star

  - X
  - Y
  - Z
- Other Items
  - Blaze Talisman
  - Netherrack-looking Sunshade
  - Rekindled Ember Armor
- All Attribute Shards
## ► Other Changes
- The bestiary kills required for the following mobs has been reduced from 1,000 → 500:
  - Barbarian Duke X
  - Mage Outlaw
  - Bladesoul
  - Ashfang
  - Magma Boss
- The bestiary kills required for the following mobs has been reduced from 250 → 100:
  - Lord Jawbus
- The bestiary kills required for the following mobs has been reduced from 4,000 → 1,000:
  - Pyroclastic Worm
- SkyBlock XP is now awarded for attaining a certain reputation rank in each faction, as follows:
  - Friendly (1000 Reputation): 5 SkyBlock XP
  - Trusted (3000 Reputation): 10 SkyBlock XP
  - Honored (7000 Reputation): 20 SkyBlock XP
  - Hero (12000 Reputation): 40 SkyBlock XP
- Increased the number of times you can use the Matriarch's Perfume per day from 2 to 4.
- Faction quests are now automatically started instead of having to manually select them at your faction's info board.
- Completing a higher tier of Kuudra will now complete quests looking for a lower tier Kuudra completion.
- The Kuudra Pet's Kuudra Specialist perk has been buffed from 5% to 20% damage increase.
- You can now reroll Kuudra chests once per run with Kismet Feathers.
- Added upgraded versions of Thunder Bottles (Storm in a Bottle, Hurricane in a Bottle) that can store a much larger amount of thunder charges.
- Changed the piece bonuses of Magma Necklace and Glowstone Gauntlet to Crit Damage and Intelligence respectively.
  - Both pieces previously gave Health instead.
- The Mycelium and Red Sand cost for all tiers of Kuudra Keys have been reduced by roughly 1/3.
- You no longer lose reputation from the other faction while completing faction quests.
- Added a chat message for when slayer minibosses spawn.
- You can now stack the duration of Smoldering Polarization effects.
- The Blazetekk Ham Radio will now give half the stats if you're the only person in a channel.
- Endstone Sword now has a damage increase cap of 500%.
- Rolled an updated version of the Habanero Tactics recipe in anticipation of buffing the enchantment.
- Add pages to the Collection Display menu.
- Allow Great Spook Potions in the Potion Bag.
## ► Bug Fixes
- Fixed a typo in the Lumber Jack Commission rejection dialogue.
- Fixed being able to use /legacytrades in The Rift to sell items.
- Fixed beacons being unable to be fueled with Scorched Power Crystals when placed.
- Fixed beacons giving benefits when first placed until next join.
- Fixed being able to Double Hook a Reindrake (results in a Double Hook chat message but no bonus Reindrake).
- Fixed Biohazard Armor stats not working after modifying the Island Time.
- Fixed Clover Helmet decreasing the Breaking Power stat.
- Fixed Enchanted Wheat and Enchanted Hay Bale not giving Farming XP when picked up from minions.
- Fixed Great Spook Staff's "Spook" ability removing night vision.
- Fixed Gunther's Race description to accurately reflect what step is needed to complete the quest.
- Fixed Hedgehog Pet's "Fearsome Farmer" perk working as passive.
- Fixed Large Enchanted Mining Sack showing "Gravel" in its item lore.
- Fixed outdated information in the Dungeon Rules and Tips menu.
- Fixed NPCs being able to call/reach you on the Abiphone when you're in no signal areas.
- Fixed the Lapis Armor still having a full set health bonus despite being removed in a previous update.
- Fixed the Nightmare Sea Creature damaging visitors on private islands.
- Fixed Voidling Devotee/Radical attacking players that don't meet requirements to kill it.
## ► What's Next?
This update only includes some of the changes we want to do with Crimson Isle. We are fully aware that there are many aspects to the island that still need updating and will look to release more changes in the future.
Here are some quick changes we have in mind for the near future, with some changes being prioritized:
- Rework Habanero Tactics to be useful.
- Add a way to optionally negate your Trophy Fish Chance.
- Make Crimson Swipe not be "magic damage"
  - This is slightly complicated to implement with our current damage system.
- Rework Rend as the current iteration cannot be balanced.
- Find a way to let players get the newly unsoulbound items back to let them sell their items if they so desire.
Here are some of the longer-term changes we have in mind:
- Make the lower tiers of Kuudra more accessible to players at lower gear levels.
- Make improvements to the reputation quest system.
- Revamp the mechanics of Crimson Isle minibosses.
## ► Final Comments
That's all we have in store for today! While the update has now officially released, there's still room for balancing changes relating to changes this release. Feedback from the last Alpha thread has been instrumental to polishing the update and ironing out issues. Your efforts are greatly appreciated by our team, and we hope to see more feedback from you all in the future.

# Changelog/2024/February 22
Categories: 2024 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[February 22] Supercraft Update
Updated: February 22, 2024
Hey all!
Today we are releasing two new features related to a very core element of SkyBlock: CRAFTING.
## ► Supercraft
Supercraft is a new way to craft items for [VIP] players and above. It lets you craft a lot more items, a lot faster!
You can access Supercraft by opening the Recipe page from anywhere it already exists, for example the Recipe Book, a lot of Recipe Items and NPC prompts. On top of that, there is the new /recipe <query> command, which lets you search every recipe in the game.
Hoping to Supercraft a Terminator
As seen in the screenshot, Supercraft is able to take items from your inventory, ender chests, backpacks and sacks. You're able to see a breakdown of where it takes items from aswell as a bit more information if you enable the "Advanced Mode" in your SkyBlock Settings. See Settings -> Personal -> User Interface -> Advanced Supercraft
Supercrafting 8 Amalgamated Crimsonite
Supercraft allows you set the amount of crafts that you want to do. For a lot of weapons and unstackable items, the limit is 1 craft at a time, but for items which can fit into your sacks, you're able to craft hundreds at a time and they will go into your sacks! [Note for our savvy players: Recipes which transfer data over like enchants ARE supported unlike Quickcraft, but you can only craft 1 at time. Although if you're crafting a Lapis Pickaxe and have multiple Iron Pickaxes, Supercraft will use a random one as the source item.]
## ► More Quickcraft Options
You are now able to view all Quickcraft options instead of just 3.
The "More Quick Craft Options" button in the Crafting menu
Once you open the menu, you will be able to see everything that you're able to Quickcraft based on what's in your inventory.
The full Quick Crafting menu
## ► General Changes
Lava Shell Necklace This suite of changes should clarify what the necklace does and how it interacts with other items.
Updated Lava Shell Necklace lore
- Clarified the behavior of Lava Shell Necklace in its lore. Its actual behavior did NOT change.
- Lesser Orb of Healing now specifies that it ignores Vitality, meaning it WILL heal through Lava Shell Necklace. This is again, only a lore change.
- The Ruby Power Scroll now applies a heal instead of directly setting your health, meaning it is now impacted by Mending/Vitality.
## ► Bug Fixes
Some bug fixes (unrelated to crafting) were shipped along with this update!
General
- Fixed the "Cooled Forges" perk granting 3x essence instead of 2x.
- Fixed the layout of the Co-op Management Menu for 8+ members.
- Fixed Byron's Compassion effect (from Gilded reforge) proccing when swapping weapons. It now only procs on melee hits.
- Fixed the recipe for Lava Water Orb showing vanilla items instead of the real ingredients.
- Fixed Sweet Flesh dropping outside of the Halloween event.
- Fixed the spider name in the Venom Shot cast message during Arachne.
- Fixed up the bazaar main menu layout.
Item Requirement Checks
- The following items now properly check if the player meets their use requirements:
- Lesser Orb of Healing
- Soulflow batteries accesory line
- Handy Blood Chalice
- Pocket Espresso Machine
Shen's Auction
- If the number of bidders is lower than the number of upcoming winners for an item, the minimum bid amount is now correctly the amount set in the auction settings rather than the smallest bid.
- Fixed negative bid increases.
Typoes
- Fixed a typo in Fetchur's compass dialogue.
- Added missing exclamation mark in Salesman's dialogue.
- Added a space in the Secrets action bar message.
- Fixed the Ultimate Carrot Candy Upgrade item lore to reflect new XP value.
- Fixed sometimes showing less than 0 clicks after Superpairs ends.
That's it for today! Enjoy!

# Changelog/2024/February 6
Categories: 2024 Updates, Updates
0.19.11
SkyBlock Patch Notes 0.19.11 - Stackable God Potions and Other Improvements
Updated: February 6, 2024
Hello!
Welcome to SkyBlock Patch 0.19.11. Today's update includes a bunch of miniscule yet fun changes as we continue to work towards the next content update. Read on to find out more!
## ► God Potion/Mixin Rework
We have done a bunch of major improvements towards God Potions and Mixins. To support these improvements, how we store the duration of God Potions has been reworked to significantly reduce the chance of God Potions being wiped in the future.
You can now stack the duration of your God Potions as long as your active God Potion duration is under 7 days. You no longer have to worry about wasting the remaining duration of your God Potion when you drink another one.
The God Potion description has been updated to show that you can stack your God Potion duration as long as your active God Potion duration is under 7 days. It also features a better breakdown of the God Potion’s duration modifiers.
The timer on your God Potion is now automatically paused while you’re on your private island. Don’t worry, you can still enjoy the effects of a God Potion while on your private island, it’s just that you’d no longer lose any time on your God Potion while being on your private island. Nifty, eh?
Mixins can now be consumed by right-clicking them as long as you currently have an active God Potion and Cookie Buff. Consuming a mixin has the same effectiveness of brewing a mixin into three God Potions - it will give you effects for 3x the duration of a God Potion. If you don't have an active cookie buff, you can still brew mixins into your God Potions the same way as before.
If you have any Mixins that haven’t expired when your God Potion expires, they will be paused.
Last but not least, a new filter setting has been added to your /effects menu to easily figure out what effects you currently have.
## ► Phasing out Enchantment Book Recipes
All enchantments that can be obtained through the Enchantment Table have their book recipes replaced by a 25% EXP discount for that enchantment. This was done as enchantment books are no longer required to obtain those enchantments. For enchantment book exclusive enchants such as Replenish, those crafting recipes still remain and the collection requirements are the primary way you unlock that enchantment.
## ► General Changes
### Items and Equipment Changes
#### Combat Items
- Added a new recipe for an upgrade to the Bone Necklace, requiring Thorn Fragments.
- Ender Bow’s ability cooldown has been reduced from 45 seconds to 5 seconds.
- Explosive Bow can now be used in dungeons and acts the same as Superboom TNT.
- Souls Rebound, Venom's Touch, and Spider Queen's Stinger can now be converted to dungeon items using Spider Essence.
- Void Sword can now be converted to a dungeon item using Dragon Essence.
#### Mining Items
- Added 2 new tutorial commissions to the Dwarven Mines.
- The despawn timer of Powder Ghasts will now reset every time it gets hit.
#### Misc. Items
- Bubblegum now buffs all Deployables instead of just Power Orbs.
### Other Changes
#### Garden Changes
- Beth will now always give an offer when visiting your garden instead of disappearing after her initial dialogue.
- Flowering Bouquet can now be put inside a composter providing 6k Organic Matter.
- Enchanted crops can now be used to paste plots in the Garden.
  - Excess crops that have not been placed are returned to the player.
  - If the player happens to be offline when the paste finishes, the items are stored and returned the next time the player warps to their Garden.
### Misc. Changes
- Added a little Bee pet next to Bea!
- Added an audible indicator when the Wither Skeleton jumps in the 'Test Of Control' Dojo challenge.
- Added Enchanted Flint to the lore of the Large Enchanted Mining Sack.
- Added Poison Sample to the Bazaar.
- Added Polarvoid Books to the Bazaar.
- Angry Archeologist has been renamed to Angry Archaeologist for the sake of consistency with other Archaeologists in SkyBlock.
- The following items are now salvageable:
  - Netherrack Looking Sunshade for 15 Crimson Essence
  - Pickonimbus 2000 for 5 Diamond Essence
  - Handy Blood Chalice for 15 Dragon Essence
  - Pocket Espresso Machine for 15 Dragon Essence
  - Treasure Talisman for 15 Gold Essence
  - Glacial Talisman for 15 Ice Essence
  - Tarantula Talisman for 50 Spider Essence
  - Scarf Studies for 15 Wither Essence
- The reward message from the Generow NPC has been improved for clarity.
- The Royal Resident now unlocks on the Garden after receiving the cake from him.
- The Witherborn ability (from all variants of Wither Armor) will no longer attempt to attack Garden pests.
- Updated most of the mythic pet coin values at George.
### User Interface and Messaging Changes
- Added a "Claim Your Listings Only" button to the Manage Auctions screen for co-op profiles
- Clicking the pets icon in your SkyBlock Menu now takes you to the pets menu.
- Edited Tia's garden visitor dialogue.
- George’s dialogue line when you hand in an exotic pet will now only show once per pet.
  - The taming level in the message 'You can now start working towards level 52' is now green.
- The Personal Bank upgrade in the SkyBlock Menu will now show information about the next interest.
- The Wizard will now show how long is left until your next free infusion.
## ► Bug Fixes
### Item Fixes
- Fixed Lava Rods sending missing requirement error messages twice.
### Mining Fixes
- Fixed a few more custom ores in the Dwarven Mines not being breakable.
- Fixed the Instant Transmission ability being usable in the Jungle Temple.
### Achievement Fixes
- Fixed an inaccuracy in the description of the United in Blood achievement where it doesn’t explain that the achievement can also be completed with a Gilded Midas Staff.
- Fixed an inaccuracy in the description of the Accessories Galore achievement incorrectly referencing the Accessory Bag as the “Talisman Bag”.
### Misc. Fixes
- Fixed the auction house referring to coins as gold.
- Fixed clicking anywhere inside an Item Frame giving an error saying 'You cannot take this item'.
- Fixed Mana Vampire enchant showing irrelevant decimals in the enchantment’s description.
- Fixed Void Sword referencing Ender Armor as “End Armor”.

# Changelog/2024/January 16/0
Categories: 2024 Updates, Updates
0.19.10
SkyBlock Patch Notes 0.19.10 - Fann Changes and Other Improvements
Updated: January 16, 2024
Hello!
Welcome to SkyBlock Patch 0.19.10. We’re releasing a bunch of small pets changes today mostly to improve Fann’s services introduced last update and a bunch of bug fixes. We’ve been working on new content that will hopefully be ready in the near future. Read on to find out more about this patch!
## ► Fann Changes
The introduction of Fann’s Pet Training services had a bunch of issues. We based Fann’s training rates off of the cost and XP rates of Carrot Candies. What we didn’t realise is how bad the XP rates of Carrot Candies were. In this patch, we’ll be increasing the amount of XP given by Carrot Candies and significantly lower Fann’s training costs again. Here are the changes as follows:
### Taming 60 Pet Submissions
Instead of having to give pets to George in a specific order, you can now hand in the pets in any order. You still have to submit one of each of those pets, but you can put off the ones that are hardest to obtain for later.
### Carrot Candy Buffs
Carrot Candies were released over three years ago in the 0.7.8 update. Throughout the last three years, skill XP has become a lot easier to come by due to increases in skill wisdom and overall better sources of skill XP. In contrast, the Pet XP rewarded by Carrot Candies has not changed throughout this period. Here are the values changes as follows:
### Fann’s Training Buffs
With Carrot Candies being buffed, we now have plenty of leeway to buff Fann’s Training Sessions. The tiers are now much cheaper and generally award much more XP. These changes should now make Fann’s Training an enticing alternative for levelling up some pets.
- The free tier scales from 20,000 EXP/day at Taming 10 to 40,000 EXP/day at Taming 50, then reaching 50,000 EXP/day at Taming 60.
- The cost of bits is assumed to be 1,000 Coins/bit.
## ► Other Changes
### Detailed Playtime Breakdown
The /playtime command has been enhanced to show where you’ve spent most of your playtime! Click on your playtime in chat to open up this detailed playtime breakdown. Hover over each island category to see your playtime in each island!
### Kat’s Bouquet
Kat’s Bouquet is five Kat Flowers bundled together. Hopefully it makes buying and using Kat Flowers slightly less tedious for pets with a long pet-sitting time.
### Items and Equipment Changes
#### Fishing Items
- Added the Fisherman Equipment Set, consisting of 4 pieces across the Prismarine, Sponge, Clay and Clownfish collections.
- Thunderbolt Necklace and Magma Lord Gauntlet can now roll for Blazing Fortune and Fishing Experience attributes.
#### Misc. Items
- Buffed Warped Stone's reforge with more intelligence (Rare: +65, Epic: +100, Legendary: +150).
- Dwarf Turtle Shelmet / Slime Hat will now work against the magma cubes from the Season of Jerry's Mount Jerry Eruption event.
- Reworded the Griffin Feather item.
- The Coin Talisman now also works on private and garden islands.
- Updated Rock The Fish description.
### Achievement Changes
- “Always Sunny” achievement will now require you to unlock the Island Time menu in the Garden.
- Fixed the "I am Groot" achievement not actually requiring 100 HP on the Growth Armor.
### Other Changes
#### Arachne Changes
- Luxurious Spool may now be salvaged for 1 Spider Essence.
- Your Spider Essence will now be shown in the sidebar while in Arachne's Sanctuary.
- The "ARACHNE DOWN" boss breakdown will now only be shown to damagers and players in the Sanctuary zone.
#### Mining Changes
- Added a new NPC to the Dwarven Mines called Silnar in the Cliffside Veins who will introduce players to the Cult of the Fallen Star and gift players with the Fallen Star Lozenge to help them track Fallen Stars.
- Adding a new uncommon commission to kill 10 Treasure Hoarders.
- Doubled the Precious Pearl drop rate from Nucleus Runs.
- Messages for Essence drops in Crystal Hollows Loot Chests are now condensed.
- Significantly increased the drop rate of Corleonite.
#### Misc. Changes
- Added "/skill [skill name]" shortcut command to open the skill's menu.
- Added a NPC Price of 2050 to the Agarimoo Tongue.
- Added a tooltip in the collection menu showing the total collected amount once maxed out (for solo players, as co-op already shows contributions).
- Added a way to remove invalid portal blocks from your island by right-clicking on them.
- Added Sleepy Hollow to the minion upgrades section of the bazaar.
- Arguments given to the /show command are no longer case sensitive.
- "Bleeds" hologram for Minotaur will now always go away.
- Guardian Lucky Block and Magma Core have been added to the Bazaar
- Pets will now spawn after exiting the Dojo challenge.
- Pigman has been removed from the Bestiary as it has no constituents.
- Player nicks will no longer be sent to the Bazaar, now only real player names will be shown in it.
- Previewing a Fire Sale through Elizabeth's Abiphone contact will not be available unless you're in the Hub.
- Sea Witch's splash potions will no longer damage other sea creatures.
- The bestiary icon will now no longer disappear when clicking Combat skill after viewing rankings.
- Updated garden milestone tooltip in sidebar.
### User Interface and Messaging changes
- Added an extra notification on login when you have coins refunded from leaving a Dark Auction.
- Added feedback to Soul's Rebound when attempting to use it on a Training Dummy.
- Fire Sale notifications will not be shown after disabling the chat feedback setting.
- Purchasing a Boo Stone from the Tyashoi Alchemist will no longer close the menu.
- Removed Secret Potion from the Recipe Book.
- The Rhys Quest to unlock the Dwarven Mines will now indicate which items have already been given.
## ► Bug Fixes
### Item Fixes
- Fixed being able to place the Weirder Tuba.
- Fixed Ice Cube having inverted logic.
- Fixed Personal Compactor not accepting Enchanted Bone Blocks as an item to compact.
- Fixed Prismapump sometimes being placeable and turning into regular Dark Prismarine.
- Fixed being able to put Potions inside the Personal Deletor.
  - You can no longer put potions inside the Personal Deletor.
- Fixed Heat Armor not properly applying the reduced heat chance.
- Fixed Livid Dagger’s backstab bypassing Arachne Brood’s damage cap.
- Fixed Mob Sounds staying muted when disabling Silence Blocks.
- Fixed Terminator not dividing the Magma arrows Crit Chance by 4.
- Fixed Treecapitator and Jungle Axe going into cooldown if the ability was not triggered.
- Fixed the Aurora Armor Set not actually increasing the damage dealt by the Aurora Staff ability.
- Fixed the Ether Transmission ability not correctly showing the mana cost reduction from Ultimate Wise in the ability’s description.
- Several Dojo's Belt issues have been fixed:
  - Rusty will offer belts based on claimed rewards and not total points.
  - Master Tao will now require a belt on upgrade.
  - Previous Tier belts will be upgraded regardless of the level of the belt.
### Alchemy Fixes
- Fixed being able to enhance and extend Secret Potions (from The Great Spook).
### Combat Fixes
- Fixed an issue where level 25 Goblins in the Dwarven Mines wouldn't drop Goblin Eggs.
- Fixed an issue where the Vampire Slayer leaderboard would not display because it's max level was below the level 6 requirement.
- Fixed collecting Whipped Magma Cream from minions not granting Combat XP.
- Fixed Creepers not naturally spawning on Private Islands.
- Fixed Mythic Black Cat perk working on all mob drops and not just collection item drops.
- Fixed players being able to hit other players Voidgloom Seraph Slayer Boss when they don't have the requirement for that boss.
- Fixed Slayer Minibosses not working with a few on-kill effects, like Dominus stacks from Crimson Armor.
- Fixed the Cleave enchantment and Cleave ability being able to damage villagers.
- Fixed Vampire Slayer Tier 1 description wrongfully listing the Stonewrath ability.
- Fixed Vanquisher not adding Nether Stars to sack if it's killed by another player.
- Fixed Void Sepulture Portal having the wrong Combat Level Requirement.
### Farming Fixes
- Fixed Melon/Pumpkin Minions filling all blocks with stems in some cases.
- Fixed some Garden NPC's being duplicated.
### Fishing Fixes
- Fixed an issue where the Farm Armor and Farm Suit Armor would not grant bonus speed near farming and animal minions.
- Fixed an issue where the Oasis Sheep and Oasis Rabbit gave Combat EXP instead of Fishing EXP.
- Fixed Soul Whip and Shadow Fury being able to deal damage to Sea Creatures when wearing Trophy Hunter armor.
- Fixed Worm Bait being unusable on Stranded profiles.
### Foraging Fixes
- Fixed an issue where Cocoa Beans would not regenerate in the Jungle Island at The Park.
### Mining Fixes
- Fixed an issue with Rhinestone Infusion where mining an ore would not give vanilla XP orbs.
- Fixed Flint from Gravel that has fallen not being affected by Mining Fortune.
- Fixed Gravel or Sand not giving Mining EXP when mined after falling.
- Fixed some Mithril Ore blocks in the Dwarven Mines not being mineable.
### Pet Fixes
- Fixed an issue where George would say the same line multiple times.
### Misc. Fixes
- Fixed /boostercookiemenu command working in the Rift.
- Fixed a display issue with the Abiphone Contacts Directory.
- Fixed a menu button in the Elizabeth Shop that said there was no Fire Sales going on when there was.
- Fixed an issue where Golden Ghoul, Crypt Ghoul, Golden Goblin and Diamond Goblin will copy their respective Bestiary kills.
- Fixed an issue where Sam would sometimes spawn inside of blocks.
- Fixed an issue where standing on a pressure plate with a boat in hand would trigger a "You cannot use this right now." message.
- Fixed an issue where items inside of sacks would not count towards NPC objectives.
- Fixed an issue with the Ironman symbol being missing from /show.
- Fixed being able to apply pet items while your pet is hidden.
- Fixed being able to bypass bank cooldown when using the /bank command.
- Fixed being able to use the /bank command when in combat.
- Fixed being unable to place gifts near the spawn bedrock on private islands.
- Fixed certain Bestiary Milestone tiers requiring more kills for lower tiers.
- Fixed certain Scoreboard lines being cut off during Jacob's Contests.
- Fixed Cookie Buff duration sometimes not being accurate.
- Fixed Dungeons saying you aren't able to open the Booster Cookie menu when you can.
- Fixed Elise sending wrong dialogue in the Wizard Tower before entering the Rift Gallery.
- Fixed Grinch and Ice Cream minion skins having a few misplaced textures.
- Fixed Healing Tissue and Wither Catalyst being in the wrong Bazaar Categories.
- Fixed Hex showing wrong item names and descriptions when the items are starred.
- Fixed “Island” items (e.g Desert Island, Winter Island) not having a rarity.
- Fixed “Item Age” in /viewitem not being formatted correctly.
- Fixed items in sacks sometimes being randomly ordered, they're now alphabetically sorted in the lore and menu.
- Fixed muted players being able to use Teleport Pads and Parkour Plates to communicate.
- Fixed needing to shift click soulbound items into the basic reforging slot.
- Fixed Pablo not giving reputation when handed the correct flower during his dialogue when you hand in the wrong flower.
- Fixed some players missing from player list when they join a server.
- Fixed some text in the bestiary mob ranking menu carrying over the color of the mob's name.
- Fixed Stranded Villagers exceeding the required trades level up but didn’t level up.
- Fixed Tammy's menu not showing information on the Horse.
- Fixed the clickable Gunpower Collection level-up message opening up the Sulphur Collection.
- Fixed the Crimson Isle and Slayer Sack Insta-Sell buttons missing in the Bazaar.
- Fixed the mini-wither spawned by the Witherborn ability persisting after swapping armor inside the Matriarch.
- Fixed the value of a Stonk being too high in the Museum.
- Fixed unranked players receiving message to claim Holidays Rewards from the Gingerbread Man in the Hub.
- Fixed Worm Bait not being tradable through the Bazaar.
