SkyBlock Update - January 16th 2024
Updated: January 16, 2024
Hello everyone,
After rolling today's SkyBlock update, we became aware of various critical issues. We are currently investigating and working on potential fixes for the below issues. We have turned off the logic that has caused issues #1 and #2 below.
1. Players being removed from coop profiles. RESOLVED
2. Players being readded to old coop profiles. RESOLVED
3. Taming levels being removed. RESOLVED
NOTE: The players being removed from coop profiles is only a visual issue and the coop data still exists. These coop profiles ARE NOT WIPED, it is purely a visual issue due to them being removed from the in-game menu.
UPDATE 1/16/24 5:30pm EST: Issues 1 and 2 have been resolved and profiles should be restored. Profile management should also be available again.
UPDATE 1/17/24 9:45pm EST: Issue 3 has been resolved. Impacted players have had their taming level restored.
We will continue updating this thread as we identify solutions to the above.
Thank you for your patience while we work on resolving these issues.

# Changelog/2024/July 2
Categories: 2024 Updates, Updates
0.20.3
Hypixel SkyBlock Patch Notes 0.20.3 - Better Mayors
Updated: July 2, 2024
This post is brought to you by Clerk Seraphine on behalf of the Hypixel SkyBlock Mayor's Coalition.
Greetings, citizens of Hypixel SkyBlock! We're proud to announce that the highly anticipated changes to Mayors are ready to go! This update comes with changes to a many perks that each mayor provides, ranging from brand new perks, additions to previous offerings, and more!
These changes are not limited to Mayor perks, however, as some changes are active regardless of whether a certain mayor is elected for the greater good of our world.
This update definitely has something for everyone! Let's dive right in.
## ► Community Center Refurbishment
The Community Center's refurbishment is now complete! Mayors now have a dedicated area to stand at instead of loitering next to the Election Room. We've shuffled things around in the Community Center around to give the place a new look.
....
Well... almost everything. Elizabeth was too stubborn to move, so she is staying at the same location.
Many of you were also surprised that the clock on top of the Community Center actually worked. What's the point of a clock if it doesn't work?! We even hinted at this in the City Project!
## ► Election System Changes
The first Election of Hypixel SkyBlock was in Year 88. In the real world, that's almost 4 years ago! We've gained and lost a few mayoral candidates here and there, but nothing much has changed to the mayor system overall. However, this is all about to change...
### ➥ A New 4th Perk
The maximum amount of perks that a mayor can have is now 4. Many Mayors have gained an additional perk, while others have had their entire lineup of perks revamped! All of this is detailed further in the post.
### ➥ Perk Rates
When a Mayor has 1 or 2 perks, they now have a 50% chance to gain an additional perk (increased from 45%).
When a Mayor has three perks, they have an 8% chance to gain an additional perk.
### ➥ Minister System
Ministers are the runner up Candidates in an Election. Every Mayor will have a perk chosen as a Minister Perk at the beginning of every Election. If they become the Minister, this perk becomes active!
A Minister Perk will not be featured when a Special Mayor is elected as they prefer not to share the spotlight with anyone else.
### ➥ Visibility of Election Votes
Due to popular demand, we are trialing a voting system where we alternate between showing and not showing the votes for the current Election.
There are merits to both showing and hiding an Election's votes. We are fine with players working together to find the best Mayor and Minister combination during each Election cycle, but can also see the charm in learning who is elected as Mayor when the Election ends and votes are hidden.
Please do let us know what you'd prefer!
## ► Updated Mayor Perks
### ► Marina
#### New Perk: Double Trouble
For every 1% of Sea Creature Chance, gain 0.1% chance to double hook sea creatures (+0.1 Double Hook Chance).
Marina features a modest yet powerful new perk takes fishing yields to a whole new level while her perks are active.
Double Hook Chance has now also officially been incorporated as a stat, with Giant Fishing Rod and the Double Hook attribute been retconned to utilise the stat instead.
### ➥ Individual Sea Creature Cap
An individual sea creature cap of 5 in the Crimson Isle and 20 in the Crystal Hollows has been instated in response to concerns that fishing in general (especially during Marina) is an awful experience with "barn fishers" hogging the Sea Creature Cap.
You're still welcome to barn fish on other islands where you're not impacting anyone else!
### ➥ Lava Fishing Stat Changes
The Lava Sea Creatures in the Crimson Isle have mysteriously gotten weaker. This should be convenient for fishing enthusiasts.
|Lava Sea Creature|HP|Damage|
|-|-|-|
|Magma Slug|1,000,000 → 500,000|8,000 → 6,000|
|Baby Magma Slug|200,000 → 100,000|4,000 → 2,500|
|Moogma|2,500,000 → 750,000|9,000 → 7,000|
|Lava Leech|3,000,000 → 1,000,000|10,000 → 8,000|
|Pyroclastic Worm|2,500,000 → 1,200,000|9,000 → 8,000|
|Lava Flame|3,000,000 → 1,500,000|11,000 → 9,000|
|Fire Eel|4,000,000 → 2,000,000|12,000 → 9,500|
|Taurus|5,000,000 → 3,000,000|13,000 → 11,000|
Advancements in Lava Fishing Rod productions has resulted in the following changes to their damage and strength. How did the Magma Rod end up weaker?
|Lava Fishing Rod|Damage|Strength|
|-|-|-|
|Starter Lava Rod|50 → 100|50|
|Magma Rod|150|150 → 120|
|Inferno Rod|170 → 210|170 → 150|
|Hellfire Rod|180 → 270|180|
### ► Diana
#### New Perk: Sharing is Caring
You can have up to 3 EXP shared pets active at once. Your EXP Share rate is increased by 10%.
Diana now features a new perk that allows you to share Pet EXP to more pets at once, allowing you to level up your arsenal of pets faster!
The incorporation of this perk also comes with a rework to the EXP Sharing system and a favourites feature in your pets menu to pin your favourite pets.
#### ➥ EXP Sharing Rework
Rather than using the Exp Share item, you can simply use a menu to chose what Pet you want to EXP Share with. Your 'EXP Share rate' also goes up by 0.2% per Taming Level, up to a maximum of 12% at Taming LX.
By default, you will only have one "active" EXP Share Slot. When Diana is in office with her 'Sharing is Caring' perk, two more EXP Share Slots will also become enabled. Those slots will no longer provide EXP when Diana leaves office, but you can still add/remove pets from those slots - they're not stuck in there
#### ➥ EXP Share Indicators
The three slots within the EXP Share menu have colorful indicators to help indicate your pet's current status within the EXP Share Slot.
If the glass pane indicator is green, your pet currently has less than the Pet EXP required for Lvl 100 at Legendary rarity and is actively gaining Pet EXP, meaning all is well!
If the glass pane indicator is Blue, your pet currently has more than the Pet EXP required for Lvl 100 at Legendary rarity. It's still actively gaining Pet EXP but won't be gaining any more pet levels.
If the glass pane indicator is Red, the slot is either inactive as there's currently not pet assigned, or the slot is disabled as the "Sharing is Caring" perk is not active.
#### ➥ What happened to the EXP Share item?
Instead of enabling the system as a whole, the EXP Share item now boosts the effectiveness of Pets receiving EXP Share by 15%. This item stacked with the 12% Exp Share rate from Taming LX means that you can reach a total of 27%, which is higher than the original items 25% (outside of Diana's 10% boost).
#### ➥ Favoriting Pets
We've taken the time to add some minor QoL to the Pets menu. With this feature, simply Shift + Left-Click to favorite a Pet. Favorited Pets show up at the front of your Pets menu so that you can access them easily.
### ► Finnegan
#### New Perk: Blooming Business
Garden Visitors will give out Fine Flour and appear more often. Additional visitors may visit your Garden.
Higher rarity Visitors are more likely to show up & will provide 10% more Copper.
#### New Perk: Pest Eradicator
The duration of Pesthunter Phillip’s Farming Fortune bonus is now 60 minutes.
Pests are now 4x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.
Finnegan has updated his perk offerings to keep up with the times. His first new Mayor perk revolves around a new obtainable item that Garden Visitors hand out whilst the Blooming Business perk is active. The other perk Pest Eradicator interacts with the Pest system which should make Pest drops much more accessible.
Farming Simulator has been removed from Finnegan's roster of perks.
#### ➥ Fine Flour
While Blooming Business is active, Garden Visitors will reward you with Fine Flour for completing their requests in addition to their regular rewards.
The amount of Fine Flour a visitor gives out is dependent on their rarity:
UNCOMMON - 1 Fine Flour
RARE - 3 Fine Flour
LEGENDARY - 5 Fine Flour
MYTHIC - 10 Fine Flour
#### ➥ Four New Visitors
The winds of trade have attracted four new Visitors to your Garden. The Baker, the Vinyl Collector, the Pest Wrangler, and the Ravenous Rhino may now show up at your Barn when Blooming Business is in town!
These Garden Visitors generally offer a higher amount of Fine Flour than other Visitors relative to their rarity, with the exception of the Baker giving a bountiful Copper reward for trading in Fine Flour. Once you've served 50 unique Garden Visitors, the Ravenous Rhino has a chance to appear on your Garden featuring a guaranteed Dedication IV book!
#### ➥ New Pelt Shop Items
While we're at it with Finnegan changes, we've also added a couple new items to Talbot's Shop while the Pelt-pocalypse perk is active:
- Douce Pluie de Stinky Cheese Potion
  - Costs 40 Pelts
  - Grants a 20% Bonus Pest Chance increase.
  - This potion effect is not included in the God Potion!
- Brown Bandana (Pet Item)
  - Costs 200 Pelts
  - Grants a 0.2% Bonus Pest Chance increase for each Pest Bestiary level you have.
- Pest Vest (Cloak)
  - Costs 250 Pelts
  - Grants a 10% Bonus Pest Chance increase and decreases the time required to spawn a pest by 20%.
### ► Cole
#### New Perk: Molten Forge
Decreases the time it takes to forge items by 25%.
#### Updated Perk: Mining Fiesta
Schedules 5 Mining Fiesta events that last for 7 SkyBlock days each. Earn 2x drops and extra unique loot, including Refined Minerals and Glossy Gemstones. Grants +75 ☯ Mining Wisdom during the event which is only active on Public Islands.
Cole's new perk should help alleviate the issues with Forge timers taking too long. More mining changes are coming soon to address forging time and other issues, but this perk should still be a welcome addition for those who frequent the Forge, especially with the need to forge keys to loot corpses in the Glacite Tunnels!
The Mining Fiesta event has also been split into 5 events lasting 7 SkyBlock days (2h 20m each), which will make it easier for more players to attend the event.
#### ➥ New Items
The Dwarven experts have recently perfected a few more forging and crafting recipes that conveniently require Refined Minerals and Glossy Gemstones, both items that can be obtained during the Mining Fiesta event.
- Mithril Equipment (HOTM 2)
  - A simple equipment set geared towards players starting off their mining journey.
- Titanium Equipment (HOTM 3)
  - A slightly more advanced equipment set for players who have gotten the hang of mining in the Dwarven Mines.
- Gemstone Equipment (HOTM 5)
  - Handcrafted Gemstone-based equipment that's able to withstand the grit and grind of Gemstone mining.
- Divan's Powder Coating (HOTM 7)
  - A powdered substance synthesized from Divan Fragments that can be applied to Drills and Armor of Divan (incl. equipment).
- Gleaming Crystal Reforge Stone (HOTM 4)
  - Increases your mining efficiency (Mining Speed and Fortune) by harnessing the condensed power of gleaming crystals.
- Glossy Mineral Armor (Crafting Recipe, upgrade to Mineral Armor)
- Glossy Mineral Talisman (Crafting Recipe, upgrade to Mineral Talisman)
### ► Foxy
#### Updated Perk: Sweet Benevolence
Increases your chances of dropping Candy and Gifts by +30% during the Spooky Festival and Season of Jerry.
#### New Perk: A Time for Giving
Party Chests and Party Gifts can be obtained while this event is active.
#### New Event Perk: Chivalrous Carnival
Schedules a Carnival in the Hub that is active throughout the entire year.
Take on fun activities to earn Tokens and exchange them for perks and special items!
Foxy wasn't happy with the lack of partying over the last few (hundred) years, so she's giving it her all this time round.
Foxy's whole schtick is events, so her perks revolve heavily around the various events we have in Hypixel SkyBlock. Her A Time for Giving perk allows players to obtain Party Chests and Party Gifts, which are souped up versions of Trick or Treat Chests and Winter Gifts. She even has her own flagship event in the form of a Carnival that's active when she has the Chivalrous Carnival perk!
To make room for more offerings, Foxy's Sweet Tooth and Benevolence perks have been merged into one named Sweet Benevolence.
#### ➥ Party Chests and Party Gifts
Foxy's A Time for Giving perk introduces two new items: Party Chests and Party Gifts. These items are only obtainable while the perk is active, and can drop some new items!
Party Chests
Party Chests are earned throughout the Spooky Festival, including the hour before and after the event. Party Chests outright replace Trick or Treat Chests, and they have a chance to drop Carnival-related items such as the Carnival Necklace & Belt!
Party Gifts
Party Gifts are earned by killing Winter Sea Creatures whilst on the Winter Island. Higher rarity Sea Creatures have a higher chance to drop one. Party Gifts also have a chance to drop Carnival-related items, such as the Carnival Globes and Cloak!
Buffed Loot
On top of the new rewards, Party Chests and Party Gifts also buff the existing loot found in Trick or Treat Chests and Red Gifts. What might've been 4 Ectoplasm in a Trick or Treat Chest might now be 6 Ectoplasm, for example.
### ⭐ Chivalrous Carnival
Foxy’s Chivalrous Carnival perk introduces a bunch of carnival games to the world of Hypixel SkyBlock!
We highly recommend enabling SkyBlock music while in the Carnival, @ShinkoNet made some incredible songs for this event!
#### ➥ Carnival Tickets
The Chivalrous Carnival has 3 games within it, each requiring 5 Carnival Tickets per play.
You can talk to the Carnival Leader every day to get 25 Carnival Tickets for free. Carnival Tickets can also be earned by participating in many of SkyBlock's calendar events such as the Spooky Festival, Fishing Festival, and Jacob's Farming Contests. You can obtain Carnival Tickets through events even when the perk isn't active!
#### ➥ Carnival Ticket Rates
- Jacob's Farming Contest
  - Diamond Medal: 3 Carnival Tickets
  - Gold and Platinum Medals: 2 Carnival Tickets
  - Silver and Bronze Medals: 1 Carnival Ticket
- Spooky Festival
  - Top 50: 50 Carnival Tickets
  - Emerald Bracket (5000 Points): 35 Carnival Tickets
  - Diamond Bracket (2000 Points): 25 Carnival Tickets
  - Gold Bracket (1750 Points): 20 Carnival Tickets
  - Iron Bracket (1500 Points): 15 Carnival Tickets
  - Stone Bracket (1000 Points): 10 Carnival Tickets
  - Wood Bracket (100 Points): 5 Carnival Tickets
- Fishing Festival
  - 0.1% drop rate from Nurse Sharks: 1 Carnival Ticket
  - 0.15% drop rate from Blue Sharks: 1 Carnival Ticket
  - 0.25% drop rate from Tiger Sharks: 1 Carnival Ticket
  - 0.5%: Great White Shark: 1 Carnival Ticket
- Party Chests
  - 20.83% from Party Chests
#### ➥ Carnival Games
There are 3 Carnival Games available at the Chivalrous Carnival that will be available for as long as the perk is active, minus 5 minutes before the Carnival ends as the workers wrap things up!
#### Fruit Digging
The goal is to earn as many points as possible by digging up fruit scattered around a 7x7 sandbox. There are 8 different fruits each with different points values, as follows:
Quantity of fruits per board is indicated in [brackets]
- Mango [10] grants a flat 300 points.
- Apple [8] grants 100 points for each Apple you've dug up to that point.
- Watermelon [4] grants 100 base points and blows up an adjacent fruit for half the points value.
- Pomegranate [4] grants a base 200 points and increases the points earned from your next dig by 1.5x.
- Coconut [3] grants a base 200 points and prevents a bomb from exploding if you mine it during your next turn.
- Cherry [2] grants a base 200 points. Digging up a second Cherry grants 500 points instead.
- Durian [2] grants a base 800 points and decreases the points earned from your next dig by 0.5x.
- Dragonfruit [1] grants a flat 1,200 points.
15 Bombs are also planted within the sandbox. You don't lose any score when mining them, but they will destroy nearby fruit.
The Carnival Pirateman seems to be storing a stash of Rum within the sandbox. There's 5 Rum buried within the sand in each game. Digging up Rum disables your ability to dowse for a turn.
Your Carnival Shovel is equipped with a dowsing functionality that comes in three modes: Mines, Anchor, and Treasure. Your dowsing ability automatically triggers after you mine a block as long as you didn't dig up Rum beforehand.
- Mines tells you how many mines are adjacent to the block you've mined.
- Anchor tells you the location of the lowest scoring fruit near the block you've mined.
- Treasure tells you the highest scoring fruit that's near the block you've mined.
#### Catch a Fish
The objective of Catch a Fish is to... catch fish. Catch as many as possible before time runs out by hooking onto them and holding right-click to reel them in! There are different types of fish that give a different amounts of points, some are also harder to catch! Maintain your catch streak to experience a small points bonus per catch!
- Red Fish have a 60% chance to spawn and award 50 points on catch.
- Blue Fish have a 30% chance to spawn and award 100 points on catch.
- Yellow Fish have a 10% chance to spawn and award 200 points on catch.
- Gain 5 Bonus Points multiplied by your streak per catch, up to 50 bonus points per catch!
#### Zombie Shootout
Using your trusty Carnival Dart Tube, kill as many Zombies within the arena before the time runs out! Zombies wearing higher tier armor grant more points per kill, but move faster and are harder to hit. Zombies have a 10% chance of spawning as a Baby Zombie, which award double the points but run even faster.
- Leather Zombies award 30 points on kill.
- Iron Zombies award 50 points on kill.
- Gold Zombies award 80 points on kill.
- Diamond Zombies award 120 point on kill.
- Redstone Lamps are stationed around the arena. Shoot them to earn 100 points and a 1.2x points boost for the next 6 seconds!
#### ➥ Scoring System and Carnival Tokens
Each of the three Carnival Games are scored in points. Earning 100 points gets you 1 Carnival Token. Carnival Tokens can be spent at the Mask Shop or the Event Shop, both found within the Carnival.
#### ➥ Carnival Shop
The Carnival Shop contains both the Mask Shop and the Event Shop, which exclusively use Carnival Tokens as currency!
#### Mask Shop
The Mask Shop sells a Carnival-exclusive item type - Carnival Masks! Each mask has an exclusive ability that's situationally useful, but they're mostly just masks you can wear to embrace the Carnival's festive spirit. Masks can be kept in your Carnival Mask Bag for storage and ease of access, granting you Intelligence for each stored Carnival Mask!
#### Event Shop
The Event Shop sells perks for various SkyBlock events. These include perks for the Spooky Festival, Jerry's Workshop, Mining Fiesta, and Fishing Festival. Purchase them for permanent buffs!
#### ➥ Stats and Rewards
We are tracking stats for all of your Carnival Game attempts! You can talk to Chantelle to check out your cumulative and yearly stats for all of the Carnival Games.
Each time a Carnival is held, Chantelle offers refreshed objectives for players to complete. These give players significant rewards for your first few plays of each of the Carnival Games, resetting with each new Carnival.
#### ➥ Commemorative Items
If you've gotten at least 1,000 points in a Carnival Game, you will earn that game's commemorative item. These will make it easy to keep track of all of your high scores and positions from year to year. Once the Carnival ends, these are automatically sent to your Event Rewards Menu.
### ► Diaz
#### New Perk: Volume Trading
Double the item quantity from Shen's Auction, Shen's Special, and Rift Shen's on the year Diaz is elected. Two additional Shen’s Special auctions will be available for the duration of Diaz being elected as Mayor.
#### New Event Perk: Stock Exchange
Diaz is bringing more Stock of Stonks into circulation. Stock of Stonks can be obtained from the Stonks Auction and winning bids for items in the Dark Auction and Shen’s Auctions. Talk to Diaz while she’s elected with this perk to bid for Stock of Stonks and trade in your Stock of Stonks in for some cool new items!
#### New Perk: Long Term Investment
The elected Minister will appear in the next Election with all of their available perks.
As the worst-performing Mayor candidate, Diaz's current goal is to not come in last again. For starters, she's had her whole squad pull up at the Trade Center - a newly refurbished area located just behind the Bazaar that can also be accessed with the /warp stonks command, featuring a bevy of functions within the area. Stock Exchange's featured Stonks Auction and the functionality to increase the amount of coins into your Midas items are all located at this area.
Diaz is also putting her connections to good use. She's pulled a few strings to get a collaborative effort going with Shen's Auctions. Her connections are even cross-dimensional! As self-centred as Barry is, he took pity on Diaz's paltry election performance and has agreed to double the item quantity from Rift Shen's too.
Diaz no longer offers Barrier Street as a perk and has chosen to retain Shopping Spree as her last perk - who doesn't enjoy a good shopping spree?!
#### ➥ Moar Shen’s!
With this update, Shen's Auctions will be offered twice a year rather than just once.
Mayor Diaz's Volume Trading perk will do three things:
- Double the number of Shen's Auctions yet again for that year (for a total of 4)
- Double the number of items at all Shen's Auctions for that year
- A new permanent item will be added to Shen's Special (if there are items available)
Friendly Disclaimer: Only Shen's Auctions that end during Diaz's term will have doubled items.
#### ➥ Stock of Stonks
Stock of Stonks were inadvertently dumped into this world by someone just before the Bazaar opened its doors. Diaz has seemingly repurposed the Stock of Stonks concept to perpetuate her own ambitions.
You can now trade in Stock of Stonks for the following new items:
- Golden Fragment
  - Costs x3 Stock of Stonks
  - Use x8 Golden Fragments to upgrade one of the following:
    - Daedalus Axe
    - Midas' Sword
    - Midas Staff
- Avaricious Chalice
  - Costs x50 Stock of Stonks
  - Use x1 Avaricious Chalice along with other items to upgrade the Emerald Ring to the Emerald Artifact
  - Use x2 Avaricious Chalice along with other items to upgrade the Crown of Greed into the Crown of Avarice
- Freshly-Minted Coins
  - Costs x1 Stock of Stonks (capped at 5,000 global purchases every 4 hours)
  - Used in various amounts to obtain the following:
    - Scavenger Ring/Artifact
    - Ring/Artifact/Relic of Coins
- Golden Bounty
  - Costs x20 Stock of Stonks
  - Allows you to upgrade Scavenger 5 on your weapon to Scavenger 6!
#### ➥ Obtaining Stock of Stonks
It wouldn't make sense to introduce a bunch of new items without a way to obtain them.
#### Stonks Auction
You can access the Stonks Auction by talking to Richard at the trade center. These auctions are global, with one happening each SkyBlock month (every ~10 hours). The Top 100 bidders each receive 25 Stock of Stonks, followed by 15 Stock of Stonks for Top 500, 10 for Top 1,000, and so on.
#### Auction Bidding
Winning an item at any of Shen's Auctions will additionally reward 10 Stock of Stonks.
#### Relic of Coins
The Relic of Coins has a 1/40,444 chance of dropping a Stock of Stonks every 60 seconds.
#### ➥ Zarina
Zarina is an NPC that allows you to increase the amount of coins you put in your Midas Sword and Midas Staff. This allows players to upgrade their existing Midas items with Golden Fragments then sink coins accordingly to make the item’s ability stronger. You can also find her at the Trade Center!
## ► Other Mayor Changes
### ► Derpy
After some hard-fought negotiations with Derpy, we've got him to not shut down the auction house while he's in charge. He'll be settling for quadruple the taxes from Bazaar and Auction House transactions instead. Phew!
### ► Jerry
Jerry has reluctantly removed Diaz from his Perk-pocalypse rotation as the various financial services required for Diaz are on holiday while Jerry is mayor. As a result, the other perks will appear more frequently.
### ► Future Plans
#### ➥ Primal Dragons
While playtesting this update on Alpha, we ran into many large scale performance issues. Unfortunately, we have shelved this update for the time being due to these issues.
#### ➥ Many Major and Miner Mining Changes
Since the Glacite Tunnels update back in March, we have been working to make several improvements to mining as a whole, in preparation for Part 2 of the Glacite Tunnels. This update includes many things, including a Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter, new Forge Slots, and new "Dwarven" Vanilla Ores in the Royal Mines (coal, iron, diamond, etc.)! This update is scheduled to hit Alpha soon for balancing and bug fixing.
#### ➥ Foraging and Hunting
Since the last Design thread, we’ve made significant progress on the Foraging updates. We’d be ready to launch it on alpha if not for some technical difficulties with the latest versions. Consequently, we still can’t provide a precise release date. We would like to explain our issues with latest versions further in the near future, should time allows.
On the bright side, this extra time allows us to further design and develop the Hunting update, which we would prefer to release alongside Foraging. We’ve reviewed all your feedback on Hunting and made several adjustments. We’re excited to share more details with you soon.
That's it, folks! We hope that you're as excited as we are with these changes. I'd like to thank everyone for their feedback regarding the update throughout the alpha testing process and hope you enjoyed your mementos. Special thanks to SkyBlock Nerds for helping conceptualize and balance a bunch of these perks.
Looking forward to see you at the community center soon!

# Changelog/2024/July 9
Categories: 2024 Updates, Updates
0.20.4
Hypixel SkyBlock Patch Notes 0.20.4 - Bazaar Overbidding Protection and Bug Fixes
Updated: July 9, 2024
Hello!
Welcome to Hypixel SkyBlock Patch 0.20.4. Today’s update features a small patch with a Bazaar Scam Prevention feature, some clarifications and fixes for Better Mayors content, and a lot of bug fixes. Enjoy!
## ► Bazaar Overbidding Prevention
We’ve noticed that materials and products in the Bazaar have often been bought out and set to ludicrously high prices by unscrupulous actors hoping that someone would accidentally buy those overpriced items one way or another. We’re introducing some restrictions to limit the damage that can be done and hopefully help tackle this issue.
- A confirmation popup will now appear if you’re attempting to purchase an item that’s over 2x of the item’s average price over the last 7 days.
  - If you choose to confirm your purchase, this popup will not appear for the next 5 minutes.
- Similarly, a confirmation popup will now appear if you’re attempting to insta-sell an item for less than half of the item’s average price over the last 7 days.
  - If you choose to confirm your sale, this popup will also not appear for the next 5 minutes.
  - This confirmation popup will not appear if you are insta-selling multiple items at once, either through selling all items in a sack or sell all items in your inventory/sacks.
- To prevent players from setting sell orders for ridiculously high amounts, your sell offer value now has a cap of 10x the item’s average price over the last 7 days.
- You can check an item’s average price over the last 7 days by hovering on an item with Advanced Mode active.We hope these changes help prevent players from getting scammed and makes using the Bazaar a more pleasant experience.
## ► Better Mayors Changes and Fixes
### Known Issues
- Hidden Elections were disabled for the current Election due to an issue in our backend system. The upcoming Election will feature hidden votes.
- There’s currently an issue where the Minister can appear in the next Election despite them supposedly taking a break before campaigning again. This will be fixed in the near future.
### Changes and Fixes
- You can now interact with Richard when Diaz is not in office.
- Mineral Armor no longer breaks too many blocks.
- Shen’s Auction items can no longer be claimed by talking to Richard.
- Fixed a visual bug where Midas Staff items would display an incorrect Price Paid value.
- Fixed build issues with the Community Center.
- Fixed typos in the Exp Share menu.
- Fixed typos in Diaz's Volume Trading description.
## ► Other Changes
### Item Changes
- Ender Bow now properly indicates the maximum amount of damage it does.
- Kat Flowers can now stack in your inventory.
- Pesthunter Ring, Badge and Artifact now send a message when enabled/disabled.
- Sorted the Fear Mongerer's shop to be a little bit less clinically insane.
- Updated the skins of the Mender Helmet line of helmets.
### Pet Changes
- Added indications to the Golden Dragon's perk Dragon Greed as to what caps it has.
- Clarified that upgrading the Wisp Pet resets its level in the upgrade stone's lore.
- Pet Candy and Pet Items can now be applied to pets that are always visible, regardless of visibility setting.
- Updated the lore of Pet Candy and Pet Items to better indicate how they work.
### Misc. Changes
- Enchanted Seeds now appear in the Recipe Book.
- Gemstones now increment the Compact enchantment counter, but have no special drop when it triggers.
- Mineshaft owners would sometimes get messages from other player's HOTM perks, now it will message each player individually based on what perks they got.
- The Photon Pathway skip on the Rift will automatically be unlocked now after you talk to Deer.
- Trophy Fish progress on SkyBlock XP tasks now properly grants progress if you've fished a tier above the one listed in the task.
## ► Bug Fixes
### Item Fixes
- Fixed Archfiend Dice rolls not applying the health decrease.
- Fixed being able to place torches with the Infinitorch for free.
- Fixed Cacao Truffles effect lasting longer than intended.
- Fixed Fallen Star Helmet not showing up in Rusty's Shop after meeting the requirements.
- Fixed Flaming Sword being overvalued in the Museum.
- Fixed Infernal and Fiery Kuudra Keys not showing Co-op Soulbound despite being co-op soulbound.
- Fixed Scarleton Premium not having any item description.
- Fixed Spirit Shortbow's passive dealing more damage than intended.
- Fixed using a Ditto Blob on a Sack making an item with an error tooltip.
### Combat Fixes
- Fixed an issue where Phantom Rod would not deal damage in Dungeons as berserk class.
- Fixed death message being incorrect when a player is killed by a Slayer miniboss.
- Fixed dying from stepping on Bladesoul coal block saying "0 damage".
- Fixed Endstone Protector loot organization in Bestiary menu.
- Fixed not gaining Bestiary progress when killing Butterflies with abilities.
### Mining Fixes
- Fixed an issue where players could enter the Glacite Tunnels and donate fossils earlier in progression than intended.
- Fixed being unable to claim the chests in the Jungle Temple when collecting the Amethyst Crystal first.
- Fixed Crystal Hollows structure loot chests not giving essence.
  - Updated the reward message given when claiming these chests, as well as the lockpick chests.
- Fixed Custom Ore Blocks not being able to spawn Jerrys during the Jerry Mayor Event.
- Fixed more Mithril Blocks in the Dwarven Mines not being breakable.
- Fixed players gaining HOTM experience when max level.
- Fixed players with the free Crystal Hollows pass being unable to be party warped into the Crystal Hollows.
- Fixed Precision Mining always being on even when the perk is not selected.
- Fixed some unbreakable Dwarven Gold blocks in the Dwarven Mines.
- Fixed the Looting Corpse reward message stating Earned HOTM EXP when you are already HOTM Level 10.
- Fixed the Maniac Slayer Mining Commission stating the wrong zone to complete it in.
- Fixed the Powder Buff HOTM perk applying when disabled, such as with Fetchur and Puzzler.
### Pet Fixes
- Fixed flight being toggled off when despawning the Phoenix Pet with an active Cookie Buff.
- Fixed Pets converted to items getting deleted if your inventory and stash are full.
- Fixed the Armadillo Pet's Rolling Miner pet perk not working in the Glacite Tunnels and Mineshafts.
- Fixed the Auto Pet rules that trigger when dungeon bosses spawn not working in Master Mode.
- Fixed the Hound Pet Scavenger perk not displaying the coin increase on the Scoreboard.
- Fixed the Parrot Pet not properly mirroring "ez" messages.
- Fixed the Spider and Tarantula Pets' Web Battlefield pet perk not reporting its stats correctly.
### Misc. Fixes
- Fixed "/collection sulphur" opening the gunpowder collection.
- Fixed "It worked?!" achievement triggering when no items are in the transfer chest.
- Fixed a bug where some users were able to put unintended items into Hex menus through shift click.
- Fixed all players hearing Enigma Soul flower pot noises when someone is doing that challenge.
- Fixed an issue in the Rift where the inventory wouldn't update after equipping equipment in latest versions.
- Fixed an issue with Trophy Fish descriptions that included the Obfuscated trophy fish.
- Fixed Anniversary Hats sometimes displaying the wrong name in the Museum Menu.
- Fixed being able to apply Enchanted Redstone Lamps to Mushed Glowy Tonic potions despite not affecting the potion in any way.
- Fixed being able to suffocate the Silverfish in the Dungeon puzzle room.
- Fixed Billy Joe telling you to put accessories in your inventory.
- Fixed collection cost discounts not affecting enchantments when directly applied through an anvil.
- Fixed Gravel in the Spider's Den not regenerating all the destroyed blocks due to them falling.
- Fixed Hellstorm Wand being shown inside the Gemstone Guide.
- Fixed inconsistent Metaphysical Serum task title in the SkyBlock Guide.
- Fixed Incorrect True Defense values on Fervor Armor.
- Fixed Mushroom Soup flight duration not showing up on the Scoreboard.
- Fixed not being able to drag soulbound items into the Hex.
- Fixed not being able to open certain RNG Meter Menus using the /rng command.
- Fixed not being able to put Vanilla Music Discs in Jukeboxes.
- Fixed placed Summoning Eyes awarding better dragon loot if you swapped to a different profile after placing them.
- Fixed Power Scrolls being applicable to Abiphones.
- Fixed Rabbit effect being disabled when disabling the Jump Boost effect.
- Fixed Shop Confirmations button disappearing after disabling it.
- Fixed some deployables missing the "RIGHT CLICK" text.
- Fixed some unbreakable log blocks in the Park.
- Fixed the Candy Talisman line of accessories not retaining recombobulation on upgrade.
- Fixed The Hex not taking items properly (e.g Heavy Pearls) from sacks.

- Fixed Carrolyn refers to chocolate bars as "cocoa beanss".

# Changelog/2024/March 12
Categories: 2024 Updates, Updates
0.19.12
SkyBlock Patch Notes 0.19.12 - Tablist and Other Changes
Updated: March 12, 2024
Hello!
Welcome to SkyBlock Patch 0.19.12. Today's update includes the long-awaited Tablist update, a new dungeon accessory in the General’s Medallion, various pet changes and buffs, a bunch of Garden and Pests QoL, and more!
Continue reading for more details.
► Status Update
The Garden was disabled for ~6 hours upon the release of the update as we encountered an unforeseen issue that affected Garden Milestones and some other features. We apologize for any inconvenience this may have caused.
## ► Tablist Widgets
Ever felt the need to tweak what shows up on your tablist? Well, now you can! We’ve added a new setting that allows you to rearrange your tablist based on your own preferences depending on which island you are currently on using Widgets.
The tablist is now made of Widgets, each Widget revolving around one particular piece of content with ways to customize the content and appearance. Want quick access to your bank info? There's a Widget for that. How about keeping tabs on your Bestiary progress? You got it. You have the option to select which Widget you want to display and further customize how you want this information to be portrayed.
On top of that, we’ve added a bunch of new Widgets that you can display on the tablist from what you’ve been normally used to. For example, you can now track your Bestiary kills, view timers for Dark Auction or Experimentations, show additional Trophy Fish info, and much, much more.
## ► General’s Medallion
Some keen-eyed players may have noticed an unobtainable accessory was added to the game. With this update, General’s Medallion is now up for grabs!
General’s Medallion is an accessory that gives you an extra stat boost while in dungeons. You will gain a 1% stat boost for each digit on your secret count. This stat boost is additive with your dungeon stat boost from increasing your catacombs level.
General’s Medallion can be obtained by purchasing it from Zodd, who resides in a room that’s not been seen before.
### ➥ Secret Count Changes
We have been tracking the amount of secrets you’ve obtained on the player itself instead of per profile for the past four years. As this wouldn’t be ideal for the accessory, we’ve utilized some dark magic to split up your secrets into portions and assigned them to all eligible profiles. The ratio of how your secrets are split between your profiles is based on the amount of secret bat kills you’ve had. Secrets will be tracked on the player’s profile instead of on the player itself going forward.
With this change, you can now check your secret count through the /secretcount command!
### ➥ Architect’s First Draft
Architect’s First Draft is a new secret item you can obtain in Dungeons. You no longer have to fear your teammates failing Tic Tac Toe, as you can now reset failed dungeon puzzles with the Architect’s First Draft!
The drop rate of the Architect’s First Draft is the same as a Treasure Talisman.
## ► Garden QoL
Pests have been a solid addition to the Garden and helped make Farming more active while still maintaining player agency through features such as Bonus Pest Chance and Pest Repellent. This small update to the Garden focuses on further improving the pests gameplay loop while also cleaning up certain areas of the Garden, such as the SkyMart menu.
### ➥ General Garden Changes
- Visitors will now spawn at 15-minute intervals while the player is not in the Garden, up to a maximum of 5 visitors.
- Added a ‘Bulk Claim’ button to Jacob’s contest rewards menu.
- Added the ‘Swift Slicer’ ability to the Cactus Knife, which increases the player’s speed cap by 100 while in the Garden.
- Cacti can no longer destroy items.
- Some dialogue has been added to contests affected by Finnegan’s GOATed perk to make players aware of the more lenient rankings.
- Finishing in the Platinum bracket in a Farming Contest will now award 70 Bits.
- Finishing in the Diamond bracket in a Farming Contest will now award 80 Bits.
- Separated the SkyMart Menu into 3 distinct sections (Farming Essentials, Barn Skins, and Pests).
- The Garden Desk is now accessible through the SkyBlock Menu.
- The player’s Strength is once again shown in TAB when Mushroom Cow is equipped.
### ➥ Pest Changes
#### Pest Mechanics
- Pests now spawn a lot more frequently than they did before.
  - The cooldown of 5 minutes before spawning another pest (2.5 minutes with a spray active) has not changed.
  - This change affects the chance of getting a pest after the aforementioned cooldown is over.
- Pests can no longer leave the plot they spawn in.
- Pests will now always spawn on the highest Y level.
- Pests will now spawn at an hourly rate while the player is not in the Garden, up to a maximum of 8.
- Pests will no longer spawn in plots that haven’t been unlocked.
- Earthworms may no longer go down on the Y axis when burrowing.
- Moths are now attracted to lamps (glowstone blocks and beacons)!
- Players may now vacuum pests through blocks, and the range of all vacuums has been increased to 15 blocks.
- Killing a Pest now awards 5 Bits if the player has a Booster Cookie active.
#### Pest Drop Chances
In addition to the above changes to pests, some pest-related drop chances have also been changed. Take a look below!
|Pest Type|Drop|Old Drop Chance|New Drop Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|All|Dung|4%|10%|
|All|Tasty Cheese|4%|10%|
|All|Plant Matter|4%|10%|
|All|Compost|4%|10%|
|All|Honey Jar|4%|10%|
|Locust|Enchanted Baked Potato|1%|3%|
|Cricket|Enchanted Golden Carrot|1%|3%|
|Mosquito|Enchanted Sugar Cane|1%|2%|
|Moth|Enchanted Cookie|1%|3%|
|Mite|Enchanted Cactus|1%|2%|
|Beetle|Mutant Nether Wart|1%|3%|
|Earthworm|Enchanted Melon Block|1%|4%|
#### Other Pest-related Changes
- A message will now be sent to the player when their Sprayonator effect runs out.
- Added the ability to track pests to the SkyMart Vacuum.
- Added the Portable Washer item to SkyMart. This item can be used to rid your Garden of all active Sprayonator effects.
- Added a toggle for the Bonus Pest Chance stat to the Pesthunter accessory line.
  - This toggle is located on the accessory itself.
- Common crop drops from pests will now show up in chat, and the player may hover over them for a more detailed explanation of how pest drops work.
- Improved the text coloring of the ‘x Bonus Pest Spawned!’ hover text.
- Improved the text coloring of the Atmospheric Filter accessory.
- Instead of dying when falling into the void, pests will now teleport back to their spawn location.
- Particles from the pest tracker now appear faster.
- Pesthunter Phillip’s ‘Empty Vacuum Bag’ prompt now shows the player how much Farming Fortune they will receive upon emptying their vacuum.
- Pesthunter Phillip’s menu now explains how Farming Fortune reductions work to the player, and how many Pests need to be spawned for each reduction tier.
- Players with a Booster Cookie may now toggle the ‘Pest Repellent’ and ‘Pest Repellent MAX’ potion effects on/off.
- The Pesthunter Handbook now shows the player all of the possible drops of each Pest, along with their drop chance.
  - With the Pesthunter Handbook now containing so much information, it has been moved into Pesthunter Pamela’s menu.
- The Sprayonator’s itemlore now shows which pests the selected material attracts.
- Reduced Sprayonator cooldown from 5s to 3s.
## ► Pet Buffs and Reworks
It’s time for some changes to pets that have typically been underutilized! Not all of the pets involved in this update are underutilized, but all of the changes should make the pets better. Many of these changes were submitted and/or adapted based off of feedback from SkyBlock Nerds.
Quick note that the all damage buffs listed below are additive damage modifiers unless specified to be multiplicative with a “x” indicator. (e.g 1.2x the amount of damage).
### ➥ Ghoul Pet
#### Base Stats
- The Vitality from “Amplified Healing” should become a pet stat instead. The “Undead Slayer” perk (see below) should replace “Amplified Healing”.
- Add Mending to the pet’s base stats equal to the amount of vitality it has.
#### Undead Slayer (Replacement of “Amplified Healing”)
- Gain 1.005x ➜ 1.5x Combat XP against Zombies.
#### Reaper Soul (Rework)
- Reaper Soul reduces the summoning cost of mobs by 0.3% ➜ 30% and increases their damage output by 0.2% ➜ 20%.
- Reword the part about health of summoned mobs to “Increases the health of all summoned mobs by 1 ➜ 100%”.
#### Army of the Dead (Replacement of “Zombie Arm”)
- Increase the amount of souls you can store and summon by 2. Also increases the chance of getting a mob’s soul by 0 ➜ 20%.
### ➥ Giraffe Pet
#### Base Stats
- Add +0.01 ➜ +1 Swing Range to the pet’s Base Stats.
#### Good Heart (Rework)
- Gain +x Health Regen.
  - Common: 0.015% ➜ 1.5%
  - Uncommon, Rare: 0.02% ➜ 2%
  - Epic, Legendary: 2 ➜ 200
#### Long Neck (Rework)
- Increases your melee damage by 50% ➜ 100% if you are more than 3 blocks away from the target.
#### Higher Ground (Rework)
- Increase your Crit Damage and Strength by 0.0015% ➜ 0.15% for every 0.1 Swing Range over 3. Capped at 6 Swing Range.
  - There are many ways you can gain swing range and figure out how to best utilize the Giraffe Pet. You can easily reach the 6 Swing Range cap while in dungeons as Berserker, or cobble together an interesting combination of items in the overworld to reach the 6 Swing Range cap. Hopefully this opens up more options to players and enjoy theorycrafting what works best!
### ➥ Golem Pet
#### Base Stats
- Add +0.01 ➜ 1 Swing Range to the pet’s base stats.
#### Last Stand (Rework)
- While at less than 20% HP, reduce incoming damage by 20%. Additionally, gain a temporary shield equal to 40% of your maximum health and deal 40% more damage. (Lasts 12 seconds, 60s cooldown).
#### Toss (Buff)
- Toss deals 5x damage per hit. Cooldown reduced to 5s.
### ➥ Hound Pet
#### Base Stats
- Change the Attack Speed stat to +0 ➜ +25 Attack Speed.
#### Pack Slayer (Replacement of “Fury Claws”)
- Gain 1.005x ➜ 1.5x Combat XP against Wolves.
#### Finder (Rework)
- Change the scaling of the perk to 0.3% ➜ 30% chance increase to find Armor.
  - This is done as the current 10% is overshadowed by literally anything that gives Magic Find.
### ➥ Megalodon Pet
#### Base Stats
- Add +5 ➜ +10 Ferocity to the pet’s base stats.
- Add +0 ➜ 40 Fishing Speed to the pet’s base stats.
- Add 0 ➜ 10 Sea Creature Chance to the pet’s base stats.
#### Blood Scent (Rework)
- Deal up to +50% ➜ +150% Damage based on the enemy's missing health.
#### Enhanced Scales (Rework)
- Doubles the pet's base stats during the Fishing Festival.
#### Feeding Frenzy (Rework)
- Increases your chance to catch Sharks during the Fishing Festival by 20%.
### ➥ Pigman Pet
#### Giant Slayer (Rework)
- Deal +50% extra damage to monsters level 50 and up. Deal +75% extra damage to monsters level 100 and up.
  - This used to be a +25% additive modifier (that is very bad compared to every other pet) that rarely procs due to there being very little level 100 mobs in the game.
### ➥ Skeleton Pet
#### Bone Arrows (Rework)
- Increase arrow damage by +0 ➜ +75% (at Legendary). This effect is doubled while in Dungeons.
  - This damage is an additive modifier.
#### Skeletal Defense (Bug Fix)
- The arrow your skeleton shoots will no longer bounce.
### ➥ Snowman Pet
#### Blizzard (Rework)
- Enemies within 8 ➜ 16 blocks are slowed by 25% and deal 0.10% ➜ 10% less damage.
  - This effect does NOT stack with multiple Snowman pets!
#### Frostbite (Nerf)
- The damage reduction granted by this perk has been reduced from 15% to 10% with the buff to the Blizzard perk.
#### Snow Cannon (Rework)
- Shoot a snowball towards an enemy when you attack.
  - The snowman does NOT attack by itself anymore, it now only triggers when you attack.
- The snowball damage is equal to 10% ➜ 20% of the last melee damage hit you dealt.
- Cooldown: 1 Second
### ➥ Spider Pet
#### Base Stats
- Change the Strength stat to +0 ➜ +50.
#### One With The Spider (Rework)
- Add x Strength to every piece of Arachnal weapon, armor, and equipment that you have equipped. (applied to base stats).
  - +1 ➜ 3 Strength at Common, 0.02 per level.
  - +2 ➜ 6 Strength at Uncommon, 0.04 per level.
  - +3 ➜ 9 Strength at Rare, 0.06 per level.
  - +4 ➜ 12 Strength at Epic, 0.08 per level.
  - +5 ➜ 15 Strength at Legendary. 0.1 per level
- Holding an Arachnal weapon counts as having it equipped.
- Spider-related items, such as Tarantula Armor, Arachne Equipment etc. have been assigned the Arachnal tag for easier reference.
### ➥ Turtle Pet
#### Base Stats
- Change the Health stat to +50 ➜ +75.
- Change the Defense stat to +100 ➜ +150.
- Add True Defense 0 ➜ +15 to the pet’s base stats.
#### Turtle Tactics (Rework)
- Gain +3.27 % ➜ +30% Defense. Standing still will grant another +10% Defense.
#### Genius Amniote (Rework)
- Grants +1% ➜ +2.5% Defense to up to 4 players within 50 blocks of you.
  - This effect does NOT stack with multiple Turtle pets!
#### Turtle Shell (Rework)
- When under 40% maximum HP, take 0.25 ➜ 25% less damage. Gain +10 ➜ +20 Vitality for 15 seconds after taking 10 hits.
  - Hits are instances of damage.
  - 10 instances of damage will grant you the Turtle Shell vitality bonus. If you already have the bonus, it will refresh the duration of the vitality buff.
### ➥ Wither Skeleton Pet
#### Wither Blood (Rework)
- Deal +125% more damage to wither mobs.
  - Contrary to what it may seem, the change from a 25% multiplicative to a 125% additive modifier is actually a lot stronger for most players. For players a lot further into progression where this change is a slight nerf, you have other pets at your disposal that deal more damage than Wither Skeleton in general anyway.
### ➥ Zombie Pet
#### Bite Shield (Rework of “Chomp”)
- Reduce damage taken from Zombies by x% ➜ y%.
  - Common: 5% ➜15%
  - Uncommon, Rare: 10% ➜ 20%
  - Epic, Legendary: 15% ➜ 25%
#### Rotten Blade (Rework)
- 25% Damage to Zombies ➜ Deal 150% more damage to Zombies.
#### Living Dead (Rework)
- 0.25 ➜ 25% Stats on Undead Armor (instead of 0.2 ➜ 20%).
#### Unlock Requirement
- The Zombie Pet craft is now unlocked at Zombie Collection 6 instead of Zombie Collection 9.
- The Enchanted Rotten Flesh required for the Zombie Pet’s crafting recipe has been reduced from 512 (8 stacks) to 128 (2 stacks).
  - This makes the pet more accessible to its intended new-player audience!
## ► Other Changes
- The quiver arrow system has been reworked to improve visibility and fix some annoying issues:
  - The quiver arrow item in the inventory is now a client-side only item and therefore should no longer randomly wipe items that were in that slot (e.g. during the Thorn boss-fight).
  - The quiver arrow item in your inventory has been updated to show which arrow is being used and how many are left in your quiver.
  - The quiver will now prioritise the strongest arrow in your quiver instead of the first available in the slot if you haven't selected a specific type using the arrow swapper.
  - Several situations in which the quiver arrow didn't update or randomly disappeared after a shot have now been fixed.
- Arachne Fangs and Arachne Fragments have been added to the Bazaar.
- Adjusted the drawbacks from using regular Ender Pearls in The End:
  - There is now a warning message after using your first Ender Pearl.
  - Subsequent Ender Pearls thrown within 30 seconds will spawn 1 aggressive Enderman and deal 10% true damage.
  - Nearby Endermen will no longer aggro when using Ender Pearls.
  - Silent Pearls can now be crafted after unlocking the Ender Pearl I Collection.
- Added Pests to Stranded Bestiary.
- Damage dealt from punching is now affected by your Strength Stat.
## ► Bug Fixes
### Item Fixes
- Fixed Fragged Bone Necklace not being donatable to the Museum.
- Fixed Fragged Bone Necklace not increasing radius properly after being upgraded.
- Fixed not being able to add Enchanted Shark Fins into Personal Compactors.
- Fixed Raw Rabbit not being cookable in Furnaces.
- Fixed Tasty Cheese not going into Garden Sack when spawned by a Rat pet’s CHEESE! ability.
- Removed unneeded decimal from the Fervor Armor description.
### Autopet Fixes
- Fixed left-clicking furniture triggering the Autopet "Enter Combat" rule.
- Fixed some mob types options being blank in the "You kill a [mob]" rule in Autopet.
### Farming Fixes
- Fixed an issue where the Garden Paste menu would display incorrect amounts for Mushrooms.
- Fixed an issue with Garden Pastes not being able to pull some crop items out of sacks.
### Misc. Fixes
- Fixed an issue where flower pots from the "Flowery Message" Enigma Soul would break.
- Fixed an issue where the Personal Bank upgrade would not be usable for Bingo profiles.
- Fixed an issue with /show where messages from ignored players were still visible.
- Fixed an issue with Power Orbs not despawning.
- Fixed temporarily disabled NPCs being accessible through their Abiphone contact.
- Fixed not being able to start the Precursor Ruins - Anything with Runback race in the Dungeon Hub.
- Removed an incorrect Wooden Hoe Recipe being displayed from the Mushroom Minion Recipe.

[March 18] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: March 18, 2024
Hey!
We have just rolled an update to SkyBlock that contains several bug fixes and small changes.
- Fixed Summoning Eyes count not showing correctly in the tablist.
- Fixed not being able to select Fishing Bestiary families on The Park tablist.
- Fixed election candidates perk count sometimes being incorrect in the tablist.

- Fixed "Knowledge is Power!" achievement awarding when equipping a pet with the Textbook item.

If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!
