# Changelog/2024/October 29
Categories: 2024 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[October 29] Backend Skin Changes
Updated: October 29, 2024
Hello!
Today we released some changes to how we handle skins in our backend. This is a technical change to how we display skins in a variety of contexts, items, mobs, cosmetics, and much more.
We are aware that some items aren't stacking when they're supposed to. This is happening because you have items with both old and new NBT data. To update your items, simply drop them and pick them back up and they'll stack again.
That's all for today!
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!

# Changelog/2024/September 17
Categories: 2024 Updates, Updates
0.20.6
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.20.6 - Modernized Mining, Dye Changes, and more!
Updated: September 17, 2024
Hello!
Today we are bringing you Hypixel SkyBlock update 0.20.6, with massive changes to the Mining Skill, some changes to Dyes, and more! Here is the TLDR:
- Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter
- Revamped Heart of the Mountain
- New Forge Slots and Pure Ores
- Mining Balance Overhaul
- Dye Drop Chance Changes
- Various Quality of Life Changes
- Bug Fixes
Keep reading if you want to know all of the details!
NOTE: We are currently aware that Farming Fortune (and the Pig Pet) are currently not working as intended and are looking into fixing it.
## ► Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter
1,000 xp is earned per run!
We've introduced an RNG Meter to the Crystal Nucleus, allowing you to guarantee a drop of your choice after completing a certain number of runs. You will retroactively receive Nucleus XP for runs you've completed at a 25% rate.
We've also changed how Divan's Alloy drops from the Nucleus: rather than having a dynamic drop rate, the drop rate will always be ~1/1,000 runs.
## ► Revamped Heart of the Mountain
The Heart of the Mountain has been almost entirely overhauled. Most perks were either moved, changed, or deleted, and there are also quite a few new ones! Our goal was to get rid of all of the less useful perks and replace them with more useful ones. We've refunded everyone's Powder and reset their trees for free so that everyone can try the new Heart of the Mountain out!
We've also made some additional changes to the Heart of the Mountain:
- Added a warning for when you mine with unspent HotM tokens
- Disabled HotM perks now appear as Redstone Dust
- You can no longer switch to a different Pickaxe Ability while you have one currently active or on cooldown
## ► Other Mining Changes
### ➥ New Stats
Mining Fortune Variants
Similar to individual Crop Fortunes, Mining Fortune is getting 4 sub types that stack on top of your base Mining Fortune. There are Ores, Blocks, Dwarven Metals, and Gemstones. Which Fortunes apply to what can all be found in the newly revamped Handy Block Guide, accessed through Fragilis or /hbg!
Affected by Ore Fortune
- Coal Ore / Pure Coal (new!)
- Iron Ore / Pure Iron (new!)
- Gold Ore / Pure Gold
- Lapis Ore / Pure Lapis (new!)
- Redstone Ore / Pure Redstone
- Emerald Ore / Pure Emerald
- Diamond Ore / Pure Diamond (new!)
Affected by Block Fortune
- Cobblestone / Stone
- End Stone
- Glowstone
- Gravel / Flint
- Hard Stone
- Ice
- Netherrack
- Mycelium
- Obsidian
- Red Sand
- Sand
Affected by Dwarven Metal Fortune
- Mithril
- Titanium
- Umber
- Tungsten
- Glacite
Affected by Gemstone Fortune
- Ruby
- Amber
- Sapphire
- Jade
- Amethyst
- Topaz
- Jasper
- Opal
- Aquamarine
- Citrine
- Onyx
- Peridot
Mining Spread
This stat is your chance to break an additional block when mining, and it stacks above 100 similar to Fortune. If you have 350 Mining Spread, you are guaranteed to mine 3 additional blocks and then have a 50% chance to mine a fourth. Mining Spread is compatible with Ores, Blocks, and Dwarven Metals.
Gemstone Spread This stat works just like Mining Spread--but for Gemstones! The key difference here compared to how it worked previously is that Gemstone Spread is compatible with Pristine, making it super powerful!
Heat Resistance Just like Cold Resistance, Heat Resistance improves your survivability when exposed to Heat. We've also taken the time to revamp how Heat builds up so that it's more lenient, building up slower and dispersing faster.
Heat Buildup Changes Old System + While in the Magma Fields: +1 Heat/s + While in lava: +5 Heat/s - While not in the Magma Fields: -0.5 Heat/s - While in water: -1 Heat/s - Drinking a water bottle: -40 Heat
New System + While in the Magma Fields: +0.1 Heat/s + While in Khazad-Dum: +0.2 Heat/s (takes priority over Magma Fields) + While on fire: +1 Heat/s + While in lava: +3 Heat/s (takes priority over fire) - While not in the Magma Fields: -1 Heat/s - While in water: -5 Heat/s - Drinking any potion: -10 Heat
### ➥ Pure Ores
We've revamped the Dwarven Ores (such as Dwarven Gold and Emerald) into "Pure" Ores, along with adding pure variants of all the remaining vanilla ores. The Royal Mines now also contains a large variety of these Pure Ores.
### ➥ New Items
- Precursor Apparatus
  - Crafted using all 6 Automaton Parts
  - Can be used to instantly complete the Sapphire Crystal Quest or forge certain items
- Haste Artifact
  - Crafted using a Haste Ring and Enchanted Cobblestone, unlocked at Cobblestone IX
  - Grants additional Mining Speed and a higher level of Haste
- Royal Compass
  - Earned from Commission Milestone I
  - Points towards the nearest Emissary or The King (if he is closer)
- Dwarven Treasure
  - Earned occasionally from Dwarven Events
  - Equipment Reforge Stone that grants some early game mining stats
- Flowstate Enchantment
  - Earned occasionally from Dwarven Events
  - Ultimate Enchantment for Mining Tools, grants Mining Speed based on consecutive blocks broken
- Titanium Minecart
  - Earned occasionally from Dwarven Events
  - Pet Item that grants Mining Fortune on Titanium
- Titanium Tesseract
  - Forged using Refined Titanium and Enchanted Lapis Lazuli
  - Armor Reforge Stone that grants some early game mining stats
- Polished Topaz Rod
  - Forged using Flawless Topaz Gemstones and Bejeweled Handles
  - Lava Rod that's useful for fishing in the Crystal Hollows as it reduces your Heat on catch
- Rusty Titanium Pickaxe
  - Purchased from Bubu, this is the new first tier of the Titanium Pickaxe line
  - Pickaxe that grants significant Mining Fortune on Titanium, although lacks Mining Speed
- Blazen Sphere
  - Dropped from Lava Blaze Sea Creatures
  - Equipment Reforge that grants good mining stats
- Starfall Seasoning
  - Forged using Starfall and Treasurite
  - Drill Upgrade Module that provides Mining Speed & Fortune, which is doubled when near Fallen Stars
- Pocket Iceberg
  - Forged using Glacite Jewels
  - Mining Tool Reforge that grants Breaking Power
- Dwarven O's
  - Obtained through various means, there are 4 variations (one for each type of sub fortune)
  - Consuming them grants a permanent +2 stat bonus for the sub fortune (up to 5 times)
- Filet O' Fortune
  - Can be purchased from Brynmor for 100,000 coins
  - Consumable that grants Mining Fortune for a duration
- Chilled Pristine Potato
  - Can be purchased from Brynmor for 250,000 coins
  - Consumable that grants Pristine while in Glacite Mineshafts for a duration
### ➥ Miscellaneous
We made many, many, miscellaneous changes to mining that didn't really fit into any other section. The goal for most of these changes is to tie up any loose ends in progression and to improve the early game mining experience. Many are also just slight improvements to make your mining experience just a little better!
### ➥ Balancing
Many changes were made across the board to spread out Mining Fortune across more parts of mining and improve underutilized gear and items.
We probably missed a few changes to mining, there were too many to list! A more comprehensive breakdown on all the changes and why we made them can be found on the original Alpha playtest thread.
## ► Dye Changes
### ➥ Drop Rate Changes
- Periwinkle Dye's drop rate now scales based on mob level
  - Level 1-99 Runic Mobs: 1/500k > 1/500k (unchanged)
  - Level 100-199 Runic Mobs: 1/500k > 1/400k
  - Level 200-299 Runic Mobs: 1/500k > 1/300k
  - Level 300-399 Runic Mobs: 1/500k > 1/200k
  - Level 400+ Runic Mobs: 1/500k > 1/100k
- Midnight Dye drop rate now drops more frequently from regular Spooky Festival mobs
  - Werewolf, Nightmare, Scarecrow: 1/1m > 1/1m (unchanged)
  - Trick or Treater, Wither Gourd, Phantom Spirit, Scary Jerry, Wraith, Crazy Witch: 1/1m > 1/500k
  - Headless Horseman, Phantom Fisher, Grim Reaper: 1/1m > 1/50k
- Iceberg Dye now only drops from Winter Sea Creatures
  - Frozen Steve, Frosty: 1/1m
  - Grinch, Nutcracker: 1/500k
  - Yeti, Reindrake: 1/50k
- Aquamarine Dye now only drops from traditional Sea Creatures
  - Squid, Sea Walker, Night Squid, Sea Guardian, Sea Witch, Sea Archer, Rider of the Deep, Water Worm, Poisoned Water Worm, Agarimoo: 1/5m
  - Guardian Defender, Deep Sea Protector, Carrot king, Oasis Sheep, Oasis Rabbit: 1/500k
  - Water Hydra, Sea Emperor, Abyssal Miner: 1/100k
- Pelt Dye Drop Rate Changes
  - Trackable Animals: 1/1m > 1/250k
  - Untrackable Animals: 1/750k > 1/200k
  - Undetected Animals: 1/500k > 1/150k
  - Endangered Animals: 1/250k > 1/100k
  - Elusive Animals: 1/250k > 1/10k
- Frostbitten Dye Drop Rate Changes
  - Umber and Tungsten Corpses: 1/250k > 1/100k
  - Vanguard Corpses: 1/50k > 1/10k
- Celadon Dye Drop Rate Changes
  - Bacte: 1/20k > 1/10k
  - Blobbercyst: 1/100k (new!)
- Nadeshiko Dye Drop Rate Changes
  - Supreme Experiment: 1/50k > 1/75k
  - Transcendant Experiment: 1/25k > 1/50k
  - Metaphysical Experiment: 1/10k > 1/25k
- Mocha Dye Drop Rate Changes
  - T1 Potions: 1/100m > 1/100m (unchanged)
  - T2 Potions: 1/5m > 1/5m (unchanged)
  - T3 Potions: 1/2.5m > 1/2.5m (unchanged)
  - T4 Potions: 1/1.5m > 1/1m
  - T5 Potions: 1/1m > 1/750k
  - T6 Potions: 1/750k > 1/500k
  - T7 Potions: 1/500k > 1/250k
  - T8 Potions: 1/250k > 1/100k
- Brick Red, Celeste, Matcha, Byzantium, and Flame Dye Drop Rate Changes
  - T1 Slayer: 1/15m > 1/10m
  - T2 Slayer: 1/5m > 1/2.5m
  - T3 Slayer: 1/2.5m > 1/1m
  - T4 Slayer: 1/1m > 1/500k
  - (Matcha Only) T5 Slayer: 1/500k > 1/250k
- Sangria Dye Drop Rate Changes
  - T1 Slayer: 1/150k > 1/100k
  - T2 Slayer: 1/100k > 1/80k
  - T3 Slayer: 1/75k > 1/60k
  - T4 Slayer: 1/50k > 1/40k
  - T5 Slayer: 1/25k > 1/10k
- Tentacle Dye Drop Rate Changes
  - Kuudra Basic Tier: 1/250k > 1/100k
  - Kuudra Hot Tier: 1/200k > 1/80k
  - Kuudra Burning Tier: 1/150k > 1/60k
  - Kuudra Fiery Tier: 1/100k > 1/40k
  - Kuudra Infernal Tier: 1/50k > 1/20k
- Cyclamen Dye Drop Rate (from Cinderbats): 1/2.5m > 1/250k
- Dark Purple Appearance Rate: 1/500 > 1/400
- Fossil Dye Appearance Rate: 1/1m > 1/500k
- Jade Dye Drop Rate: 1/2m > 1/500k
- Holly Dye Drop Weight: 0.00025 > 0.000125 (twice as rare)
- Dung Dye Drop Rate: 1/500k > 1/250k
- Carmine Dye Drop Rate (from Plhlegblast, Thunder, and Lord Jawbus): 1/250k > 1/50k
Please note that the Chocolate Dye will be changed at a future date - we haven't forgot about it.
### ➥ Other Changes to Dyes
- Special Zealots can now drop Pearlescent Dye.
- Secret Dye now goes directly into your inventory when found.
- Dyes can now be previewed directly from the Dye Compendium.
- Updated the Dye Donation menu to better illustrate that Fire Sale Dyes cannot be donated.
- Listed drop chances for a Dye in the Dye Compendium now always show from rarest to most common.
- Added an Anvil to the Artist's Abode so that you can apply Dyes more easily.
- Swapped the positions of the Bucket of Dye and Dye Compendium in Vincent's menu.
- Fixed Holly Dyes dropping from all Gifts, rather than just Red Gifts.
- Fixed not being able to claim the Dark Purple Dye out of a Dark Auction after just winning it in a Dark Auction.
## ► Other Changes
### ➥ Dungeon Changes
- Added a button for quickly salvaging uncommon rarity items to the salvage menu.
- Changed an outdated Dungeon Quiz question that asked about a Painter in The Hub.
- Ice Fill puzzle now rewards 2 Blessings (was 1).
- Ice Fill puzzle now only spawns on Floor 7 (was Floor 3 and above).
- Higher or Lower puzzle is now only in Floor 3 and above (was Floor 2).
- Quiz Answers will now appear twice as quickly.
- Sped up the animation that plays for the Higher or Lower Puzzle.
### ➥ Misc. Changes
- All instances of Scavenger VII on weapons are now Scavenger VI.
- The Golden Dragon pet's 'Shining Scales' perk now caps out at 100m Gold Collection instead of 1b.
  - The maximum stats you earn are still the same, you just earn them faster
- All references to "The Garden" are now colored green.
- Added /stats as an alias for /equipment.
- Added 2 Combat Slots to the Phantom Rod.
- Added a +1x base multiplier to the Chocolate Production icon for clarity (visual only).
- Added a Diamond Jacob's Contest Emblem for getting a Diamond Medal in all crops.
- Added a Private Island Bestiary for Bats (200 to max).
  - This is also tracked retroactively!
- Added a progress bar to the Lifetime Chocolate Milestones item lore.
- Added Chocolate Syringes and Mana Disintegrators to the Bazaar.
- All Deployables now use coherent item lore to describe their abilities.
- Bingo profiles can now use /bank.
- Changed the colors of the spawning particles so that they align with the Slayer Boss you are spawning.
- Changed the warp pad message colors for the Election Room and Community Center.
- Each Pest turned in to Phillip now gives a flat +5 Farming Fortune, rather than scaling (still caps at 40 Pests).
- EXP Share now automatically disables once a Pet reaches Level 100.
- Gave the Lazy Miner's Pickaxe the Efficiency I enchantment.
- Items dropped from breaking Minion Storages will now go directly into your sacks.
- Moved the Glacite Golem Pet to the Master tier of the SkyBlock Guide (was the Skilled tier).
- Overhauled Kat's dialogue for when she is Pet Sitting so that it is more informative.
- Pure Mithril Gems can now be salvaged for 100 Diamond Essence.
- Removed the chat message mentioning you picked the lock on a Crystal Hollows Treasure Chest.
- Selling Lil' Pads to Kay in The Rift now has a confirmation menu.
- Separated Boss Corleone from the Grunt Bestiary (100 to max).
  - This is tracked retroactively.
- Slayer regen timer is now paused while Riftstalker Bloodfiends are in Mania.
- Supercrafting Minions no longer gets rid of Mithril Infusions.
- Updated the item lore of Ditto Skulls to better describe their functionality (visual only).
- Updated the textures for Beetle and Cricket Pests.
- Using /getfromsacks without specifying a value now pulls out only 1 item.
- The limit on Abiphone Contact Trios has been increased to 15 (up from 12).
- Vincent will now have a Mole Pet equipped after you have earned his Abiphone contact.
- You can now purchase EXP Bottles while in the Experimentation Table menu.
- Zones of the Deep Caverns will now unlock for teleporting even if you haven't talked to the Lift Operator.
## ► Bug Fixes
### ➥ Mining Fixes
- Fixed Ascension Rope item lore not mentioning it could be used to get out of Glacite Mineshafts.
- Fixed Fortunate using Mithril Powder instead of Gemstone Powder.
- Fixed outdated text in a Glacite Tunnels commission description.
- Fixed outdated text in an error message for the Lift Operator.
- Fixed outdated text in an error message for Vanguard Corpses.
- Fixed the Pickonimbus 2000 not counting as an EPIC item in the salvage menu.
- Fixed the Sapphire Power Scroll not displaying the fact that it has a cooldown.
- Fixed Treasure Chests saying you need to aim at 0 particles even though you just maxed the Great Explorer perk.
- Power Scrolls can now be applied to mining tools with Pickaxe Abilities on them.
- Power Scrolls can now be applied to weapons with Necron's Blade Scrolls applied (Hyperion).
- Updated Sky Mall description so that it now mentions "all Mining Islands" rather than just the Dwarven Mines.
  - It already worked this way, the description was just outdated.
### ➥ Other Fixes
- Fixed /gfs not working properly for certain Gemstones.
- Fixed a misleading error message ("too much light") from Mushroom Minions.
- Fixed an error when clicking a Slimeball in the daeL recipe.
- Fixed an exploit that allowed for players to get higher than intended Speed while in The Rift.
- Fixed an instance where balloons spawned from Bonzo's Staff could go through walls.
- Fixed an issue where Tier XII Minions were not being visually counted in the SkyBlock XP task progress bar.
- Fixed an issue where you were able to reset your Heart of the Mountain multiple times in succession.
- Fixed an issue with the Active/Inactive text in the Beacon Widget.
- Fixed being able to add multiple Etherwarp Conduits to the Aspect of the End/Void.
- Fixed being able to feed the Staff of the Volcano to Cows and Sheep.
- Fixed being able to use Frozen Blaze Armor despite not meeting its requirements.
- Fixed Conjuring being able to activate Mage Class Abilities even if you weren't a Mage.
- Fixed Experience Bars visually being replaced by the Killer's Experience Bar due to Loot Sharing.
- Fixed Dungeon Power Stones not getting deleted despite being in your Personal Deletor.
- Fixed Gemstones dropped from Treasure Chests not contributing towards Commissions in the Crystal Hollows.
- Fixed Gunpowder Collection level up messages navigating you to the Sulphur Collection menu when clicked.
- Fixed Jacob's dialogue mentioning Finnegan's GOATed perk despite it not being active.
- Fixed Lonesome Miner not working at the edge of the Crystal Hollows.
- Fixed Melons/Mushrooms being counted inconsistently between their Collections/Crop Milestone counters.
- Fixed Mycelium turning into Dirt when Sulphur Ore landed on it, rendering them unbreakable.
- Fixed players getting kicked from viewing a museum when going too high.
- Fixed Power Scrolls applying stat increases despite an ability not actually activating.
- Fixed some Garden Plots having the incorrect Preset shown when creating a new Custom Preset.
- Fixed some signs showing up in the Crystal Hollows.
- Fixed sometimes being able to catch 2 Golden Fish from a single Golden Fish spawn.
- Fixed Terror Armor's Tiered Bonus working on Star Sentries.
- Fixed the Spider Whisperer perk on the Spider Pet not mentioning that it works on Cave Spiders.
- Fixed the Spirit Mask not saving the player from dying to Dungeon Traps.
- Fixed X, Y, and Z not going into Nether Sacks.
- Fixed Zealot Bruisers sometimes spawning outside of the Zealot Bruiser hideout.
- Fixed Zombie Commanders being able to kill Blazes in the Higher or Lower puzzle.
#### ➥ Typo Corner
- Fixed a consistency issue for the Bubba and Crumbly Magical Powers.
- Fixed a grammatical issue in the Blaze Minion item lore.
- Fixed a grammatical issue in the Refined Mind Mining Fiesta Perk item lore.
- Fixed a grammatical issue in the Tactician's Sword item lore.
- Fixed a grammatical issue when toggling Plot Holograms.
- Fixed a miscolored character in the Glacite Autopet rule description.
- Fixed a miscolored character in the seasonal cosmetics reminder message.
- Fixed several grammatical issues in the Inferno Demonlord menu.
## ► Future Plans
Below is a list of some of the content we're currently working on and would like to share with you. Please note that, as always, this is not an exhaustive list, and some things discussed here might not ultimately make it into the game. We should also point out that we are also working on other secret things that are not on this list, as always.
### ➥ Chocolate Factory
We're pleased with the progress of the Chocolate Factory and have a few small updates on the way. These will address current issues and include new features like seasonal automatic egg collection, guaranteed methods to acquire the rarest rabbits, and more!
### ➥ Museum Progression Revamp
An upcoming update will overhaul the Museum's reward system. Instead of gaining SkyBlock XP based on the total number of items displayed, you'll now earn XP based on each item's individual value. For example, late-game items will provide more XP. While the total XP might be slightly reduced at launch, this change allows for greater XP growth in the long term. Plus, expect enhanced Museum rewards alongside this update.
### ➥ Pesthunter's Wares
Now in the early stages of development, the Pesthunter's Wares is a minor Garden update that will add some more farming content to the game, mostly pest-related.
### ➥ Foraging
As mentioned in our recent Dev Blog, we've made excellent progress on Foraging, with the new Hunting skill nearing completion. While we're not yet ready to implement features from the latest Minecraft version, the Foraging update is nearly polished. We're just waiting for the right tech to catch up.

# Changelog/2024/September 19
Categories: 2024 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[September 19] Mining Changes
Updated: September 19, 2024
Hello!
Over the past couple of days we've been rolling some balance changes and bug fixes for the 0.20.6 update.
EDIT: We've reverted the halved Gemstone Powder amounts from Treasure Chests and pushed a few more bug fixes. EDIT 2: We're going to revert the bug fix to Fuel being consumed on Mining Spread blocks.
Balance Changes
- Halved the weights of Gemstone Powder in Treasure Chests
  - Designer's Note: Gemstone Powder rates were out of control following the update, with some players getting upwards of 5m Gemstone Powder per hour. While we do want to buff the rates a little, this was far far more than we were expecting.
- Halved the amount of Gemstone Powder dropped from Treasure Chests
- Nerfed the Mining Spread Armadillo gives on Hard Stone to 300
  - Designer's Note: This change is also related to Gemstone Powder rates being too high, but we were also uncomfortable with the sheer amount of max Mining Spread you could get on Hard Stone.
- Removed the Gemstone Gauntlet's "Gemstone Snap" ability
  - Designer's Note: We had performance issues with the reworked Gemstone Gauntlet (mainly due to the fact it had so many abilities/too much item lore) so we had to pick an ability to remove. We chose Gemstone Snap as many players find it annoying to refill their Gauntlet Meter every hour.
- Added a 2s cooldown to the Gemstone Gauntlet's "Reduced to Atoms" ability
  - Designer's Note: We looked at many options for how to limit the sheer amount of money this ability could make, and ultimately we landed on adding a simple cooldown. We still want the Gemstone Gauntlet to be your go-to item for fighting mobs on Mining Islands, but it was simply too good left unchecked.
- Refined Mineral drop chance has been reworked as follows (originally 1/350):
  - The drop rate of Refined Mineral now scales with breaking power, with the formula of Breaking Power/2000
- Glossy Gemstone drop chance has been reworked as follows (originally 1/725):
  - The drop rate of Glossy Gemstone now scales with breaking power, with the formula of Breaking Power/2000
    - Breaking Power 6 (Ruby): 6/2000
    - Breaking Power 7 (Amber, Sapphire, Jade, Amethyst, Opal): 7/2000
    - Breaking Power 8 (Topaz): 8/2000
    - Breaking Power 9 (Jasper, Peridot, Onyx, Aquamarine, Citrine): 9/2000
Bug Fixes
- Fixed Dark Auction accessory line not being compatible with Brynmor's shop
- Fixed the new forge slots not showing their progress bars
- Fixed being able to mine the Fossil Research Center's roof
- Fixed items being sent to stashes not taking into account Mining Fortune or Smelting Touch
- Fixed Mithril Golem pet showing lower powder rates for other powders
- Fixed Melon Fortune not working on Melon Blocks
- Fixed Corpse Looter commission being stuck on 2/2 corpses
- Fixed Miner's Blessing perk not working in the Crystal Hollows
- Fixed Subterranean Fisher perk not working in many areas of the Crystal Hollows and Glacite Tunnels
- Fixed Gwendolyn's Garden offer allowing players to enter the Crystal Hollows without meeting the requirement
New Bug Fixes
- Fixed Night Vision talisman/effects not working outside dynamic lobbies
- Fixed Mithril Golem and Scatha Pet not applying to powder gained from garden Emissary Visitors
- Fixed Drill Fuel not taking into account Mining Spread
That's all for today! We are continuing to monitor mining rates and will continue to make changes if they are deemed necessary.

# Changelog/2025/August 20
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[August 20] Primordial Broodfather Changes + Dungeonbreaker
Updated: August 20, 2025
Hello!
Today on the Alpha Network we've got an official implementation of "stonking" along with some Primordial Broodfather follow up changes from SkyBlock 0.23.2.
## ► Stonking (but real)
### ➥ What Is Stonking?
"Stonking" is a term used to describe the act of breaking blocks as a shortcut to access areas or locations in Dungeons. Stonking heavily reduces the amount of time required to complete rooms in The Catacombs. While there is a learning curve for secret paths in rooms, gathering secrets becomes much easier and faster once you’ve memorized them all. As such, it’s an expectation for most players at higher levels to do some degree of stonking.
### ➥ New Item: Dungeonbreaker
The Dungeonbreaker is an item that allows you to temporarily break blocks within a dungeon. Blocks will automatically respawn after 10 seconds. You can have up to 30 blocks broken at once, and can break more once the blocks you’ve previously broken have respawned. This is indicated by the charges on the item.
Dungeonbreakers can be purchased for 500 Wither Essence once the player has completed Catacombs Floor III. The item is intended to be both useable on Minecraft 1.8.9 and the latest versions of the game.
With the addition of this item we have patched the original method for stonking. You will no longer be able to break any blocks with tools not named Dungeonbreaker.
### ➥ Design Comments
What is the purpose of the Dungeonbreaker?
Our philosophy with the Dungeonbreaker is basically to let you do what you could have done before with previous stonking behavior. If there’s parts that don’t meet such expectation, please raise it in the comments below.
Why make stonking an official mechanic?
There’s quite a few players who are reluctant to stonk as they see it as cheating. Stonking was most definitely not an intended feature, and not exactly a healthy mechanic from a design perspective. It invalidates the whole level design when you can take unintended shortcuts. Stonking not being an official mechanic means you’d have to rely on someone (another player, YouTuber, etc) to teach you. It’s cool if it’s only the top 0.1% of speedrunners need to be doing it, but not so much when all players are expected to do an obscure mechanic.
With all that being said, Stonking is here to stay. For one, the window for patching stonking has long passed, it should have been curbed much, much earlier if we deemed it an issue. Another reason is that players have also grown accustomed to the speed at which they can complete Dungeon runs with stonking, patching it would make it all seem more tedious. Our last reason is that the original stonking mechanic required players to be on Minecraft 1.8, since Mojang recently made a change in newer versions that would prevent client-side block breaking. So by making it an official mechanic across all versions you no longer have to switch back to 1.8 to play Dungeons if you don't want to.
## ► Primordial Broodfather Changes
### ➥ Boss Changes
- Enabled the Spider Slayer RNG Meter
- Enabled Ananke Feather usage on the Spider Slayer RNG Meter
- Added Spider Catalyst to the Tier II loot table (125 weight)
- Increased the weight of the Shriveled Wasp from 10 → 20
- Adjusted Tarantula Catalysts to now be able to drop upon reaching Spider Slayer 4 (was 5)
- Adjusted the Tarantula Broodfather/Primordial Broodfather to now deal damage in a consistent AOE every 1s
### ➥ Item Changes
- Adjusted Spider Sword, Recluse Fang, Tarantula Fang, and Sting's damage bonuses on Spiders to be multiplicative
- Adjusted the Shriveled Bracelet's ability to activate on all Ж Arthropod mobs instead of only in the Spider's Den
- Adjusted the recipe of the Mosquito Shortbow:
  - Replaced 128x Enchanted Emerald with 2x Tarantula Catalyst
  - Replaced 64x Enchanted Gold with 1x Digested Mosquito
- Adjusted the Recluse Fang:
  - Reduced Damage from 120 → 60
  - Reduced Crit Damage from 20% → 10%
  - Reduced item rarity from Rare → Uncommon
  - Reduced Combat Wisdom bonus from 30 → 0
  - Reduced craft/usage requirement from Spider Slayer 3 → 2
- Adjusted the Tarantula Fang:
  - Adjusted texture from an Iron Sword → Stone Sword
  - Increased Strength from 40 → 45
  - Reduced Damage from 130 → 90
  - Reduced Crit damage from 30% → 20%
  - Reduced rarity from Epic → Rare
  - Reduced Combat Wisdom bonus from 45 → 15
  - Reduced craft/usage requirement from Spider Slayer 6 → 4
- Adjusted the Scorpion Foil:
  - Adjusted recipe to now require 32x Tarantula Silk, 8x Tarantula Catalyst, and 1x Tarantula Fang
  - Adjusted Dungeonization/upgrade requirements to be the same as Sting's weapon line
  - Added new passive - Deal +250% damage to Spiders and gain +30 Combat Wisdom against Spiders
  - Added "Webcutter" ability
  - Removed "Heartstopper" ability
  - Increased Damage from 80 → 120
  - Increased Crit Damage from 0 → 30%
  - Reduced Strength from 65 → 60
  - Reduced Vitality from 5 → 0
- Adjusted Sting:
  - Increased Strength from 60 → 75
  - Increased passive damage to Spiders from +250% → +300%
### ➥ Wood Singularity Changes
- Now applied to Axes instead of Wooden Swords
- Grants +25 Foraging Fortune, can only be applied once
- Talk to Master Tactician Fink in The Park for refunds if you had any Wood Singularities applied to Swords
If you encounter any bugs while on the Alpha Network please report them HERE.
For any suggestions, please use the Alpha Network Subforum located HERE.
That's all for today!

# Changelog/2025/August 28
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[August 28] Dungeonbreaker Adjustments
Updated: August 28, 2025
Hello!
NOTICE, 8/28 3:30PM ET: We are closing testing for now for the Dungeonbreaker. We are aware that the testing session was short and we apologize for it, however, we have gathered decent feedback for now and will be using Alpha for a different update very soon. We will be circling back to the Dungeonbreaker once (and if!) we make a few changes in the near future. Please note that the stonking changes and the Dungeonbreaker are not planned for this upcoming release. Thank you all for testing it.
We're back on Alpha today to test some adjustments to our Stonking implementation.
After reviewing community feedback, here are the planned implemented changes:
- Stonking is now “clientside”.
  - It’s basically vanilla mining and should result in no latency while mining. Please test this out for yourselves!
- Dungeonbreaker changes
  - The Dungeonbreaker now has 20 charges. Charges now regenerate at 2/s.
  - You are not limited to only breaking 20 blocks, just be aware that upon breaking the 21st block, the 1st block broken will regenerate.
Here’s some other feedback we’d like to address:
- We have considered an upgrade line to the Dungeonbreaker multiple times. We initially decided against it as we thought an upgrade line wasn’t necessary when our goal was to seamlessly integrate stonking into latest versions. However, we are aware that there’s definitely progression potential within the Dungeonbreaker upgrade line, and are open to having upgrades to the Dungeonbreaker.
- To make stonking a bit more purposeful and interactive with secrets, we’re considering the following upgrade:
  - New Wither Essence Shop Perk: Echoes of the Lost
    - Gain 1-5 charges on your Dungeonbreaker after collecting a secret.
    - Costs 250/500/1000/2000/5000 Wither Essence to upgrade.
  - Let us know what you think about it.
- We are aware that players want to have ghost blocks in F7. With the clientside stonking fix, there shouldn’t be much time loss if you were to just break the route again. Let us know if you run into any other issues relating to this.
- We are aware that players may want to be able to break blocks in puzzle rooms. While we will not be granting this, we can offer the following compromises:
  - Let the Tic-Tac-Toe secret not be stonking-reliant.
  - Fix boulder puzzle not being able to be reset by an Architect’s First Draft despite being effectively bricked.
- We are aware that new stonking prevents players from pre-entering the dungeon, which may result in slower runs. We currently do not plan on allowing this functionality because we believe it’s not a fair game mechanic to access the dungeon before the game starts.

# Changelog/2025/August 8
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[August 28] 0.23.4 - Release Candidate
Updated: August 8, 2025
Hello!
Today we are opening the Alpha Network with the release candidate for the SkyBlock 0.23.4 update. This release will not include the Dungeonbreaker Pickaxe.
### ► Primordial Broodfather Changes
(from a previous Alpha test)
- Enabled the Spider Slayer RNG Meter
- Enabled Ananke Feather usage on the Spider Slayer RNG Meter
- Added Spider Catalyst to the Tier II loot table (125 weight)
- Increased the weight of the Shriveled Wasp from 10 → 20
- Adjusted Tarantula Catalysts to now be able to drop upon reaching Spider Slayer 4 (was 5)
- Adjusted the Tarantula Broodfather/Primordial Broodfather to now deal damage in a consistent AOE every 1s
- Adjusted Spider Sword, Recluse Fang, Tarantula Fang, and Sting's damage bonuses on Spiders to be multiplicative
- Adjusted the Shriveled Bracelet's ability to activate on all Ж Arthropod mobs instead of only in the Spider's Den
- Adjusted the recipe of the Mosquito Shortbow:
  - Replaced 128x Enchanted Emerald with 2x Tarantula Catalyst
  - Replaced 64x Enchanted Gold with 1x Digested Mosquito
- Adjusted the Recluse Fang:
  - Reduced Damage from 120 → 60
  - Reduced Crit Damage from 20% → 10%
  - Reduced item rarity from Rare → Uncommon
  - Reduced Combat Wisdom bonus from 30 → 0
  - Reduced craft/usage requirement from Spider Slayer 3 → 2
- Adjusted the Tarantula Fang:
  - Adjusted texture from an Iron Sword → Stone Sword
  - Increased Strength from 40 → 45
  - Reduced Damage from 130 → 90
  - Reduced Crit damage from 30% → 20%
  - Reduced rarity from Epic → Rare
  - Reduced Combat Wisdom bonus from 45 → 15
  - Reduced craft/usage requirement from Spider Slayer 6 → 4
- Adjusted the Scorpion Foil:
  - Adjusted recipe to now require 32x Tarantula Silk, 8x Tarantula Catalyst, and 1x Tarantula Fang
  - Adjusted Dungeonization/upgrade requirements to be the same as Sting's weapon line
  - Added new passive - Deal +250% damage to Spiders and gain +30 Combat Wisdom against Spiders
  - Added "Webcutter" ability
  - Removed "Heartstopper" ability
  - Increased Damage from 80 → 120
  - Increased Crit Damage from 0 → 30%
  - Reduced Strength from 65 → 60
  - Reduced Vitality from 5 → 0
- Adjusted Sting:
  - Increased Strength from 60 → 75
  - Increased passive damage to Spiders from +250% → +300%
- Adjusted Wood Singularity:
  - Now applied to Axes instead of Wooden Swords
  - Grants +25 Foraging Fortune, can only be applied once
  - Talk to Master Tactician Fink in The Park for refunds if you had any Wood Singularities applied to Swords
### ► Chapters Rewrite
The Chapters system currently only found within Hina has been rewritten so that we are capable of adding Chapters for other islands in the near future.
- Added a /chapters command
  - You can use /chapters moonglade to quickly open Hina's Chapters
- Island Chapters can now also be accessed through the Quest Menu
- Minor improvements to the Chapters menu and item descriptions
### ► Abiphone Improvements
We've made several changes to Abiphones in preparation of a new Abiphone Keynote event. Stay tuned for that event in the near future!
### ➥ Abiphones & Co.
Say hello to Abiphones & Co! This new building in The Hub will let early game players purchase an Abiphone Basic, a new budget model of Abiphone with 6 contact slots. Alda (the employee who works here) will also teach you how to add NPC contacts.
### ➥ /call
Players with the Cookie Buff and an Abiphone in their inventory have access to the new /call command. Need to reforge something? Use /call blacksmith! Checking what Dyes are in rotation this year? Use /call vincent! The command works for all NPCs that currently have a contact, so long as you have them added.
### ➥ Other Abiphone Changes
- Adjusted the various Abiphone Shop menus to condense the various available models into submenus.
- Adjusted the build of Udel's Abiphone Shop in the Crimson Isle.
- Adjusted the names of several Abiphone models for clarity.
- Rescaled the features and pricing of existing Abiphone models.
- Removed the ability to trade-in Abiphones as a result of Abiphone prices having been lowered.
- Removed the upcharges/discounts in Abiphone prices from Nuvian and Udel respectively
### ➥ Stat Menu Improvements
- Sorted all SkyBlock Stats into new categories within the SkyBlock Menu.
  - Categories include Combat Stats, Mining Stats, Farming Stats, Foraging Stats, Fishing Stats, Hunting Stats, and Misc. Stats.
- Adjusted the layout of the /stats menu.
- Adjusted various items and perks to use the new stat classification system:
  - Superior Dragon Armor: Only buffs Combat Stats and Miscellaneous Stats
  - Blaze Pet: Only buffs Combat Stats, Fishing Stats, and Miscellaneous Stats on the Crimson Isle
  - Ender Dragon Pet: Only buffs Combat Stats and Miscellaneous Stats
  - Mithril Golem Pet: Only buffs Combat Stats on Mining Islands
  - Dragon Horn: Only buffs Combat Stats and Miscellaneous Stats
  - Legion Enchantment: Only buffs Combat Stats and Miscellaneous Stats
  - Bobbin' Time Enchantment: Only buffs Fishing Stats and Magic Find
    - As a result of it now increasing Treasure Chance and Double Hook Chance, Bobbin' Time's values have been reduced from 0.48%/0.64%/0.8% to 0.3%/0.4%/0.5% per bobber.
  - Mayor Jerry: Only buffs Combat Stats and Miscellaneous Stats
- Base stats on items are now colored based on which category they fall into.
- When you unlock a new stat in SkyBlock a chat message will be sent about it explaining what it does.
- Simplified the item description for the stats menu button to make it less overwhelming.
- True Defense now shows Damage Reduction % in the stat menu similar to regular Defense
### ➥ Taylor's Shop Improvements
- Added the command /warp taylor, which teleports you to Taylor's Shop (available by default).
  - This warp along with /warp elizabeth can now be found in the warp menu.
- A chat message now gets sent in Hubs when Taylor releases a new seasonal bundle.
- You can now click on Fire Sale Dyes in Vincent's menu to teleport to Elizabeth.
- Global Sales of unlimited Fire Sale Dyes are now shown in Vincent's menu.
- Modernized the item description of the SkyBlock Gems button.
- Other minor text and coloring fixes.
### ➥ Axe Runes
There's a new cosmetic type in this update: Axe Runes! This new type of Rune leaves particles behind it when throwing your Axe. For now there are only two Axe Runes available: Fading White and Fading Green. These new Runes drop from Fig and Mangrove Tree Gifts respectively.
### ➥ Reclaim Menu
We're introducing a new menu for reclaiming updated items with old data. Our aim is to add all future reclaims to this menu to reduce the amount of places in the game where items can be reclaimed. You can access this menu using the command /reclaim. Items with available reclaims will have a notice in their item description as well.
In addition to this menu, these six items are no longer dungeonizable and can now be reclaimed:
- The Primordial
- Halberd of the Shredded
- Reaper Scythe
- Reaper Sword
- Revenant Sword
- Undead Sword
You will be able to reclaim the applied master stars and the dungeonization cost from the applicable items.
### ➥ Miscellaneous
- Various buttons in the SkyBlock Menu now show what command you can run to quickly access them
- Added a 'Claim All' button to The Forge
- Uncapped the amount of Suspicious Scrap you can get in a Glacite Minehsaft
  - Note: Each Suspicious Scrap you drop still halves the drop rate for you to get the next
- Crafted Minions button now shows how many unique crafted Minions are in the game
- Shards now list their Hunting requirement
- Moved certain Backwater Bayou mob drops into the Backwater Bayou section of the Bazaar and out of the mob drops section
- Dark Goggles, Shadow Goggles, and Wither Goggles now upgrade with Wither Essence instead of Undead Essence
- Added +5 Mining Fortune to each piece of Glacite Armor
- Adjusted the Junk Sinker to only function while in the Backwater Bayou
- Added Enchanted Charcoal and Match-Sticks to Nether Sack
- Capped Chomp Bonus from the Kuudra Mandible to -20% Health
- Enchanted Hoppers now use Budget Hoppers in their crafting recipe
- /warp forge now makes players face towards Fred the Foreman
- Updated the names of various vanilla items in SkyBlock so that they match the names of their latest version counterparts (i.e. Clownfish → Tropical Fish)
- Simplified the HOTM item description
- Increased the rarity of Enchanted Golden Carrots, Baked Potatoes, and Blaze Rods to RARE (from UNCOMMON)
- Increased the rarity of Hyper Cleavers to LEGENDARY (from EPIC)
- If Mayor Nancy is somehow in office she now has a special skin
- Renamed the Mini Fish Bowl (Carnival Reward) to 'Aquarium Diorama' to minimize confusion with the accessory line
- Renamed Anomalous Desire HOTM perk to 'Tunnel Vision'
- Renamed 'Spooky Hook' in Carnival Shop to 'Spooky Savant' to minimize confusion with actual Hooks
- Added an easter egg to Odexar's dialogue if you meet certain conditions
### ► Bug Fixes
- Fixed Enderman Pet's 'Enderian' perk not working
- Fixed Kelp/Tall Grass removing Diver Armor's 'Deep Sea Diver' bonus
- Fixed Melody's Hair not being purchasable at Rusty despite having 90% completion
- Fixed Personal Harp not being purchasable from Melody despite having 90% completion
- Fixed Bane of Arthropods VII still being obtainable in the Experimentation Table
- Fixed Fig Armor having inconsistent requirements
- Fixed all Benefactor Bonus time from Starborn Temples being double what it should be
- Fixed Ricochet HOTF perk targeting trees that can't be broken
- Fixed Fig Hew and Figstone Splitter now having mirrored recipes
- Fixed Liz's dialogue saying Diaz isn't in office even when she is
- Fixed the Lapis Creeper Fusion not requiring a Legendary Shard
- Fixed Voidgloom Seraph bosses being able to spawn/get stuck in blocks
- Fixed Spider Artifact having an inconsistent unlock and usage requirement (they are both now Spider Slayer 6)
- Fixed Premium Flesh not using the Undead mob type
- Fixed Lava Flames not having the Infernal mob type despite being Blazes
- Fixed Cleave being able to kill Baby Pandas D:
- Fixed being able to apply Recombobulators to Reforge Stones
- Fixed Galatea missing its tier from the warp menu
- Fixed Bal's Health being displayed incorrectly in the Bestiary
- Fixed Luslilac Collection recipes missing from the Recipe Book
- Fixed Beach Balls from overriding Shard drops while using Wooden Bait
- Fixed an entire stack of Boosters being consumed when applying them in an Anvil
- Fixed certain mob types not listing what enchantment boosted your damage towards it
- Fixed Huntaxes allowing you to bypass item requirements on the weapon you put in it
- Fixed various minor typos and coloring issues
Please report any bugs or unexpected behavior that you fine HERE!
That's all for today!

# Changelog/2025/December 12
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[December 12] Garden Alpha Change
Updated: December 12, 2025
Hello,
We have updated the Alpha Network with the following changes:
- Reduced costs of Composter Upgrades
- Changed cost of the Rose Dragon pet, now also requiring Condensed Helianthus and various Mutations
- Reduced drops for base crops in the Greenhouse
- Added npc sell prices to various items
- Greenhouses will now paste with the dirt automatically tilled
  - This also means that unlocking slots with Ethereal Vines will automatically till the dirt.
- Fixed an issue where Dustgrain wouldn't mutate onto a dirt block in the Greenhouse tutorial
- Fixed Cocoa Beans not being breakable with Replenish
- Fixed Cultivating not increasing on Wild Rose and Sunflower
- Adjusted growth cycle speeds in the Greenhouse:
  - The base time per growth cycle: 3 -> 4 hours
  - Garden Levels are a quarter as effective in reducing time per growth cycle
  - The unique crop bonus is half as effective in reducing time per growth cycle
Design Note: The previous values were overtuned, not giving players enough time to step away from the game for an extended period of time without crops dying of thirst. Upgrades should still feel signifcant and max around 1h50m~ per cycle, which should give people plenty of time to take a breather.
- Mutations no longer count towards the unique bonus
- Wild Rose and Sunflower outside of the Greenhouse now grows half as fast
- Moved Sam closer to the Greenhouse watering source when doing the Greenhouse tutorial
- Fixed decorative plants detecting as custom crops when pasting, leading to unusual builds.
- Fixed Wild Rose and Sunflower crops not spawning Pests
- Increased Warty drop chance through the Wart Eater attribute: 0.01-0.05% → 0.02-0.1%
  - Warty is now counted as a rare crop and is affected by Rarefinder and Rose Dragon pet
- Made a text change to Helianthus and Condensed Helianthus
- Fixed Crop Bug attribute to also work on Helianthus
- Fixed various typos
- Fixed Wild Rose, Sunflower and Moonflower not giving Farming XP
- Fixed certain pest drops not being affected by Farming Fortune
- Rebalanced Farming XP, Tool XP and Sowdust gain when harvesting in the Greenhouse
- Removed duplicate items from the Pesthunters shop
- Added Tool Exp Capsule to the Hex
- Fixed a soft lock in Jeff's quest
- Fixed Hypercharge Chip working on more Farming Fortune sources than intended
- Fixed Evergreen Chip not working properly
- Added recipes for the Eclipse and Wild Rose hoe
- Added a more descriptive message when trying to place a Greenhouse crop on the wrong soil type
- Fixed being able to use the InfiniDirt Wand inside the Greenhouse
- Added mutation-specific information to the Diagnostics Tool
- Fixed All-in Aloe hologram and growth stage not lining up
- Modified the Farming Armors to also have chances to drop Cropie/Squash/Fermento/Helianthus for crops harvested within the Greenhouse
- Added total growth stages tooltip to crops and mutations in the Greenhouse through the Carpenter
- Changed the Hedgehog Pets perk from Nocturnal to Hunter's Insight which gives Farming Fortune based on your Bestiary Pest Tier
- Added Fermento and Helianthus to the Agronomy Sack
- Reduced NPC sell price of Helianthus from 300k to 275k and Condensed Helianthus to 2,4m
- Modified Mutations Sack to hide Mutations that haven't been discovered yet
- Fixed Chloronite behaviour when harvested with Silk Touch
- Fixed guests not being able to right-click to view players profiles on the Garden
- Reworked the Sundial to set a player's Speed Cap based on the last crop broken instead of the plot they're standing in. Once a player has given the Sundial to Sam, the menu is accessible through the Desk
- Added a command to set the crop-specific speed cap, /setcropspeed <speedCap> <crop>
- The Ultimate Harvester Helmet Swappable skin now has 9 skins for each of the 3 new collections. This is available retroactively to anyone that has the skin applied.
Additional non-garden related changes:
- Added Sulphur to Mining Sacks
- Formatted leaderboard values with commas in Museum entries
- Fixed being able to pick up arrows shot from traps in Crystal Hollows & Dungeons
- Fixed being able to claim the Fallen Star Tracker from Silnar multiple times
  - You can now purchase the tracker from Rusty
- Fixed HOTM/HOTF perks with reset timers resetting at 12am UTC instead of EST
- Fixed Training Weights and a couple other items having the Brewing Ingredient label

# Changelog/2025/December 5
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[December 5] Garden Greenhouse Update Alpha
Updated: December 5, 2025
Hello everyone!
Today we are opening alpha with the Garden Greenhouse Update!
This update includes revamped Farming Tools, adds a powder-like currency called Sowdust to upgrade Chips, 3 new crops, new visitors, and a lot more.
Here is an overview of each to help guide you while testing. As always, if you do not meet any requirements or require some items speak to Centauri in the Hub.
Spoiler: EDIT: Composter Upgrades
We are aware that a lot of you have requested nerfs to Composter Upgrades, we will look into it. However, we would like everyone to get more familiar with this alpha first as it already provides a huge boost in Copper gains through mutations before making the final decision.
## ► New Plot: Greenhouse
Spoiler: Greenhouse
The Greenhouse is unlocked at Garden Level 7, and can be accessed after completing the Carpenter's Offer when he appears as a Visitor. After a short quest, you should be able to place a Greenhouse in place of a Garden Plot.
After a further introduction about the basics of the Greenhouse, you'll be able to view additional information through the Carpenter, which will stay in your Greenhouse if you need any more information.
To unlock being able to analyze Mutations, you'll have to complete Beth's Questline first, which also has a new part added.
## ► Revamped Farming Tools
Spoiler: Farming Tools
Most of the Farming Tools have been revamped, they have been standardized in terms of how much Fortune and upgrades they give and we also added a new levelling system that improves tools while farming. Using these tools now will also drop Sowdust, which is used to upgrade Chips.
All of these should be retroactive, your tools should automatically be converted to the new version with an appropriate level depending on your counters or your cultivating enchantment. Sowdust will also be given back based on your previous Crop Milestones at a 50% rate.
## ► New Chip System
Spoiler: Chips
Jeff is a new NPC in the Garden that will introduce you to Specialized Farming Tools and Chips.
Chips can be unlocked from many different sources in the game and upgraded with Sowdust. You can further upgrade Chips by consuming duplicates to increase their rarity and therefore their max level and scaling.
## ► New Crops
Spoiler: New Crops
We added 3 new crops, Sunflower, Moonflower and Wild Rose. Each have their respective Collection, Farming Tools, Enchants, Crop Upgrades and Milestones.
You can first unlock them by spreading them in the Greenhouse, then farm them like any other crop.
## ► New Visitors
Spoiler: New Visitors
We have added 40+ new Visitors, among them is a shady businessman that will sell you a special dragon pet! We also added Mayors, which will only visit you while they are elected and provide you with a unique accessory.
## ► Crop & Visitor Milestone Nerfs
Spoiler: Crop & Visitor Milestone Nerfs
As promised, we have adjusted Milestones for Crops and Visitors by a major amount:
To also account for the previous Crop milestone nerfs, we also want to adjust Visitor milestones accordingly:
|#|New|Old|
|-|-|-|
[1]
|2|5|5|
|3|10|10|
|4|20|20|
|5|30|30|
|6|50|50|
|7|75|75|
|8|100|150|
|9|150|250|

# Changelog/2025/February 6
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[Feb 6] Year of the Seal & Shiny Pigs
Updated: February 6, 2025
Hello everyone!
Today we're releasing an update containing the following to alpha.hypixel.net:
- Year of the Seal
- Return of the Shiny Pig Event in the form of the Year of the Pig
### Year of the Seal​
This new event lasts an entire SkyBlock Year and follows a 12-year rotation. In the future, we are aiming to have a yearly event for each year within the 12 year rotation, but for now, only the Year of the Seal and the Year of the Pig are available. These two events will alternate every six years until more yearly events are added.
This is how this new event works:
- During the Year of the Seal, you have a chance to fish up Beach Balls or Giant Beach Balls from Treasure.
- Use the Beach Balls to play a minigame by yourself that involves bouncing it on your head.
- Use the Giant Beach Ball to play the same minigame but everyone around you can participate as well.
- Based on your performance, you receive Fishy Treats which you can spend at Lukas near the Hub Blacksmith for various items.
### Year of the Pig​
We are returning the Shiny Pig Event in the form of the Year of the Pig within the new yearly rotation system. We also made a couple small changes:
- Moved the Pig Shop near the Hub Blacksmith
- Talking to Piggy gives a brief introduction to the event
- Added an Event Info within the Pig Shop menu
In other news, here's a list of general changes and bug fixes that will be coming out with this update.
#### General Changes​
- Added a new Rift objective for obtaining Detective Amog's wallet.
- Added better error message when trying to collect a Relic after collecting all of them.
- Adjusted the Time Chamber modifier descriptions to be more accurate and consistent.
- Changed the Spiky achievement to unlock when applying Thorns 3 to an armor piece.
- Rabbits now give Combat Exp like other animals.
- Limited the Squeaky Mousemat's pitch and yaw settings to their actual minimum and maximums.
- Updated Carrolyns initial dialogue to say mysterious instead of spooky.
#### Crystal Hollows Changes​
Jungle Temple
- Significantly shortened parkour and removed some lava and traps.
- Moved respawn location closer to temple.
- Added more gemstones.
- Improved navigation through the room significantly by raising the river canyon.
- Prevented people breaking into the temple by mining through it.
- Added ways to escape the lava.
Lost Precursor City
- Significantly improved navigation throughout the city.
- Added more Automaton spawns.
Mines of Divan
- Improved navigation to aid travel between treasure points.
- Removed difficult to access treasure locations.
- Widened pathing around the Dwarven statues and crystal.
### ► Bug Fixes​
#### Rift Fixes​
- Fixed being able to use Hemoglass in the Wizardman Brawl.
- Fixed Temporal Pillars being able to spawn as Runic.
- Fixed Super Sharp Steak Stake giving the wrong amount of Combat Exp boost.
- Fixed the Hazard Zone in the Sun Gecko fight showing the wrong damage dealt.
- Fixed the time shown when Ubik's Cube has one second or less left on cooldown.
- Fixed Wizard's Assistant having purple inner arms.
- Fixed Wizardman forgetting that he gave you fragments in some cases.
- Fixed some dialogue inconsistencies.
#### Item Fixes​
- Fixed Biohazard Armor not linking to the wiki page in the guide.
- Fixed Bottles of Jyrre and Dark Cacao Truffles not getting upgraded if you leave the room immediately after completing the Dungeon Quiz.
- Fixed Extremely Real Shurikens working on The Garden and when guesting on private islands.
- Fixed Flamebreaker Armor not counting towards Madam Eleanor I's quest.
- Fixed Medium Cyan Backpack showing an incorrect texture.
#### Combat Fixes​
- Fixed the Golden Dragon's perk "Gold's Power" not applying to Blaze Slayer Daggers in Auric mode.
#### Farming Fixes​
- Fixed message that only farming tools can break crops when only swords are disallowed.
- Fixed Ravenous Rhino and Baker not showing up when Blooming Business is active.
#### Mining Fixes​
- Fixed an issue that allowed players to keep the same Sky Mall Perk for multiple days.
- Fixed gap behind wall in Royal Mines that caused Golden and Diamond Goblins to spawn outside the map.
- Fixed Maniac Miner perk capping bonus at 990 instead of 1000.
- Fixed Quartz Blocks in fossil structures not instantly breaking.
#### Misc. Fixes​
- Fixed /savethejerrys attempting to move you while Jerry's Workshop is closed.
- Fixed Aranya not giving Chymes if speaking to her immediately after feeding her.
- Fixed Bingo profiles being able to access the Museum.
- Fixed double drops being possible for Speckled Teacup when fishing with Blessing Enchantment.
- Fixed not being able to add enrichments to campfire badges.
- Fixed the "Shrimp!?!" Achievement not being awarded after obtaining Shrimp The Fish from an Ender Node.
- Fixed the dialogue options from Lone Adventurer not working correctly.
- Fixed the More Trading Options menu icons sometimes not being centered.
- Fixed the Viking Longhouse area not playing the Park music.
As usual, you should report any bugs at https://hypixel.net/bug-reports/create, making sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
That's it!

# Changelog/2025/January 15
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[Jan 15th] Experimentation Table RNG Meter, Abandoned Quarry, and More!
Updated: January 15, 2025
Spoiler: Day 1 Changes
None of the following changes are currently on Alpha at the time of writing, but they are planned. If they do not make release, they can be expected to follow soon:
- Added Aquamarine Dye as a drop from the Mithril Grubber at 1/5M odds.
- Disabled Ragnarock Axe being used in The Garden
- Reduced the Pest Cooldown Reduction of Finnegan's "Pest Eradicator" perk from 25% -> 20%
- Increased the Pest Cooldown of each piece of Pesthunter's Equipment from 5% -> 10%
- Increased the rarity of each piece of Pesthunter's Equipment from UNCOMMON -> RARE
- Added a Tiered Set Bonus to the Pesthunter's Equipment granting 20/30/40 FF on Pests at 2/3/4 pieces.
- Added a Pest Spawn Cooldown indicator tablist widget
Spoiler: Day 2 Changes
Hope you're all having a very blocky weekend. Upcoming changes listed below.
- Removed Smite 6, Bane of Arthropods 6 from "Rare Books" loot pool in Experimentation Table.
- Added Scavenger 5, Thunderbolt 6 to "Rare Books" loot pool in Experimentation Table.
- Added Luck 7, Titan Killer 7, Critical 7, and Thunderbolt 7 to "Ultra-rare Books" loot pool in Experimentation Table.
- Adjusted Pesthunter's Equipment tiered bonus from 20/30/40 FF on Pests to 50/75/100 FF on Pests.
- Fixed an issue causing Livid Dye's required Meter XP to be way lower than it should be.
- Adjusted the "Spiky" achievement -> now triggers by applying Thorns III enchantment to armor.
- Buffed Pure Quartz base yield to be in line with other pure ores.
- Adjusted the Experimentation Table itemlore to note how many of your bits refreshes you've used today.
- Increased Mithril Grubber Bestiary to Bracket 4, Tier 15 (1000 to max)
Hey all!
Today we're releasing an update containing the following to alpha.hypixel.net:
- Experimentation Table RNG Meter
- The Abandoned Quarry
- Small Pest Patch
Since this is a pretty varied update, we've separated each of the above into their own spoiler.
Experimentation Table RNG Meter
Features:
- An Experimentation Table RNG Meter has been added to the game.
  - Players have been given 10%/1000 of their Enchanting XP as retroactive progress.
- This Meter contains all "Rare" and "Ultra-rare" books, as well as physical items, such as the Metaphysical Serum.
- Adjusted all "Rare" and "Ultra-rare" books to now have 5 weight in their respective loot tables.
- Removed "Basic" and "Advanced" books from the Experimentation Table and replaced them with varying amounts of Enchanting XP.
  - To level this out, the Enchanting XP given upon table completion has been reduced.
- Removed Bane of Arthropods VI and Critical VI from the "Rare" book loot pool.
- Removed Thunderbolt VI, Vampirism VI, and Lethality VI from the "Ultra-rare" book loot pool.
- Added endcap enchantment items, such as the Golden Bounty and A Beginner's Guide to Pesthunting, to the "Ultra-rare" book pool.
- Increased Thunderlord VI to Thunderlord VII in the "Ultra-rare" book loot pool.
- Removed Luck VI, Looting IV, and Scavenger IV from "Rare" book loot pool. They're now purchasable at Einary.
- Added the following dyes to their respective RNG Meters:
  - Jade Dye (Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter)
  - Matcha Dye (Zombie Slayer RNG Meter)
  - Brick Red Dye (Spider Slayer RNG Meter)
  - Celeste Dye (Wolf Slayer RNG Meter)
  - Byzantium Dye (Enderman Slayer RNG Meter)
  - Flame Dye (Blaze Slayer RNG Meter)
  - Sangria Dye (Vampire Slayer RNG Meter)
  - Livid Dye (Master Mode Floor 5 RNG Meter)
  - Necron Dye (Master Mode Floor 7 RNG Meter)
QoL:
- Added a /meter alias to the /rng command.
- "Your RNG Meter was reset" message now only plays AFTER obtaining your selected drop. No more ruined surprises!
- Added some hologram text above the Experimentation Table to make it stand out more.
- Added a search bar to all RNG Meters.
- Added overflow charges to the Experimentation Table.
- Changed the reset button on all RNG Meters from a Sign to an Exclamation Mark player head.
The Abandoned Quarry
Features:
- Added a new location to the Dwarven Railroad System - the Abandoned Quarry.
- Added a new Pure Ore, which can be found in the Abandoned Quarry - Dwarven Quartz.
- Added a new early-game Sea Creature, the Mithril Grubber.
  - Can be fished up starting from Fishing Level 6.
  - When killed, drops varying amounts of Mithril Powder, depending upon whether the Grubber was small, medium, large, or bloated.
- Added an Abiphone contact for Brynmor
  - This can be obtained by bringing him the Speckled Teacup - an item fished up as a treasure in the Abandoned Quarry.
- Added a new NPC, Bomin, to the Dwarven Tavern. Go talk to him, he's a vibrant guy.
- Added a new NPC, Guard Gornum, to the Abandoned Quarry.
Small Pest Patch
Features:
- Added the Pesthunter's Cloak, Pesthunter's Necklace, and Pesthunter's Belt to the Pesthunter's Wares.
- Added NPC sell prices to the following items:
  - Wriggling Larva: 250k
  - Squeaky Mousemat: 100k -> 1m
- Added Squeaky Mousemat to the Museum
QoL / Balancing:
- Added more bulk trading options to /ltrades.
- Added a "Release All" button to Pest Traps.
- Added EPIC and LEGENDARY Slug Pet that can drop from the Slug pest to the same drop pool.
- Updated the itemlore of Sprayonator materials to make their function clearer.
- Improved Pest Trap hologram text.
- Made the message sent when Pest Traps catch a Pest also activate when the player's online.
- Swords can no longer break crops in The Garden.
- The type of Pest that spawns in a Pest Trap is now influenced by the current disc you have playing in your vacuum.
- Adjusted Strawberry Dye's drop chance from Cactus, Cane from 1/300M -> 1/150M on account of the dye now only rolling once.
- Buffed Dung Dye's Drop chance from Field Mouse to 1/50k
- Removed the "Vacuums don't work on these creatures!" when vacuuming near a Pest Trap.
- The Field Mouse now gives 1.5x the crop drops of other pests.
- Made Pest Trap messages clickable.
- Made Pest Spawn Cooldown additive instead of multiplicative, and shifted around some numbers. The optimal Pest CDR setup now looks like:
  - Pesthunter's Necklace: 5% CDR
  - Pest Vest: 15% CDR
  - Pesthunter's Gloves: 5% CDR
  - Pesthunter's Belt: 5% CDR
  - Squeaky Reforge: 10% CDR (2.5x4)
  - Finnegan's "Pest Eradicator" perk: 25% CDR
    - Total: 65% Pest Cooldown Reduction.
Other Changes & Bug Fixes
Changes:
- Added a new secret achievement to the Rift.
- Restored the player's health after fighting Sun Gecko.
- Added skill experience for mining and farming resources in the Rift.
- Fixed Chrono pickaxes not working on Living Metal ore, despite claiming to.
- Fixed breaking Living Metal ores with the wrong tool spawning unmineable blocks around it.
- Added a combo counter in the action bar for Puffs in The Rift.
- Reduced the loot share from Puffs from 100% to 10%.
- Fixed Trophy Fish Chance not working as intended.
  - This fixes the issue where you can't catch Trophy Fish without any Trophy Fish Chance.
- Added a new item (Tera Shell Necklace) to prevent Trophy Fish from being caught while wearing this item.
- The lava immunity effect of Ember Armor has been added to Magma Lord Armor.
- Radioactive Vial's drop chance has been increased from 0.3% to 0.5%.
- Reworked Habanero Tactics to better synergize with slayers.
- Fixed bow shots increasing stacks on Crimson Armor.
- Magic Damage now increases stacks on Hollow Armor.
- Reduced the particles of various mobs in the Crimson Isle.
- Fixed being able to increase stacks on Crimson Armor by hitting another player.
Bug Fixes:
- Fixed an issue causing the Sprayonator to unintentionally reduce Pest spawn cooldowns.
- Fixed an issue causing Finnegan's Pest Eradicator perk unintentionally reduce Pest cooldowns.
- Fixed some typing errors within Pest Traps.
As usual, you should report any bugs at https://hypixel.net/bug-reports/create, making sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
We look forward to hearing your feedback!

# Changelog/2025/July 17
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[July 17] 0.23.2 Release Candidate
Updated: July 17, 2025
Hello!
Today we are opening the Alpha Network complete with the 0.23.2 Release Candidate. Have a quick read of what's going on before hopping onto alpha.hypixel.net to test!
## ► The Primordial Broodfather
The Broodfather is back on alpha, and we've taken the time to fix some critical bugs and implement some key design changes. Most notably, The Primordial now has its new ability - give it a try!
### ➥ Stats and Abilities
Spoiler: T1 Broodfather Stats & Abilities
- Backstab : Occasionally leaps behind you. Attacks from behind deal +50% Damage.
Spoiler: T2 Broodfather Stats & Abilities
- Backstab : Occasionally leaps behind you. Attacks from behind deal +75% Damage.
- Noxious Paralysis : Reduces your healing by 25% and inflicts a stacking -2.5% debuff to your Speed Cap every 5th time you get damaged.
Spoiler: T3 Broodfather Stats & Abilities
- Backstab : Occasionally leaps behind you. Attacks from behind deal +100% Damage.
- Noxious Paralysis : Reduces your healing by 50% and inflicts a stacking -5% debuff to your Speed Cap every 5th time you get damaged.
- Web of Lies : Spews webbing along the ground, disabling teleportation abilities until they are cleared. Also spawns 2 Eggs Sacs, break them before they hatch! (at 66% and 33% Health)
Spoiler: T4 Broodfather Stats & Abilities
- Backstab : Occasionally leaps behind you. Attacks from behind deal +125% Damage.
- Noxious Paralysis : Reduces your healing by 75% and inflicts a stacking -7.5% debuff to your Speed Cap every 5th time you get damaged.
- Web of Lies : Spews webbing along the ground, disabling teleportation abilities until they are cleared. Also spawns 2 Eggs Sacs, break them before they hatch! (at 66% and 33% Health)
- Bat Barrage : Becomes immune to magic damage for the rest of the fight and releases a horde of Bats. Take 1% of Max Health as Damage per second for each Bat alive. (starting at 50% Health)
Spoiler: T5 Broodfather Stats & Abilities (NEW!)
10,000,000 HP
3,500 DPS
- Backstab : Occasionally leaps behind you. Attacks from behind deal +150% Damage.
- Noxious Paralysis : Reduces your healing by 100% and inflicts a stacking -10% debuff to your Speed Cap every 5th time you get damaged.
- Web of Lies : Spews webbing along the ground, disabling teleportation abilities until they are cleared. Also spawns 3 Eggs Sacs, break them before they hatch! (at 66% and 33% Health)
- Bat Barrage : Becomes immune to magic damage for the rest of the fight and releases a horde of Bats. Take 2% of Max Health as Damage per second for each Bat alive. (starting at 66% Health)
- Til Death Do Us Part : Upon initial "death", calls out to the Primordial Broodmother, who will awaken and fuse with the Broodfather, creating the Primordial Brood. The Brood has x2 Health and Damage of the Primordial Broodfather but only uses passive abilities.
### ➥ New Items
Please note that we are aware just how much Spider Slayer RNG Meter XP some players have stockpiled over the years, and while we will not be removing any Meter XP from players, we may not allow for new chase items to be redeemed in the meter for a little while after the update drops in order to prevent the market being flooded on day 1.
Spoiler: Spider Slayer 6 Items
- Tarantula Fang
  - Upgraded version of the newly revamped Recluse Fang (see 'Other Changes' section)
  - Can be crafted at Spider Slayer 6
- Vial of Venom/Festering Maggot
  - Can be consumed up to 5 times, each time granting a permanent +1 Crit Damage/+1 Defense
  - Rarely dropped from T4 and T5 Spider/Zombie Slayer bosses upon reaching Spider/Zombie Slayer 6 respecitvely
Spoiler: Spider Slayer 7 Items
- Primordial Armor
  - Upgraded version of the newly revamped Tarantula Armor with higher base stats, buffed abilities, and Gemstone Slots.
- Mosquito Pet
  - Perks include buffing Pest Vinyls, Sugar Cane Fortune, and reducing the Pest Trap cooldown by up to 20%. A maxed-out legendary mosquito currently gives +175 Sugar Cane Fortune.
- Shriveled Bracelet
  - A Bracelet which grants Magic Find and Combat Wisdom while in the Spider's Den.
- Ensnared Snail
  - Can be applied to a weapon with Bane of Arthropods VI to increase it to level VII.
  - Replaces Bane VII books in the game.
- Tarantula Ring
  - Ability: Every 10th melee hit on the same enemy now deals +15% Damage.
Spoiler: Spider Slayer 8 Items
- The Primordial
  - A new LEGENDARY Belt that gives the player a 10% chance to cocoon Slayer Bosses and Slayer Minibosses upon killing them. Cocooned enemies will quickly respawn and can be killed again.
    - Crafted from an item exclusive to the Tier V boss. Has a 0.014% drop chance.
- Sting
  - Upgraded version of the Tarantula Fang
  - This weapon always crits!
  - Crafted using several of an item exclusive to the Tier V boss. Has a 0.071% drop chance.
Spoiler: Spider Slayer 9 Item
- T12 Tarantula Minion
  - No surprises here, it's a faster version of the T11 Tarantula Minion
  - Can be purchased from the Bartender upon reaching Spider Slayer 9
### ➥ Changes Since Last Alpha
- Added the Darkness Within rune
- Allowed the egg sacks to be damaged by arrows
- Allowed The Primordial to be starred and converted to a dungeon item
- Reworked The Primordial to grant a 10% change to cocoon a Slayer boss or Slayer Miniboss
  - In the event that a Slayer Boss is cocooned, it will start a new slayer for you, but skip to the boss fight
- Altered some of the drop tables weights
- Altered the Vial of Venom to grant +1 Crit Damage per consumption
- Altered the Festering Maggot to grant +1 Defense per consumption
- Altered the recipe for The Primordial to be closer aligned with its new role
- Altered the amount of Toxic Arrow Poison given by each tier
- Altered the Shriveled Necklace
  - Buffed the stats to +5 Combat Wisdom and +10 Magic Find when in the Spider's Den
  - Changed to a glove item type, now called the Shriveled Bracelet
- Granted immunity from magic damage to the Primordial Bats
- Raised the cap of the Buzzin' Bartender Mosquito pet perk to +175 Sugar Cane Fortune
- Fixed a bug with teleportation abilities being disabled for the whole server
- Fixed a bug with The Primordial spamming "You cannot use this item here"
- Fixed a bug with the Spider Egg Mixin granting +15 Crit Damage rather than +15% Crit Damage
- Fixed a bug with Noxious Paralysis not persisting for the duration of the fight
- Fixed a bug with teleportation abilities being disabled for longer than intended
- Fixed a bug with being able to corrupt the Primordial Hatchlings/Minions using a Totem of Corruption
- Fixed a bug with the new Spider Slayer recipes not showing in the rewards preview
- Fixed a bug with the new Spider Slayer recipes not showing in the maddox recipes menu
Please note that any further changes mentioned on the July 8th Alpha Thread that have not yet been implemented are still planned for a later date.
## ► Kuudra Swapping Shenanigans
- Added support for swapping your Kuudra Armor Type.
  - The menu can be accessed with the /kuudratransfer command, or at the faction blacksmiths Alwin and Igor at the Crimson Isle.
## ► Damage Reduction Fixes
When it came to damage reduction, some items were working in strange and mysterious ways. The most notable issue is being able to stack 100% damage resistance to get full damage immunity. Fear no more, for they have been corrected!
- Changed how damage reduction was calculated in Pets, resulting in lower reduction than stated
- End Stone Sword’s ability now gains damage reduction every 100 mana consumed instead of 5 and is also now capped at 20% multiplicative damage reduction
- Fixed an issue which allowed all variations of the Sea Creature Accessories to stack their damage reduction together
- Changed the following items from an additive damage reduction to a multiplicative reduction instead:
  - Monster Hunter’s Set Bonus
  - Magma Lord Armor and Magma Lord Gauntlet
  - Taurus Helmet, Flaming Chestplate, Moogma Leggings, Slug Boots
  - Thunder Armor and Thunderbolt Necklace
  - Wither Impact and Wither Shield
  - Soulweaver Gloves
  - Pumpkin Armor
  - Ender Relic/Artifact
  - Nether Artifact
  - Zombie Artifact/Ring/Talisman
  - Spider Artifact/Ring/Talisman
  - Wolf Ring/Talisman
  - Fire Talisman
  - Lava Talisman
  - Wither Relic/Artifact
## ► Bugfixes
- Fixed an issue preventing the placing of deployables near the Soul Campfire.
- Fixed an issue causing Cinderbats to die to lava when attempting to lasso them.
- Fixed an issue causing scaffolding to despawn when sneaking near the edge or jumping on it.
- Fixed the Sea Creature Guide not detecting when you'd killed a Tadgang for the first time.
- Fixed an issue causing Black Holes not pulling in mobs correctly while underwater or when obstructed by transparent blocks such as wheat.
- Fixed an issue allowing players to circumvent Emerald Blade's Curse of Greed by placing it in a Huntaxe.
- Fixed an issue allowing players to fish up Common shards from a GOOD CATCH! while using Wooden Bait in the Tomb Floodway.
- Fixed an issue allowing Pandas to phase through blocks when growing via Bamboo.
- Fixed collecting an auction while nicked shows the nick to your co-op members.
- Fixed some typos and cleared up some descriptions.
- Fixed Crystal Nucleus Guide sometimes not opening correctly.
- Fixed Being able to damage sea creatures while wearing Hunter Armour using Explosive Arrows.
- Fixed non-Zombie kills on Reaper/Revenant Armor counting.
- Fixed Dwarven Mines events color on scoreboard not displaying correctly.
- Fixed not being able to use Etherwarp on carpet.
- Added an "Essence Guide" button to the Forest Essence Shop.
- Added a menu icon to claim coins from inaccessible co-op bank, due to the profile being a solo profile.
- Added Unlock Cost to HOTM and HOTF Ability descriptions.
- Changed requirements to reset HOTM and HOTF to their first perks (Mining Speed for HOTM and Sweep for HOTF).

# Changelog/2025/July 3
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[July 3] Pet Crafting Changes & Bug Fixes
Updated: July 3, 2025
Hello!
Today on the Alpha Network we're testing a rework to Pet Crafting, a new progression system through Hina, along with the usual miscellaneous changes/bug fixes. Keep reading for all of the details!
## ► FYI: Spooky Festival Data Change
We are deploying a change that introduces an expiry date to Spooky Festival rewards. This change is being made in an effort to improve performance. You will now have 10 in-game years to claim your Spooky Festival rewards before they expire.
## ► Hina's Chapters
After getting your Heart of the Forest from Hina, you'll get access to Hina's Chapters by talking to her again. This menu will guide you through progression on Galatea with several tiers of objectives. Every objective will give you rewards so make sure to complete them all!
## ► Pet Crafting Changes
We've completely removed randomized pet crafts from the game. Now, all crafting recipes for pets start at Common rarity. To compensate for this, the crafting recipes for almost all pets have been made dramatically cheaper, along with their Uncommon and Rare upgrades at Kat. Pet recipes are also unlocked a lot earlier in collections, making them a lot more accessible for early game players. In the future we may look into re-evaluating Pet Luck as a stat and what it could do.
We've also added reminder text to the bottom of un-upgraded Pets that you can upgrade them at Kat in the Hub now that it is the only way to get higher rarities of certain Pets.
>> SPREADSHEET LINK WITH CHANGES <<
### ➥ New Pet Rarities
To coincide with this change, we've added Common, Uncommon, and Rare versions of the following Pets:
- Blaze Pet
- Glacite Golem Pet
- Pigman Pet
- Wither Skeleton Pet
### ➥ Revamped Chicken Pet
We've revamped the Chicken Pet for this update to make it more useful for early game players as an alternative to the Rabbit Pet for farming.
### ➥ Rabbit Drop Changes
- Added Enchanted Cooked Rabbit crafted with x160 Enchanted Raw Rabbit
- Enchanted Rabbit Hide is now crafted with only x160 Rabbit Hide (previously it was x576 Rabbit Hide)
- Rabbit Minion recipes now only use Raw Rabbit (previously it also used Rabbit Hide/Rabbit's Foot)
- Fixed an issue where Rabbit Minions were dropping half as much Rabbit Hide/Rabbit's Foot than intended
## ► Other Changes
### ➥ Farming
- Added a line to Pest and Mouse traps that shows the trap limit
- /tptoplot now tries to teleport you to a yaw that's a multiple of 90°
- Farming For Dummies can now be applied to Vacuums
### ➥ Mining
- The Better Together event now takes into account players that are in your zone rather than distance in blocks
- Reduced the collection requirements for Glacite, Tungsten, and Umber
- Added Suspicious Scrap to the Dwarven Sack
- Flowstate now only requires Enchanting XV to use
- Eternal Flame Rings can now be sold to NPCs for 10,000 coins
### ➥ Combat
- Blood God Crests now have a cap of +7 Strength
- Blood God Crests can now be bought more than once
- Increased the drop rate of Rampart Armor from Bezals from 0.2→1%
- Increased the drop rate of Cyclamen Dye from Matcho
### ➥ Fishing
- Reverted fix to being able to catch Obufscated 1 Trophy Fish outside of the Crimson Isle
- Added Diver Fragments to Fishing Sacks
- Adjusted the action speed of Fishing Minions so that it's better distributed across upgrade tiers
- Increased the weight of Bayou Sludge
- Reduced the Rusty Coins required to purchase Backwater Armor and Equipment
- Increased the George Sell Price of the Mythic Hermit Crab Pet to 2,000,000 coins
- Added Enchanted Glowstone Dust as a drop from Fireproof Witches
- Reduced the drop rate of Enchanted Glowstone Blocks from Fireproof Witches (because of the above change)
- Reduced the NPC Sell Price of Fried Feathers to 1,000 coins
- Bobbin' Time now requires Enchanting XX to use (swapped with Flowstate)
### ➥ Miscellaneous
- When Hoppity calls you he now always offers you a Chocolate Rabbit you don't already have
- Added Enchanted Ancient Claws to the Event Sack
- /ahsearch now tries to automatically select the Auction House category with the most results
- You can no longer sell Abiphones to NPCs
- Made it more clear that Pets in the Dark Auction are sold at Lvl 1
- Updated the Booster Cookie description on Ironman Profiles
## ► Bug Fixes
### ➥ Farming
