# Changelog/2025/July 31
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[July 31] Foraging Changes & Mob Types
Updated: July 31, 2025
Hello!
Today on the Alpha Network we've got an array of improvements to content introduced in the Foraging along with an improved Mob Typing system. Keep reading for all of the details!
Spoiler: Day 1 Changes
The following changes have been rolled to alpha or are pending roll.
- Added /mobtypes alias for /mobtypesmenu.
- Added the "Animal" typing to all Sharks (now aquatic/animal).
- Adjusted "Arcane" enchantment into new "Woodsplitter" enchantment - now deals +10/20/30/40/60% damage to "Woodland" mobs.
  - T1-5 are available through the Enchanting Table, while T6 can drop from Chillblade/Chillshot at a 0.1% chance.
- Adjusted "Impaling" enchantment to now have 5 levels dealing 10/20/30/40/60% more damage to "Aquatic" mobs.
- Adjusted Wither Blades to make their damage bonuses explicitly apply to the "Wither" Mob Type.
- Removed the Catacombs level requirement to purchase Forest Essence from the Bazaar.
- Removed Smite 7 and Ender Slayer 7 from the RNG Meter - they've been replaced with their relevant endcap items.
- Fixed inability to hit Glacite Walkers with Huntaxes
- Fixed Phantom mobs appearing as "Shards" on Tree Gift loot tables
- Fixed Bogged, Wetwing, Headless Horseman, and several Slayer Minibosses not having any Mob Types.
- Fixed an issue preventing Glacial mobs from being damaged by the Gemstone Gauntlet.
- Fixed an issue causing Mob Types to replace Sea Creature rarities in /scg.
- Fixed an issue causing some shards with a recipe to not grant 2 shards when fused.
  - This includes the Ananke, Moltenfish, Apex Dragon, Galaxy Fish, Goldfin and Sun Fish Shards.
- Fixed some typos in Michael's dialogue.
## ► Foraging
### ➥ New Museum Items
We've added many of the items from the Foraging update to the Museum.
Spoiler: New Museum Items
- Abysmal Lasso (+1 XP)
- Abyssal Armor (+10 XP)
- Basic Fishing Net (+1 XP)
- David's Cloak (+2 XP)
- Decent Axe (+2 XP)
- Entangler Lasso (+4 XP)
- Everstretch Lasso (+10 XP)
- Fig Armor (+7 XP)
- Fig Hew (+2 XP)
- Figstone Splitter (+5 XP)
- Hunting Toolkit (+2 XP)
- Large Scaffolding (+4 XP)
- Mangrove Equipment (+8 XP)
- Medium Fishing Net (+2 XP)
- Medium Pocket Black Hole (+4 XP)
- Medium Scaffolding (+2 XP)
- Park Armor (+1 XP)
- Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanium (+15 XP)
- Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus (+4 XP)
- Savage Huntaxe - Praedator (+10 XP)
- Seriously Damaged Axe (+1 XP)
- Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus (+2 XP)
- Small Pocket Black Hole (+2 XP)
- Snorkeling Armor (+3 XP)
- Tiny Scaffolding (+1 XP)
- Tormentor (+20 XP)
- Turbo Fishing Net (+7 XP)
- Vinerip Lasso (+2 XP)
- Worn Huntaxe - Genesis (+1 XP)
### ➥ New Achievements
We've added 135 new AP through some new Foraging-related achievements. We've also renamed 'The Dragon's Egg' to 'The Golden Dragon's Egg' and it now gives 15 AP instead of 10.
Spoiler: New Achievements
- Predator: Achieve Hunting level 5/10/15/20/25 (+5/10/15/20/25 AP)
- The Family: Donated 20 Fishes to Coral (+10 AP)
- Handyman: Tune the beacon on Galatea (+5 AP)
- Gotcha!: Hunt a mob with a Black Hole (+5 AP)
- Shine Bright: Reach Starlyn Contest Level 5 (+10 AP)
- Forgotten Shrine: Reactivate a Starborn Temple (+10 AP)
- Weird Science: Syphon 50 Attribute (+10 AP)
- Evolution: Fuse into 50 Shards (+10 AP)
### ➥ Other Foraging Changes
- Added 3 new hidden brackets to Agatha's Content with even better rewards
- Melody Changes
  - You now only need to get 90% perfection on Melody's Songs for the maximum SkyBlock XP and Intelligence bonus
  - By extension, Melody's Hair now also only requires 90% perfection on all non-Personal Harp songs to earn
  - Melody's Harp now physically appears as damaged before you fix it
- New Galatea Sea Creature: Bogged
  - Drops Mangrove Logs and Sea Lumies
  - Unlocked at Fishing V
  - Sea Archer Shard is now the Bogged Shard and is dropped from hunting Bogged
  - Renamed the Attribute from "Monster Bait" to "Creature Fisher"
- New Epic Attribute: Chop
  - Effect: Grants a 10-100% chance to Chop an extra log, on top of the ones you swept.
  - Obtaining: 0.15% drop from Fig Tree Gifts and a 0.25% drop from Mangrove Tree Gifts
- New Legendary Attribute: Bayou Biter
  - Effect: Grants +0.25-2.5 (NEW!: +0.5-5) Double Hook Chance while in the Backwater Bayou.
  - Obtaining: From hunting a Titanoboa
- Kuudra now gives 1/1/1/1.25/1.5 Kraken Shards for tiers 1-5.
  - Instead, the Daemon fusion now outputs 2 shards instead of 1.
- In Master Mode 7, an Apex or Power Dragon Shard is now guaranteed to appear in Emerald Chests and higher.
  - The shards do not affect what other loot you get from the chests, it's purely a bonus reward.
- Improved some fusion recipes:
  - 2× Sun Fish = 5× Shinyfish + 5× Firey Mob (same as firefly)
  - 2× Cropeetle = 5× Termite + 5× Praying Mantis
  - 2× Wartybug = 5× Dragonfly + 5× Lunar Moth
    - 2× Dragonfly = 5× Firefly + 5× Dragon (unchanged)
      - 2× Firefly = 5× Praying Mantis + 5× “fiery mob” (same as firefly)
        - 2× Praying Mantis = 5× Pest + 5× Combat
    - 2× Lunar Moth = 5× Ladybug + 5× Bug Rare+ (unchanged)
      - 2× Ladybug = 5× Termite + 5× Invisibug
        - 2× Termite = 5× Bug + 5× Mining
- Added Emblems for reaching Agatha Level 5, HOTF 5, and HOTF 7
- HOTF EXP rewards are now converted to Forest Whispers once HOTF is maxed.
- Added Flexbones to the Bazaar
- Doubled the Foraging Fortune given by Citrine Gemstones
- Booster Changes
  - All Boosters can now be sold on the Bazaar
  - All Boosters are now stackable
    - NOTE: Old Boosters created before this update will still not stack
  - Sweep and Foraging Wisdom Boosters can now be salvaged for 5 Forest Essence
  - Sweep and Foraging Wisdom Boosters can now be sold to NPCs for 25,000 Coins
- Foraging Armor Progression Changes
  - Reduced Park Armor's rarity to Common
  - Reduced Canopy Armor's rarity to Uncommon
  - Fig Armor now uses Canopy Armor in its crafting recipes
- Abysmal Lasso Changes
  - Can now also be crafted with x5 Sea Lumies now
  - Added to the recipe to Sea Lumies I
  - Vinegrip Lasso now uses the Abysmal Lasso in its crafting recipe
- Kiara will now give you her Abiphone Contact if you give her a Chameleon Shard
  - Having her contact will also allow you to buy an additional Shard each day
- Only Galatea Sea Creatures can now be fished up in Galatea
  - This does not include Event or Hot Spot Sea Creatures
- Scaffolding Changes
  - Now stays up as long as you are within 5 blocks of it
  - Can now be placed next to blocks, in water, and on non-full blocks
  - Can now be placed in Tangleburg
- Added 4 new Rabbit Residents to Galatea
- Added Emperor's Skulls to the crafting recipe of Abyssal Armor
- Stretching Stick Recipe Changes
  - Fig Hew now requires x8 Stretching Sticks
  - Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus now requires x8 Stretching Sticks
  - Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus now requires x16 Stretching Sticks
  - Savage Huntaxe - Praedator now requires x32 Stretching Sticks
  - Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanium now requires x64 Stretching Sticks
  - Mangrove Vine now requires x64 Stretching Sticks
- Sturdy Bones are now used to craft the Medium and Turbo Fishing Nets
- Fig Tree Gift Changes
  - Stretching Sticks: 20% → 10%
  - Sweep Booster: 1% → 0.5%
  - Foraging Wisdom Booster: 0.5% → 0.3%
  - First Impression I: 0.4% → 0.25%
  - Missile I: 0.2% → 0.15%
- Mangrove Tree Gift Changes
  - Sweep Booster: 1.5% → 1%
  - Foraging Wisdom Booster: 0.8% → 0.5%
  - First Impression I: 0.5% → 0.4%
  - Missile I: 0.25% → 0.2%
- Trevor the Trapper now gives Hunting XP for completing hunts
  - Trackable: +50 Hunting XP
  - Untrackable: +100 Hunting XP
  - Undetected: +250 Hunting XP
  - Endangered: +500 Hunting XP
  - Elusive: +3,000 Hunting XP
- You no longer get Hunting XP for lootsharing
- Snorkeling Shoes now come with Depth Strider III when purchased
Halved the amount of Double Hook Chance given by the Crimson Hook (Lord Jawbus) attribute
  - NEW: This has been reverted.
- Buffed the Hunter's Pressure (Python) attribute, now scales from 5-50%
- The Reborn (Prince) attribute now sends a chat message when it procs
- You can now shift-right click Shards in your Hunting Box to convert all of that Shard into an item
- Axolotl's now "lock on" to the player that alerted them and ignore any other players
  - This change should make their Shards easier to collect without getting accidentally griefed by others
- Phantom Changes
  - They no longer deal any damage
  - When lassoing them they no longer dive super far
  - They now despawn after 60s of not interacting with them
- Salamanders are now fused into by using a Newt Shard + Any Water Shard
- Chameleons now only require 1 Shard to fuse
- Getting multiple Shards via Hunting Fortune now doubles/triples/etc the points you get for Agatha's Contests
- Buffed the amount of Foraging XP and SkyBlock XP earned from Swoop's Milestones
- Shulker bullets now ignore block collisions for the first few seconds after being fired, they also home in slightly now
- Stealth I Books are now x10 as rare from Chillblades/Chillshots
- The Hina's Chapter task that required you to get 100 Shards from Lushlilac Bonbons now only requires 25 Shards
- Added /warp murk and /warp loch as aliases of /warp murkwater
- All Shards now actually have the word "Shard" in their name, rather than just being the name of the mob
  - e.g. "Seagull" → "Seagull Shard"
- You are now able to click on the Attribute Fusion and Transfer chat messages to quickly open your Hunting Box
- Sorting filters are no longer retained when reopening the Attribute Menu to reduce confusion
- Ryan can now detect logs obtained from the Personal Compactor and Bazaar for the Campfire questline
- Added a "Right-click to view recipes!" to the Runeblade Talisman and Ring
- You can no longer place Hunting Toolkit transformed items into the Reforge Anvil
- Tree Gift chat messages are now dark green instead of blue
- Removed the '+' from the description of the Cookie Eater (Bitbug) attribute as it was misleading.
### ➥ Foraging Bug Fixes
- Fixed David's Cloak upgrading its rarity at the wrong Attribute Milestone levels
- Fixed the Hellwisp (Matriarch Cubs) Attribute not working
- Fixed your Trap Hunts Milestones incorrectly just showing you Salt Hunts Milestone
- Fixed an issue with Joydives despawning in certain situations when they shouldn't be
- Fixed the "Max out a Foraging Attribute" task in Hina's Chapters not unlocking for some players
- Fixed the Fish the Fish task in Hina's Chapters not completing under certain circumstances
- Fixed Fig and Mangrove Log collections not counting towards the Raider Sword's bonus
- Fixed Ocelot Pet's Tree Essence perk not working on Fig or Mangrove trees
- Fixed being unable to buy the Early Bird HOTF perk when you had Tree Whisperer perk but not the 250 Gifts perk
- Fixed Chameleon Shards not being Syphon-able, not giving XP, and falsely displaying as maxed in the Hunting Box
- Fixed Chameleon Shards saying they required Hunting Level XX when they actually don't
- Fixed the Rain Slime Mana Steal attribute description not being accurate to what it actually does
- Fixed the Woodcutting Crystal recipe not using Enchanted Spruce Logs despite being in the Spruce Log Collection
- Fixed Molbert's quest not sending a chat message when players have collected enough Jungle Logs
- Fixed Yogs being able to bypass the damage cap on Huntaxes
- Fixed Carrot Kings acting weird around Black Holes
- Fixed being able to use a Signal Enhancer even after the Beacon is maxed out
- Fixed Loch Emperor Shards being called Sea Emperor Shards
- Fixed Hideonleaves not using their names in death messages
- Fixed a text inconsistency with Molbert's quest
- Fixed a couple of instances that mentioned needing to be on 1.21.4 to join instead of 1.21.5
- Fixed some weird coloring in the chat message that you get when sending a Shard to your Hunting Box
- Fixed two missing exclamation points in the Beacon tuning menu
- Fixed an issue with the dialogue of the Jade Dragon
- Fixed a minor text issue when obtaining a Bambuleaf Shard
- Fixed a minor grammatical issue with one of Hina's tasks
- Fixed a minor grammatical issue with Molbert's trap
- Fixed Shell Attribute not granting knockback immunity against certain mobs
## ► Mob Types
Currently, it is quite difficult for a player to be able to immediately tell which mob-specific enchantments, such as Smite, Ender Slayer, etc., grant bonuses against each mob. For example, is an Endermite an ender mob, or an arthropod, or does it take bonus damage from both enchants? Opinions on this will change depending upon who you ask, since it changes with later versions of Minecraft. Similarly, it's not currently super intuitive when figuring out which mobs/sea creatures you come across in Galatea can only be damaged by axes. Thus, a simple system for players to reference was deemed necessary.
Mob Types is a system that aims to clear up this confusion for new players and allow for greater customization when creating mobs in the future. Players can now figure out which type a mob is by either looking at the symbol(s) next to its name, checking their Bestiary, or by talking to our new friend Ike in the Spider's Den.
### ➥ List of Mob Types
Spoiler: All Mob Types
|Mob Type|Special Behavior|
|-|-|
|༕ Undead|Takes increased damage from the Smite enchantment.|
|Skeletal|N/A|
|⊙ Ender|Takes increased damage from the Ender Slayer enchantment.|
|Ж Arthropod|Takes increased damage from the Bane of Arthropods enchantment.|
|✰ Humanoid|N/A|
|♨ Infernal|Takes increased damage from the Smoldering enchantment.|
|⚂ Cubic|Takes increased damage from the Cubism enchantment.|
|❆ Frozen|N/A|
|☽ Spooky|N/A|
|✿ Mythological|N/A|
|☠ Wither|N/A|
|⸕ Subterranean|N/A|
|Aquatic|Takes increased damage from the Impaling enchantment.|
|ൠ Pest|Can only be damaged with a vacuum.|
|☮ Animal|N/A|
|♆ Magmatic|N/A|
|♣ Elusive|N/A|
|⚙︎ Construct|N/A|
|♃ Arcane|N/A|
|⛨ Shielded|Takes 1HP per hit (e.g Golden Goblin, Worm).|
|✈ Airborne|Takes increased damage from the Gravity enchantment.|
|❆ Glacial|Can only be damaged with a pickaxe/drill.|
|⸙ Woodland|Can only be damaged with an axe.|
### ➥ The Mob Types Crib
As part of this update, a new house has been added to the Spider's Den that aims to onboard new players in the art of mob types:
- Michael will introduce players to the concept of mob types
- Ike lists all of the mob types and will reward players for the first time they slay each of them
### ➥ Items & Enchantment Changes
As part of this update, several items have been changed to adhere to the new Mob Types system.
Spoiler: Item & Enchantment Changes
- Adjusted End Sword to now deal bonus damage to ⊙ Ender mobs.
- Adjusted Undead Bow to now deal bonus damage to ༕ Undead mobs.
- Adjusted Undead Sword, Revenant Falchion, Reaper Falchion, Halberd of the Shredded to now deal bonus damage to all ༕ Undead mobs and removed the damage reduction from them.
- Adjusted Spider Hat, Spider Sword, Scorpion Foil to now deal bonus damage to all Ж Arthropod mobs.
  - Coming soon to Recluse Fang, Tarantula Fang, and Sting.
- Adjusted Reaper Armor's "Trolling the Reaper" bonus to apply to all ༕ Undead mobs.
- Adjusted Smoldering enchantment to now apply to all ♨ Infernal mobs.
- Adjusted Smite enchantment to now apply to all ༕ Undead mobs.
- Adjusted Impaling enchantment to now apply to all Aquatic mobs.
- Adjusted Cubism enchantment to now apply to all ⚂ Cubic mobs.
- Renamed Dragon Hunter enchantment to Gravity & adjusted its bonus to now apply to all ✈ Airborne mobs.
- Replaced all instances of Ender Slayer VII dropping with new "Endstone Idol" endcap item.
- Replaced all instances of Smite VII dropping with new "Severed Hand" endcap item.
### ➥ Other Mob Type Changes
- Improved many references to specific types of mob throughout the game (e.g Pest -> ൠ Pest)
- Updated the Bestiary to now include each mob's relevant typing beneath its name
## ► Miscellaneous
### ➥ Changes
- All Fortune stats are now disabled on Private Islands
- Golden Dragon's Symbiosis perk and Hound's Scavenger perk no longer work on Private Islands
- Diamond the Fish are now Soulbound and are sold in Odger's Lost Rewards Shop for 10m Coins
- Stew the Fish is now x6 more common from drinking Suspicious Stew
- Flake the Fish is now an OUTSTANDING Treasure catch from fishing in Jerry's Workshop
  - It was previously not tied in to the Treasure Fishing system at all, this should make it easier to obtain
- Renamed Thunder Shards to Thunder Fragments
- You can no longer fish in water on the Crimson Isle
- Hid vanilla item attributes for icons in the Stats breakdown menu
### ➥ Bug Fixes
- Fixed an issue with Jacob's Contest brackets not scaling properly when Finnegan's GOATed perk was active
- Fixed teleport abilities not resetting fall damage, leading to taking more damage than expected when landing
- Fixed a misplaced ladder in the Sand Castle Barn Skin (requires pasting the skin again)
- Fixed the Jerry Visitor for the Garden tutorial not showing up
- Fixed players not receiving Experimentation Table rewards if their game ends from the server restarting
- Fixed Vincent refusing to show which dyes have increased chances for the year
That's all for today!

# Changelog/2025/June 10
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[June 10] Foraging Alpha - Foraging & Kuudra Changes
Updated: June 10, 2025
Hello!
We're making a variety of changes to content in the Foraging update to smooth out progression and alleviate some of your concerns regarding Kuudra. Some of these changes have already rolled to alpha, and some are pending roll. This thread is documenting all of those changes.
### ➥ Collection Changes
We've standardized the collection requirements and rewards across all of the vanilla log collections. The new collections can be seen below. Notably the Jungle Axe (renamed to Spruce Axe) has been moved to the Spruce Log II and the Treecapitator has been moved to the Jungle Log VII.
Oak Log Collection
- Oak Log I [50]: Oak Minion Recipe
- Oak Log II [100]: Leaflet Armor Recipe, Oak Leaves Trade
- Oak Log III [250]: Enchanted Oak Log Recipe
- Oak Log IV [500]: Small Storage Recipe
- Oak Log V [1,000]: Forest Biome Stick Recipe
- Oak Log VI [2,000]: Medium Storage Recipe
- Oak Log VII [5,000]: Wood Affinity Talisman Recipe
- Oak Log VIII [10,000]: +10,000 Foraging XP
- Oak Log IX [25,000]: Large Storage Recipe
Birch Log Collection
- Birch Log I [50]: Birch Minion Recipe
- Birch Log II [100]: Birch Leaves Trade
- Birch Log III [250]: Enchanted Birch Log Recipe, Portal to Birch Park Recipe
- Birch Log IV [500]: Sculptor's Axe Recipe
- Birch Log V [1,000]: Birch Forest Biome Stick Recipe
- Birch Log VI [2,000]: Small Foraging Sack Recipe
- Birch Log VII [5,000]: Medium Foraging Sack Recipe
- Birch Log VIII [10,000]: Large Foraging Sack Recipe
- Birch Log IX [25,000]: Large Enchanted Foraging Sack Recipe
Spruce Log Collection
- Spruce Log I [50]: Spruce Minion Recipe
- Spruce Log II [100]: Spruce Axe Recipe, Spruce Leaves Trade
- Spruce Log III [250]: Enchanted Spruce Log Recipe, Portal to Spruce Woods Recipe
- Spruce Log IV [500]: +500 Foraging XP
- Spruce Log V [1,000]: Taiga Biome Stick Recipe
- Spruce Log VI [2,000]: +2,000 Foraging XP
- Spruce Log VII [5,000]: Woodcutting Crystal Recipe
- Spruce Log VIII [10,000]: Wolf Pet Recipe
- Spruce Log IX [25,000]: +25,000 Foraging XP
Dark Oak Log Collection
- Dark Oak Log I [50]: Dark Oak Minion Recipe
- Dark Oak Log II [100]: Dark Oak Leaves Trade
- Dark Oak Log III [250]: Enchanted Dark Oak Log Recipe, Portal to Dark Thicket Recipe
- Dark Oak Log IV [500]: Roofed Forest Island Recipe
- Dark Oak Log V [1,000]: Roofed Forest Biome Stick Recipe
- Dark Oak Log VI [2,000]: +2,000 Foraging XP
- Dark Oak Log VII [5,000]: -25% Growth Exp Discount
- Dark Oak Log VIII [10,000]: +10,000 Foraging XP
- Dark Oak Log IX [25,000]: Growth Armor Recipes
Acacia Log Collection
- Acacia Log I [50]: Acacia Minion Recipe
- Acacia Log II [100]: Acacia Leaves Trade
- Acacia Log III [250]: Enchanted Acacia Log Recipe, Portal to Savanna Woodland Recipe
- Acacia Log IV [500]: Savanna Bow Recipe
- Acacia Log V [1,000]: Savanna Biome Stick Recipe
- Acacia Log VI [2,000]: +2,000 Foraging XP
- Acacia Log VII [5,000]: Repelling Candle Recipe
- Acacia Log VIII [10,000]: +10,000 Foraging XP
- Acacia Log IX [25,000]: Acacia Birdhouse Recipe
Jungle Log Collection
- Jungle Log I [50]: Jungle Minion Recipe
- Jungle Log II [100]: Jungle Leaves Trade
- Jungle Log III [250]: Enchanted Jungle Log Recipe, Portal to Jungle Island Recipe
- Jungle Log IV [500]: Vines Trade
- Jungle Log V [1,000]: Jungle Biome Stick Recipe
- Jungle Log VI [2,000]: +2,000 Foraging XP
- Jungle Log VII [5,000]: Treecapitator Recipe
- Jungle Log VIII [10,000]: +10,000 Foraging XP
- Jungle Log IX [25,000]: Ocelot Pet Recipe
### ➥ New Armor Set
We've added a new armor set earned through The Park. Each piece of the set is earned through completing a specific NPC's quest. Each piece gives a bit of Foraging Fortune on top of Health and Defense.
- Charlie → Charlie's Trousers
- Kelly → Kelly's T-Shirt
- Melody → Melody's Shoes
- Molekin → Molekin's Hat
### ► Kuudra
NEW KUUDRA LOOT TABLE: CLICK HERE
Here's some important things to note:
- The Kraken (Vitality) shards are no longer in the Kuudra loot table. Instead, Kraken shards are now guaranteed from every Kuudra run, at the following rates:
  - T1/T2: 1 Shard
  - T3/T4: 2 Shards
  - T5: 3 Shards
- The Tormentor and Hellstorm Wand are new additions to the Kuudra loot table.
  - Tormentor replaces the Enrager within Kuudra's loot table.
  - Its drop rate is significantly higher than what Enrager's once was, but still should be rare enough to be exciting when it drops.
  - It has an Infernal Kuudra Clear requirement and a Combat Requirement of Lv 42.
- There's a bunch of Kuudra-exclusive "demon" family shards that have very powerful effects. You can fuse into them with your Kraken shards, or directly get them as a drop from Kuudra!
- Travel Scroll to Kuudra's Hollow has been added to the Kuudra loot pool.
- Kuudra Mandible now has a salvage value of 500 Crimson Essence.
- The Crimson Essence from Kuudra's Free chest has been bumped up to 10/25/50/100/200.
In addition, we are planning on doing the following changes:
- Rework the Inferno enchant to increase your damage while in Kuudra's Lair and the Crimson Isles.
- Add an upgrade to Molten Equipment that increases your stats while in Kuudra's Lair and the Crimson Isles.
### ➥ Infernal Kuudra Armor Essence Costs
A set of 15✪ Infernal Armor used to cost ~3,700 runs worth of Crimson Essence, which is far more runs than reasonably required. The total Crimson Essence cost required is being reduced by approximately 60%.
You can view the detailed changes HERE.
The increased Crimson Essence gains and the reduction in upgrade costs drops the number of runs required for a set of Infernal Kuudra Armor to ~1,100.
### ➥ FYI: Ananke Feathers & Attribute Refunds
As promised we are refunding players for their "god rolls" which were essentially the RNG drops of Kuudra. We are compensating for these RNG drops with Ananke Feathers, a new RNG drop with approximately the same market value. Here's how the system works:
Ananke Feathers
- Ananke Feathers are a new item that grants progress toward RNG meters.
- They are designed to hold consistent market value, regardless of the item you claim them from.
- Only the highest-value “god roll” attribute combos receive feather compensation.
- Feather amounts are based on the actual market value of your item, not just the fact that it was a god-roll.
You can check the refund values HERE for each item/attribute combination.
NOTICE: We have removed the restriction of god-rolls not rewarding attributes when obtained after a certain date. They will now award Ananke Feathers just like their older counterparts.
Ananke Feathers in Kuudra
- Ananke Feathers also drop from T5 Kuudra at a 0.35% drop rate.
- This is intentionally low, to limit market disruption while keeping the item desirable.
- If farming Kuudra becomes more efficient than other RNG sources, we’ll adjust the drop rate accordingly.
- The total number of refunded Ananke Feathers is finite as it's a one-time event.
Attribute Shards
- Attribute Shards are refunded based on the tier of each attribute.
- This was previously 1/2/3/4/6/8/16/32/64/128 shards from T1-10 respectively on Alpha.
- We have reduced the conversion rates of Attribute Shards at earlier tiers to better reflect their value.
- Certain items (Rampart Armor, Volcano weapons, fishing sets, Vanquished gear) now refund Attribute Shards at half the normal rate.
As part of this cleanup, we’ve also temporarily disabled attributes on most non-Kuudra gear until the Foraging update drops along with the /attributemenu until the Foraging update releases. You can still grind Kuudra for Attribute Shards in the meantime.
### ➥ Aurora Armor & Mana Attributes
Aurora Armor is now a lot weaker without exclusive attributes. It will be compensated by an increase to the armor's base Intelligence, as follows:
Aurora Helmet
||Intelligence (previous)|Intelligence (new)|
|Basic|190|275|
|Hot|239|347|
|Burning|302|437|
|Fiery|380|550|
|Infernal|479|693|
Non-Helmet Aurora Armor
||Intelligence (previous)|Intelligence (new)|
|Basic|125|175|
|Hot|158|221|
|Burning|198|278|
|Fiery|250|350|
|Infernal|315|441|
We also plan to buff the set ability to grant mana for 10 seconds upon ability activation. Note that this is mana and not intelligence, thus it only increases your mana pool and not your damage. Let us know what you think about that ability. You can see the details of the proposed changes along with a bit more intelligence values HERE.
### ➥ Other Attributes & Balancing
This update removes quite a bit of stats from players. While we do believe that the distribution of certain few stats through attributes was wildly unbalanced, we did not intend for these changes to have such a big impact.
- Vitality
  - Vitality was giving too much healing that would have been rebalanced in the near future.
  - Inferno Demonlord's T4 damage will be reduced by 30% to compensate for the loss of healing.
- Veteran
  - Veteran was also giving far too much Combat Wisdom, especially in respect to other Combat Skill XP buffs.
  - Veteran will be bumped up to 10 Combat Wisdom at max.
  - We will consider giving slayer-specific XP buffs as an alternative to boost the rates of spawning slayer bosses.
- Speed
  - Speed allowed players to attain high amounts of speeds without using speed-specific armors.
  - This Speed was not necessary, and exclusively useful in The Catacombs to avoid having to sacrifice damage stats to get wanted speed.
  - There are many other options to get Speed such as Tuning Points, Enrichments, Pets, and Armor.
  - We will consider giving more options for gaining Speed in the near future.
- Mana Steal
  - Mana Steal has been reduced from 20 per hit to 5 per hit.
  - It's still about a 5% increase to your mana regeneration per second, assuming you regenerate 400 mana/s through passive regeneration, Power Orbs, and the Mana Steal enchant.
- Magic Find
  - We are planning on doing a larger rework to Magic Find sometime soon so this was going to be changed regardless. The plan with that update is to move temporary/conditional sources of Magic Find into permanent ones to get rid of the need to swap around gear, so this Attribute was going to be changed anyways.
  - For now, the Magma Lord Armor passive has been buffed from 5/10 Magic Find to 15/25 Magic Find against Lava Sea Creatures.
### ► Other
- Chill Bestiary has been reduced from 10k to 1k to max.
- Flare Bestiary has been reduced from 100k to 10k to max.
  - This is done in anticipation of the Gauntlet of Contagion's "kill duping" functionality being significantly harder to proc/entirely fixed.
  - Also rolled a small change that may fix the spawning issue with Flares.
- The Frog Pet is now a Foraging Pet instead of a Fishing Pet.
  - Thanks for pointing it out...
- Blaze Slayer damage will be nerfed by 30% across the board for T4, with a slight damage adjustment for T3.
- Rescaled the number of attributes required to upgrade shards at each tier of rarity, as shown HERE.
That's all for today!

# Changelog/2025/June 5
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[June 5th] Foraging Update Alpha
Updated: June 5, 2025
The Foraging Update ALPHA is now live!
The most technically complex update in SkyBlock’s history is now available for public playtesting on the Alpha Network.
Here’s everything you need to know for this testing session:
► What is this?
This is the early test of the Foraging Update, which includes:
- A complete revamp of The Park
- A brand new island: Galatea
- The Hunting Skill, with permanent Attributes
- A massive tech leap: uses latest Minecraft version mechanics
- Dozens of new systems, mobs, tools, UI, and more
► How to join
You will need to be MVP+ or higher to join this testing session!
Log in with Minecraft 1.21.5 (latest version) and connect to:
alpha.hypixel.net
Make sure to switch your client version first, Galatea and the new Park are only accessible on the latest Minecraft version.
► What’s available to test
We’re opening up testing for the following systems:
- New Foraging Islands
- Tree Chopping Mechanics (Sweep, Toughness, Styles)
- The Heart of the Forest
- New Collections
- The full Hunting Skill
- Shards, Attributes, Fusions
- Kuudra loot table changes
- Dozens of new items, mobs, and NPCs
- New underwater Pressure mechanic
- And more
► We need your help
This is a huge update, and your feedback is critical.
Found a bug?
Submit a bug report >HERE<
Have feedback or ideas?
Please voice your opinion, good or bad. Constructive criticism is extremely important. You can either:
- Comment on this thread
- Write a thread in the Alpha section
► A quick note on the content of Foraging and Latest Versions
It's important to mention and temper some expectations. Foraging is an update that comes with a LOT of content, and a few features from latest versions. Some of the stuff you guys may be expecting from latest may not have made it to Foraging right away - the best example of this are resource pack menus. Foraging is a technological marvel, but mostly from the backend. A lot of the work necessary to bring you guys this update won't technically be noticeable from players perspective, but it paves the way for future content, both within and outside of SkyBlock on the entirety of the Hypixel Network. Yes, we are working on fancy resource pack menus and UIs, and we'll be showing them off to you guys soon™.
► Thank you
This update has taken us months years of work, brand-new tech, and deep system reworks.
We’re incredibly proud of how far SkyBlock has come, and we’re even more excited to hear what you think.
This is just the beginning.
See you in the forest.

Hi all!
Today we're releasing an update containing Hypixel SkyBlock's first-ever dedicated fishing island to alpha.hypixel.net.
If you don't want to be spoiled, you can hop ingame right now and head towards the Fisherman's Hut, where you can sail to the Backwater Bayou following the completion of a new early-game quest. If you're fine with spoilers, read on for more!
### Backwater Bayou
The Backwater Bayou is a dedicated fishing island, having content for early and late-game fishers alike, though it's more heavily focused on helping new players gear up for the journey ahead. It requires fishing level 5 to enter. A non-exhaustive list of the content introduced as part of this update can be found below.
- Ship System
  - Talk to Fisherman Gerald by the Fisherman's Hub to get your ship up and running!
- Treasure Revamp
  - Treasure Chance is now an actual stat and treasure loot tables have been remade.
  - There are now 3 tiers of treasure catches:
    - GOOD (89% of catches)
    - GREAT (10% of catches)
    - OUTSTANDING (1% of catches)
- Junk Fishing
  - Talk to Junker Joel in the Backwater Bayou to learn more.
- Rod Parts
  - Talk to Roddy in the Backwater Bayou to learn more.
- Fishing Hotspots
  - Talk to Hattie in the Backwater Bayou to learn more.
- 14 New Sea Creatures
  - 6 exclusive to the Backwater Bayou, 4 exclusive to Water Hotspots, and 4 exclusive to Lava Hotspots.
    - Sea Creature unlock levels range all the way from fishing 5-47!
- Removed Underground Fishing Speed Penalty
Additionally, there is an Alpha Checklist for this update, and completing it will net you an exclusive memento.
If you experience any bugs while testing, please report them at https://hypixel.net/bug-reports/create, making sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
Thanks!

# Changelog/2025/October 10
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[October 10] 0.23.5 Release Candidate (/pity, Ironman W's, QoL)
Updated: October 10, 2025
Hello!
Today on the Alpha Network we are testing the SkyBlock 0.23.5 update. This update introduces a brand new pity system, the Ironman W's update, some high-impact quality-of-life (QoL) changes, and the previously-tested Dungeonbreaker. Keep reading for more information!
Spoiler: Day 1 Changes
These changes are live on the Alpha network at the time of posting.
- Ironman Shen's Auctions now use half the quantity of regular Shen's Auctions rather than only 15%
  - Note that this change will not show up on Alpha as the current auction is ongoing
- Made some changes to the conversion time for converting to a Normal Profile
  - SkyBlock Level 0-24: Instant
  - SkyBlock Level 25-49: 2 Days
  - SkyBlock Level 50-99: 7 Days
  - SkyBlock Level 100+: 35 Days
  - For Co-ops the player with the highest SkyBlock Level is used
- Hoppity's Pity is now retroactive for up to 1 MYTHIC and 1 DIVINE Rabbit
  - You can calculate how much retroactive pity you will receive by doing the following (example is for divines):
    - 2,500 * UniqueDivines = X
    - TotalEggsOpened -X =Y
    - Y is your retroactive pity progress. Keep in mind that it cannot exceed 2,500/2,500
    - For example, a player with 8,800 eggs opened and 3 unique divines would do 2,500*3=7,500. 8,800-7,500=1,300 retroactive pity out of a maximum 2,500.
      - If you've opened 2,500+ eggs with 0 divines, you'll still get 2,500/2,500 towards a divine.
      - You can also use the above formula to calculate your mythic pity, but use 500 instead of 2,500
- Duplicate MYTHIC/DIVINE Chocolate Rabbits no longer reset Hoppity's Pity
- Buying Chocolate Rabbits from Hoppity now counts towards Pity
- Increased the NPC Sell Price cap on all profile types to 500m
- Added the Frozen Scute to Umber + Tungsten corpses.
  - Has 10 weight in Tungsten + Umber and 8 weight in Vanguard. Note that it's more common in vanguards because they contain significantly less weight overall.
- Added Dungeonbreaker to Museum, worth 8 SkyBlock XP
- Pocket Black Holes can no longer pull in mobs that belong to other players
- Now only the owner of a Chumcap Bucket can fish up Agarimoos
  - Nearby players still get the Fishing Speed bonus
- Makes Pickaxe Ability Widget default on some Mining Islands
- Nerfs the health of Spider Slayer's Egg Sacks from 5/10/15 → 3/6/9
- Added 'Click to View!' text under Rusty Category descriptions
- Swapping off of Final Destination Armor while the ability is active to no longer retains the ability's stats
- Made it more clear in Hoppity's Pity menu that you will get a Chocolate Rabbit you don't already have
- Decreased the Tree the Fish Pity requirement from 1/5,000 to 1/1,000
- Fixed the recipe of the Mosquito Shortbow using Tarantula Web instead Tarantula Silk
Spoiler: Day 2 Changes

## ► /pity
We have now added a Pity system to the game, the details of which can be found within the /rng menu or by directly typing /pity. Going forward, we aim to utilize pity systems in areas of content where an RNG Meter might not necessarily make sense due to their small scale, but where bad luck can be particularly punishing/frustrating. Meters may still be used going forward for large pieces of content with a variety of desirable drops.
The following areas of the game are currently included in the new Pity system, with more potentially being added in the future. Our goal is for this system to primarily be used for drops that are part of the overarching/intended progression of the game - those which you are expected to get on a functional (as opposed to a social) level - such as certain drops required as part of Hina's Chapters.
- Crimson Isle
  - Migrated existing Trophy Fish pity to this menu.
- Glacite Tunnels
  - Migrated existing Glacite Mineshaft spawn pity to this menu.
- Garden
  - Every 3,000th visitor is guaranteed to be the Spaceman.
  - Designer Note: Pity only increments if you are the necessary Garden Level to spawn Spaceman.
- Hoppity's Hunt
  - Every 500 eggs is guaranteed to be a Mythic rabbit.
  - Every 2,500 eggs is guaranteed to be a Divine rabbit.
  - Designer Note: This pity system for Hoppity's Hunt replaces the previously teased RNG Meter.
  - Designer Note: Eggs claimed from the Rabbit Hitman DO count towards pity.
  - Designer Note: When you proc pity, you are guaranteed to get a rabbit you do not have yet, if there is one.
- Moonglade Marsh
  - Every 2,500 treasure is guaranteed to be Fish the fish.
  - Every 2,500th mob kill is guaranteed to drop Mob the fish.
  - Every 5,000th tree gift is guaranteed to drop Tree the fish.
* Numbers are subject to change throughout the Alpha period. Pity will be retroactive where applicable.
Additionally, we have added Tablist Widgets for each of the above pity systems, meaning you can view your pity in real-time via the tablist feature.
## ► Ironman W's
Included in this update are a few improvements in and around Ironman profiles.
### ➥ Ironman Shen's Auctions
Ironman Profiles now participate in Shen's Auctions completely separately from Normal Profiles to help even the playing field, although there is a reduced quantity of items available. Any items earned through Ironman's Shen's Auctions are automatically soulbound.
### ➥ Normal Profile Conversion
Solo Profiles are now able to convert their Ironman Profiles into Normal Profiles if they so choose. This can be done through the Profile Management menu. Once you declare that you want to convert your profile to a normal one you'll have to wait 30 days to give a final confirmation that you want to convert it. If you ever want to cancel during the 30 days you'll need to restart the cooldown all over again.
Ironman Co-ops are also able to convert to Normal Co-ops, but the process is a little bit different. Each person has 3 days to vote once someone stats the process in the Profile Management menu. If at any point someone votes "No" or doesn't vote in time, the conversion process fails. If every member of the Co-op votes "Yes" however, the 30 day wait begins. If at any point during those 30 days someone has second thoughts, they can go back to the Profile Management menu and stop the process entirely.
We would like to once stress that this system was designed to prevent impulsive decisions. A Normal Profile cannot be turned back into an Ironman Profile under any circumstances. Make sure you really want to convert it.
### ➥ Cosmetics Tab in AH
We've replaced the 'Blocks' tab in the Auction House with the 'Cosmetics' tab for Normal Profiles. Most of the things found in the Blocks tab can now be found under the 'Tools & Misc' tab.
Ironman players now opening the Auction House menu will be greeted with a "Cosmetics Browser" which allows them to purchase most of the items found in the new Cosmetics tab. Ironman players are not able to sell items here, only purchase.
As a side note, the Ironman Bazaar now allows you to buy a custom amount of Booster Cookies rather than any of the preset amounts like before.
### ➥ Dwarven Ironman Guild
The area above spawn in the Dwarven Mines has been revamped into a space for the Dwarven Ironman Guild. This area has a few NPCs that introduce players to Ironman Profiles, and one of the NPCs even allows you to create an Ironman Profile directly through his menu.
## ► QoL Changes
We've also taken the time to make some key QoL changes to certain areas of the game.
### ➥ Dungeons
- Thorn Shard Changes
  - Now drops exclusively from Master Mode Floor IV 100% of the time in Emerald Chests and above as a bonus
  - Is now a LEGENDARY Shard with the ID of L48
- Added a Scarf Shard
  - Drops exclusively from Master Mode Floor II 100% of the time in Emerald Chests and above as a bonus
  - Grants 2-20% more Dungeon Class experience
- All of Livid's clones are now Humanoid/Arcane mob types rather than a bunch of different ones
### ➥ Farming
- Doubled the Pest damage buff provided by the Praying Mantis Shard
- Field Mice are now "Elusive" mobs
- Replaced the Mosquito Pet's 'Fool Me Once' perk with a new one
  - Perk: Bloodsucker's Betrayal
  - Effect: Pest Traps work 0.2-20% faster.
- If you participated in multiple Farming Contests, you'll now receive the best medal out of the crops you farmed
  - Previously it was simply given to the crop you farmed the most in, but this could occasionally result in you receiving a worse medal than you would've otherwise gotten
### ➥ Fishing
- Bobbin' Time Ultimate Enchantment Changes
  - 10 ➜ 5 max players
  - +0.3/0.4/0.5% ➜ +0.6/0.8/1.0% stats per player
  - In effect you get the same stats as before but with half as many people
- Ember Armor's 'Nether Lord' ability is now disabled near Fishing Hotspots
- Added Raw Cod to the Fishing Merchant
- Adjusted the Totem of Corruption to now count as a deployable and only corrupt Sea Creatures that you spawn.
### ➥ Foraging
- Added Wet Water, Gill Membranes, Flexbones, Stretching Sticks, Sturdy Bones, Starlyn Prizes, Deep Roots, Bamboo, and Sticks to the Foraging Sack
- Added Warty, Lushlilacs, Candycombs, Oceandies, and Bonbons to the Bazaar
- Shellwise, Pandas, and Joydives now respawn faster
### ➥ Mining
- Mythic Armadillo Pet
  - Upgraded using a 'Frozen Scute', which is dropped rarely from Vanguard Corpses
  - New perk 'Well-Worked' which consumes up to 10% less Energy while tunneling
  - Mythic Armadillos have 400 Energy
- The Travel Scroll to the Crystal Nucleus no longer has an MVP+ requirement
- Mithril Powder Buffs
  - Raffle
    - Participation Bonus: 5,000 ➜ 10,000 Mithril Powder
    - Per Ticket: 10 ➜ 25 Mithril Powder
  - Goblin Raid
    - Participation Bonus: 5,000 ➜ 10,000 Mithril Powder
    - Per Goblin: 20 ➜ 50 Mithril Powder
  - Mithril Gourmand
    - Participation Bonus: 2,000 ➜ 10,000 Mithril Powder
    - Removed the cap on Tasty Mithril
  - Commissions
    - ALL Dwarven Mines Commissions now give 500 Mithril Powder
    - ALL Crystal Hollows Commissions now give 1,000 Gemstone Powder
    - ALL Glacite Tunnels Commissions now give 1,500 Glacite Powder
    - Each of these are doubled if you are HOTM 10
  - Fetchur Quest Reward: 1,000 ➜ 2,500 Mithril Powder
  - Puzzler Quest Reward: 1,000 ➜ 2,500 Mithril Powder
  - Powder Ghast: 50-100 ➜ 100-250 Mithril Powder (per hit)
  - Golden Goblins: 200 ➜ 500 Mithril Powder
  - Diamond Goblins: 400 ➜ 1,000 Mithril Powder
  - Dwarven Treasure Reforge Bonus: +5% ➜ +15% Mithril Powder from Dwarven Events
  - Powder Pumpkin Effect: +5% ➜ +15% Mithril Powder
- All Glacite Mineshafts now spawn with at least 10 mobs
- Peridot Mineshafts are bad no more
- Amber, Jade, Ruby, Amethyst, and Sapphire now give 8/2,000 towards Mineshaft pity (from 4/2,000)
- Aquamarine, Onyx, Peridot, and Citrine now give 10/2,000 towards Mineshaft pity (from 4/2,000)
- Added a Tablist Widget for Pickaxe Ability cooldowns
- Added Precursor Apparatuses to the Crystal Hollows Sack
- Sped up King Yolkar's yapping after you finish the Crystal Nucleus quest for the first time
### ➥ Miscellaneous
- Doubled the quantities of Artifacts of Control, Pandora's Boxes, and Shen's Regalia at Shen's Auction
- Infinite Quiver is no longer disabled while you're sneaking (don't ask)
- Rusty got around to organizing his shop menu into different categories
- You can no longer Huntaxe a mob that another player spawned
  - Correction: Huntaxe will no longer take ownership of a mob spawned by another player
- On a Mayor's 3rd, 5th, and 7th appearance they are now guaranteed to get their 2nd, 3rd, and 4th perk respectively
  - They have a 50% chance on their 2nd, 4th, and 6th appearance to get that perk early
- Reduced the minimum weight needed to get a Skeleton the Fish from 1,000 LB Loudmouth Bass to only 50 LB
- Oops the Fish, Spook the Fish, and Chill the Fish can now be auctioned
## ► Other Changes
- Scorpion Bow Changes
  - Renamed to 'Spider Shortbow'
  - Made crafting recipe significantly cheaper
  - Deals x2 Damage to the Tarantula Broodfather's Egg Sacs
  - Is now a Shortbow
  - Reduced stats to compensate
- Mosquito Bow now deals x3 Damage to the Tarantula Broodfather's Egg Sacs
- Egg Sac Changes
  - Tier 3: 2 Egg Sacs -- 5 Health each
  - Tier 4: 3 Egg Sacs -- 10 Health each
  - Tier 5: 4 Egg Sacs -- 15 Health each
- T5 Tarantula Broodfather's 'Til Death Do Us Part' ability now has 'Noxious Paralysis' ability and can Backstab
- T5 Tarantula Broodfathers now always have Magic Resistance instead of only being after its 'Bat Barrage' ability
- Points gained for Hunting during Agatha's Contests are now per-mob instead of per-shard to prevent players from getting way more Points than intended
- The Starborn Shard is now a Water shard instead of a Combat Shard
- Reduced the scaling of enchantments that increased damage to specific mob types
- Renamed 'Explorer' achievement to 'Wanderer', changed description to more clearly indicate that it requires you to visit more zones even beyond what's required for the 'Explorer' quest (like the Regalia Room)
- The Soul Eater Ultimate Enchantment is now capped at 1m Damage outside of Dungeons
- Removed Smite VII from Tomioka's shop
- Capped the 'Maniac Miner' Pickaxe Ability to 500 Mining Fortune
- Reduced the drop rate of Glacite Shards to 0.1% from various mobs
- Nerfed Enchanted Hoppers to only give 70% of an item's NPC sell price
- Unequipping Reaper Armor now takes away stats from the Enrage bonus if they were active
- Fossil item descriptions no longer mention that they give Fuel as it's not their primary purpose
  - They can still be used for Fuel but it's a "secret" feature
## ► Bug Fixes
As usual we have fixed another round of bugs with this upcoming release.
- Fixed Gauntlet of Contagion's damage scaling when it's just supposed to be a percentage of mob health
- Fixed Primordial Jockey's doing way more damage than intended
- Fixed Ragnarock's recipe being in the shape of an axe despite now being a sword
- Fixed the Hollow Wand's 'Ichor Pool' damage buff persisting even after leaving radius
- Fixed the Great Spook Staff's 'Spooker' ability not working on Private Islands
- Fixed Vampire Witch Mask being able to damage certain immune Galatea mobs
- Fixed an issue with the 'Front Loaded' HOTM perk
- Fixed a rare issue when claiming items from The Forge
This release also includes the Dungeonbreaker from previous Alphas. We would also like to let you know that we hope to release another patch to Alpha in the near future with some further important QoL changes, such as having Mort ask for something other than a Spirit Mask as part of his Abiphone contact and for the Spirit Mask itself to come from Master Mode Floor IV instead of from the Dark Auction.
We hope you have a good time testing today's update and, as usual, please report any bugs at https://hypixel.net/bug-reports/create, making sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
That's all for today!

# Changelog/2025/October 16/0
Categories: 2025 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[October 16] Mythological Ritual v2
Updated: October 16, 2025
Designer Note: As was the case with the Dungeonbreaker Alpha, we are looking to do a prolonged testing session for this update in order to polish it as much as possible before an eventual release. Due to this, we have supplied prospective Alpha testers with a particularly detailed outline of the update - so get your reading glasses on!
### ► New Mobs
Several new mobs have been added to the Mythological Ritual event. Increasing the rarity of your Griffin Pet unlocks 2 new mobs per tier. Additionally, whenever you upgrade your pet all mobs get stronger but drop more items (gold & ancient claws), in addition to gaining an increased chance of dropping their signature drop(s):
|Mob Name|Req. Griffin Rarity|Weight|Signature Drop(s)|Drop Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Minos Hunter|COMMON|1,000 (17.30% at Mythic Griffin)|Hilt of Revelations|0.25% -> 2%|
|Siamese Lynx|COMMON|1,000|Crochet Tiger Plushie|0.05% -> 0.4%|
|Stranded Nymph (NEW!)|UNCOMMON|800 (13.84 % at Mythic Griffin)|Washed-up Souvenir|0.2% -> 0.5%|
|Cretan Bull (NEW!)|UNCOMMON|800|Cretan Urn|0.2% -> 0.5%|
|Harpy (NEW!)|RARE|600 (10.38% at Mythic Griffin)|Antique Remedies|0.35% -> 0.6%|
|Gaia Construct|RARE|600|Dwarf Turtle Shelmet|0.35% -> 0.6%|
|Minotaur|EPIC|400 (6.92% at Mythic Griffin)|Daedalus Stick|0.04% -> 0.08%|
|Minos Champion|EPIC|400|Minos Relic|0.02% -> 0.04%|
|Sphinx (NEW!)|LEGENDARY|75 (1.30% at Mythic Griffin - Inquis up from old 1.23% base)|Brain Food|0.25% -> 0.5%|
|Minos Inquisitor|LEGENDARY|75|Chimera I|1% -> 1.25%|
|Manticore (NEW!)|MYTHIC|15 (0.26%)|Fabled Stinger Manti-core|0.2% 0.1%|
|King Minos (NEW!)|MYTHIC|15|Crown of Greed Shimmering Wool|2% 0.1%|
Additionally, some mythological mobs also have a chance to drop some new attributes!
- Cretan Bull (COMMON Shard):
  - Grants +4-40 Defense against ✿ Mythological mobs.
- Minotaur (RARE Shard):
  - Mythological Ruler: Grants +0.5-5 Magic Find on ✿ Mythological mobs.
- Sphinx (EPIC Shard):
  - Big Game Hunter: Grants +0.5-5 Tracking on Griffin Burrows.
- King Minos (EPIC Shard):
  - King of Greed: Increases Coin gain from Griffin Burrows by 1-10%.
### ► New Items
With the addition of several new mobs and Mythos Fragments being added to Griffin Burrows, there are a bunch of new Mythological Ritual items to collect. A non-exhaustive list of some particularly interesting ones can be found below.
- Golden Fleece (LEGENDARY Belt)
  - Grants +5 Magic Find on ✿ Mythological mobs and +15 Tracking on Griffin Burrows
- Manticore Claw (LEGENDARY Gloves)
  - Copies 10% of your active pet’s Combat Stats.
- Mythological Dye
  - Dyes a piece of armor #6F6F0C.
- Fabled Stinger
  - Upgrades Venomous VI -> Venomous VII.
- Brain Food
  - Can be consumed up to 5 times. Each consumption grants +1 Tracking.
- Mythos Armor (EPIC Armor)
  - Grants +10 Magic Find on ✿ Mythological mobs at 4/4 and reduces damage taken from them.
- Mythos Equipment (EPIC Equipment)
  - Adds +2 length to Griffin Burrow chains and reduces damage taken from ✿ Mythological mobs.
All items listed above can be obtained directly from Centauri on the Alpha Network.
### ► New Stat: ❃ Tracking
❃ Tracking is a new stat introduced as part of this update. For every 1 tracking you are 1% more likely to find mobs with the ♣ Elusive typing. Examples of mobs with this typing includes Field Mice, Diamond Goblins, Scathas, and Mythic SC's.
In terms of the Mythological Ritual, having more tracking will make you more likely to find the following mobs, which also have the elusive typing:
- Minos Inquisitor
- Sphinx
- Manticore
- King Minos
A sizable amount of the new content added as part of this update will involve the Tracking stat, so keep it in mind!
Please note that we do not intend to add Inquisitor pity or anything similar for ✿ Mythological mobs - if you want to find them more often you need to increase your Tracking stat!
An example of ❃ Tracking in effect:
- Base chance of Minos Inquisitor and Sphinx with 0 ❃ Tracking is 1.30%
- Base chance of Manticore and King Minos with 0 ❃ Tracking is 0.26%
- Let's give the player 30 ❃ Tracking!
  - 15 from the Golden Fleece
  - 5 from Brain Food
  - 5 from Big Game Hunter X Attribute
  - 5 from Enderman Slayer 9
- Base chance of the Minos Inquisitor and Sphinx now goes from 1.30% -> 1.67%!
- Base chance of the Manticore and King Minos now goes from 0.26% -> 0.33%!
So, as you can see, for optimal Mythological Ritual rates you'll want to max out your Tracking stat, as it actually makes a sizable difference.
### ► Carnival Shop Perks
The following perks for the Mythological Ritual have been added to Foxy's Carnival.
- Fabled Slayer I-V
  - Increases damage dealt to ✿ Mythological mobs by +5/10/15/20/25%.
- Deadly Greed I-V
  - Gain +2/4/6/8/10% more Coins from Griffin Burrows.
- Diana’s Favor I-III
  - Grants +1/2/3 Magic Find on ✿ Mythological mobs.
    - Designer Note: This is intentionally a very small amount of Magic Find for...reasons.
- Elusive Hunter I-II
  - Grants +2.5/5 Tracking while digging up Griffin Burrows.
### ► Hephaestus Items
The Hephaestus Anvil can be crafted from 64x Enchanted Gold Blocks, 2x Braided Griffin Feathers, 1x Zog Anvil, 128x Enchanted Ancient Claw, and 64x Mythos Shards.
When placed in a crafting grid alongside 3 of any of the following items, all of the items will be consumed and a Hephaestus item will be created.
- Crochet Tiger Plushie
  - Becomes Hephaestus Plushie. Bonus Attack Speed buff increased from 35% -> 50%.
- Cretan Urn
- Becomes Hephaestus Urn. Pet Luck increased from 5 -> 10.
- Antique Remedies
  - Becomes Hephaestus Remedies. Strength buff increased from 80% -> 100%.
- Minos Relic
  - Becomes Hephaestus Relic. % buff increased from 33% -> 50%.
- Dwarf Turtle Shelmet
  - Becomes Hephaestus Shelmet. True Defense increased from 10 -> 20.
- Washed-up Souvenir
  - Becomes Hephaestus Souvenir. Sea Creature Chance increased from 5% -> 10%.
### ► Burrow Length Changes
Before, there were always 4 burrows in a chain. Burrow #1 always contained an arrow, burrows #2 and #3 mob or treasure, and #4 was guaranteed to be something from the burrow treasure table.
With this update, though the base burrow length remains at 4, we have added some new ways to extend burrows:
- The Mythos Equipment set grants +2 burrow length.
- The Archaic Spade and Deific Spades, upgrades to the Ancestral Spade, grant +2 and +4 burrow length respectively.
Instead of the previous rules, all burrows now have a 75% chance to be a mob and a 25% chance to be a treasure. This change should actually sizably increase your Inquisitors/hr due to Burrow #1 no longer being forced to be an arrow and Burrow #4 no longer being forced to be a treasure.
It is also worth noting that longer burrow chains are desirable because of the Mythic Griffin's perk:
- Ancient Earth
  - Grants +1 Tracking on Griffin Burrows for each burrow excavated in your current chain.
This perk means that, if you hit the maximum burrow length of 10, you are 10% more likely to find an elusive mob in that final burrow.
### ► Burrow Loot Changes
The various treasures you can find when excavating a Griffin Burrow now varies based upon which tier of spade you excavate it with:
Ancestral Spade (T1 Spade)
|Drop|Chance|
|-|-|
|Griffin Feather|66.67%|
|2.500 Coins|15.63%|
|5,000 Coins|10.42%|
|10,000 Coins|5.21%|
|25,000 Coins|2.08%|
Archaic Spade (T2 Spade)
|Drop|Chance|
|-|-|
|Griffin Feather|66.73%|
|Mythos Fragment|1.78%|
|5,000 Coins|17.79%|
|10,000 Coins|7.12%|
|25,000 Coins|3.56%|
|50,000 Coins|1.78%|
|100,000 Coins|0.89%|
|250,000 Coins|0.36%|
Deific Spade (T3 Spade)
|Drop|Chance|
|-|-|
|Griffin Feather|66.87%|
|Mythos Fragment|3.31%|
|Braided Griffin Feather|0.03%|
|10,000 Coins|16.55%|
|25,000 Coins|6.62%|
|50,000 Coins|3.31%|
|100,000 Coins|1.66%|
|250,000 Coins|0.83%|
|500,000 Coins|0.50%|
|1,000,000 Coins|0.33%|
Additionally, all treasure burrows have a 1/1m chance to contain the new Mythological Dye.
### ► Mythological Ritual QoL
- Added NPC sell prices to several preexisting items:
  - Enchanted Ancient Claw: 32,000
  - Crochet Tiger Plushie: 250,000
  - Antique Remedies: 250,000
  - Dwarf Turtle Shelmet: 250,000
  - Washed-up Souvenir: 250,000
  - Daedalus Stick: 1,000,000
  - Minos Relic: 5,000,000
- Added itemlore to Zog's Anvil to explain its functionality.
- Added Ancestral Spade to the Museum (2 SBXP).
- Added +10 True Defense to the Dwarf Turtle Shelmet.
- Added a requirement of having a Griffin Pet equipped to damage other player's Mythological mobs.
- Added a new texture to the Zog's Anvil.
- Adjusted itemlore of Griffin Feather to explain its functionality.
- Adjusted Minotaur's Bestiary from 4,000 kills to max -> 1,000 kills to max.
- Added a short Mythological Ritual guide to Mayor Diana's in-office menu.
- Added 50% Magic Resistance to all Mythological mobs that didn't already have it.
- Added "Right-click to view recipes!" to Ancient Claw, Enchanted Ancient Claw, Braided Griffin Feather.
- Adjusted Braided Griffin Feather's Bazaar section from Voidgloom Seraph -> Mythological Ritual.
- Adjusted the Sword of Revelations recipe, making it significantly cheaper.
- Adjusted the Crown of Greed, replacing its ability:
  - Passive: Indulgence
    - You gain 2x coins from Griffin Burrows and +10 Magic Find on Mythological mobs, but they deal 1.25x damage.
- Adjusted the Crown of Avarice, replacing its ability:
  - Passive: Overindulgence
    - You gain 5x coins from Griffin Burrows, but they are consumed by the helmet. Gain +10% Damage and +2.5 Magic Find damage against Mythological mobs for each digit of coins consumed, but they deal 1.5x damage. (Capped at 1B)
      - Designer Note: The goal here is for this item to become a staple of the Mythological Ritual, providing up to +25 Magic Find on Mythological mobs.
- Adjusted the rarity of several pet items:
  - Washed-up Souvenir: LEGENDARY -> EPIC
  - Dwarf Turtle Shelmet: RARE -> EPIC
- Adjusted the recipes of the various Griffin Upgrade Stones so as to include the new drops.
- Adjusted Mythological mob prefixes:
  - COMMON → No prefix
  - UNCOMMON → Blessed
  - RARE → Stalwart
  - EPIC → Venerable
  - LEGENDARY → Exalted
  - MYTHIC → Empyrean
- Adjusted the Blade of Daedalus to now only copy Combat Stats, and slightly reduced the Magic Find per Mythological bestiary tier on account of there now being significantly more Mythological bestiary tiers due to us adding new mobs.
  - Designer Note: The Blade of Daedalus has been a massive pain when it comes to balancing for years now - involving itself in any content affected my Magic Find in a way we deem unhealthy. We have compensated for the Magic Find lost during the Mythological Ritual with the new armor & equipment set, anguish talisman line, reworks to Griffin Pet, CoA, and CoG, Carnival Shop Perk, Golden Fleece, and more. Additionally, players should get more Inquisitors per hour due to the new Tracking stat as well as the changes made to burrows allowing players to spawn mobs more often.
- Adjusted Griffin Pet's "Odyssey" perk to list how many Mythological mobs it can find at each tier.
- Adjusted Griffin Pet's "King of Kings" perk to the following:
  - Grants +20 Magic Find on Mythological mobs.
- Adjusted Griffin Pet's base stats:
  - Added +25 Strength & +25 Health Regen
  - Removed +10 Intelligence
- Removed Griffin Pet's "Legendary Constitution" perk and rescaled the existing perk unlocks:
  - COMMON: Odyssey
  - UNCOMMON: Odyssey
  - RARE: Odyssey, Perpetual Empathy
  - EPIC: Odyssey, Perpetual Empathy
  - LEGENDARY: Odyssey, Perpetual Empathy, King of Kings
- Removed the random Minos Follower spawns when killing mobs throughout the game.
- Removed the ability to recombobulate the various Griffin Upgrade Stones.
### ► Other QoL Changes
- Added the Pesthunter Relic to the Pesthunter's Wares. Costs 200 Pests + 1x Pesthunter Artifact.
- Added an Anguish Talisman line, crafted from various Ghost drops.
- Adjusted Ghost Bestiary from 100,000 kills to max -> 40,000 kills to max.
- Adjusted Ragnarock Axe's "Ragnarock" ability to now deteriorate over the 10s period.
  - Designer Note: Following changes to melee in recent updates we will be foregoing this change. Thanks for the feedback!
- Adjusted Venomous enchant as a result of adding Venomous VII, reducing the walk speed penalty inflicted and slightly reducing the venom damage per tick.
- Adjusted Enderman Slayer 9's perk from 15 Tracking -> 5 Tracking.
  - Designer Note: While we still want it to be necessary to achieve optimal rates, we don't want this perk making up such a large percentage of the max tracking stat.
- Adjusted the amount of Soul String needed in several crafts:
  - Venom's Touch: 10x per slot -> 16x per slot
  - Venom's Touch (Fragged): 50x per slot -> 64x per slot
  - Spider Queen's Stinger: 6x per slot -> 16x per slot
  - Spider Queen's Stinger (Fragged): 6x per slot -> 64x per slot
We hope you have a good time testing today's update and, as usual, please report any bugs at https://hypixel.net/bug-reports/create, making sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
It's also worth noting that, once on the Alpha Network, you can obtain the majority of the items featured in today's update by talking to Centauri in The Hub.
That's all for today!
